### [1] [Probing the QCD Conformal Window with Four-Quark Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18828)
*Junchen Rong,Emmanuel Stamou,Tom Steudtner*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了QCD类理论在红外区域的行为及其共形窗口，通过计算 Veneziano QCD 中四夸克算符（含/不含手征对称性）的四圈反常维度及六维胶子算符的三圈反常维度，并采用共形展开与多种重求和技术进行分析，未发现支持其与QCD不动点合并的复合算符证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCD类理论的红外行为及其共形窗口随味数和色数变化仍不明确，且该问题在高能物理与凝聚态物理中均有重要应用；相变中经典无关但红外相关的算符可能起关键作用。

Method: 在Veneziano极限下，计算所有四夸克算符（含/不含手征对称性）的四圈反常维度及六维胶子算符的三圈反常维度；采用系统化的共形展开推导标度维度，并结合多种重求和技术提升精度并估计不确定性。

Result: 得到了四夸克与六维胶子算符的高阶反常维度结果；经重求和与不确定性评估后，未发现存在能有效描述与QCD不动点合并的复合算符。

Conclusion: 当前计算结果不支持QCD类理论在红外区通过某复合算符与QCD不动点发生合并，其共形窗口边界及相变机制仍需进一步探索。

Abstract: The infrared fate of QCD-like theories and their conformal window for different numbers or flavours and colour remains an open question with applications ranging from high-energy to condensed-matter systems. A hallmark of phase transitions from a conformal to symmetry breaking phase can be the presence of classically irrelevant operators that provide relevant deformation in the infrared. We investigate this scenario by computing anomalous dimensions of all four-quark operators, with or without chiral symmetry, at four-loop order as well as the dimension-six gluonic operator at three loops in Veneziano QCD. Scaling dimensions are derived in a systematic conformal expansion. These results are improved with various resummation techniques and we estimate their uncertainties. We find no concrete evidence for a composite operator effectively describing a merger with the QCD fixed point.

</details>


### [2] [Black Hole Superradiance and the Gravitational Auger Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18833)
*Joshua W. Foster,Nicholas L. Rodd*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了黑洞超辐射现象中引力相互作用对超轻玻色子云演化的影响，指出引力作用会显著改变超辐射动力学，并影响可观测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超轻玻色子（如QCD轴子）在旋转黑洞附近可通过超辐射形成宏观云，其可观测效应（如黑洞自旋变化、引力波与玻色子辐射）对玻色子自相互作用敏感；但以往研究忽略了云内纯引力相互作用的影响。

Method: 理论分析与物理建模，类比Auger效应，研究超辐射云中引力驱动的散射过程及其对不同径向和角向激发态的影响。

Result: 发现引力相互作用可快速耗散第一超辐射能级，并抑制更高能级的增长；导致连续引力波信号及双星并合晚期特征显著减弱；黑洞自旋下降虽更稳健，但高阶模式起始更早、减速更慢。

Conclusion: 纯引力效应不可忽略，需修正现有对轴子等新粒子的天文观测限制，尤其在引力波天文学和黑洞自旋测量中。

Abstract: Black hole superradiance is a powerful probe of nature's light degrees of freedom. In the vicinity of a spinning black hole, it exponentially amplifies ultralight bosons from vacuum fluctuations to a macroscopic cloud of enormous occupation number. Observational superradiance effects - modified black-hole spins, gravitational-wave and coherent boson emission - are known to depend sensitively on bosonic self-interactions as induced by a quartic, $λφ^4$. Here we point out that purely gravitational interactions within the cloud significantly modify superradiance dynamics, through an analog of the Auger effect. Moreover, we identify nontrivial evolution of the superradiant levels beyond the first, driven by scattering into even higher levels and their radial overtones. For the QCD axion, the irreducible gravitational coupling rapidly dissipates the first superradiant level after it reaches saturation, through scalar emission and absorption by the black hole, while truncating the growth of higher levels. This can dramatically suppress the observability of continuous gravitational-wave signals or signatures in late binary inspirals. Black hole spin-down remains more robust, but for higher modes begins earlier and proceeds more slowly, requiring revision of existing limits.

</details>


### [3] [$K_1^*(1870)$ and $3S$-$2D$ mixing in the strange meson sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19498)
*Ting-Yan Li,Zi-Yue Bai,Xiang Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究BESIII合作组新发现的Kη'系统中J^P=1^-奇异共振态K_1^*(1870)，探讨其在介子谱中的归属，提出其为3S-2D混合态的低质量成员，并预测其高质量伴态；通过夸克对产生模型计算了两者的OZI允许衰变性质，低态总宽度与实验测量一致，但其Kη'次主导衰变模式与观测到的Kη'衰变通道存在矛盾，需进一步研究；还探讨了Kp反应中这两态的产生机制，为未来实验提供参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BESIII合作组最新观测到Kη'系统中首个J^P=1^-奇异共振态K_1^*(1870)，亟需确定其在夸克模型中的内部结构归属。

Method: 采用3S-2D态混合方案解释K_1^*(1870)的质量，并利用夸克对产生模型（QPC）计算其OZI允许衰变宽度；同时分析Kp反应中该态的产生机制。

Result: K_1^*(1870)可被描述为3S-2D混合的较低质量态，其质量与实验值吻合，并成功预测其较高质量混合伙伴；低态总衰变宽度与实验测量一致；但其Kη'次主导衰变模式与观测到的Kη'衰变通道存在表面矛盾。

Conclusion: K_1^*(1870)最可能为s\bar{q}（q=u/d）系统的3S-2D混合态，该解释在质量和衰变宽度上自洽，但Kη'衰变分支比的理论与实验差异提示需更精细的强子动力学建模或实验验证；Kp反应是未来检验该态的理想平台。

Abstract: Very recently, the BESIII Collaboration reported the first observation of a $J^P=1^-$ strange resonance in the $Kη'$ system, the $K_1^*(1870)$. We examine whether this state can be accommodated in the kaon meson family. Although it cannot be assigned as a pure $3^3S_1$ or $2^3D_1$ state, we show that it can be described as the lower state of $3S$-$2D$ mixing, whose mass agrees with the measured value, and we predict its higher-mass partner. Using the quark pair creation model, we calculate the OZI-allowed decay properties of both \textcolor{black}{mixed} states; the total decay width of the lower state is consistent with the measured width of the $K_1^*(1870)$. We note that the subordinate $Kη'$ decay mode of this state appears to contradict its observed $Kη'$ decay channel, which calls for further study. We further investigate the production of these two states via the $Kp$ reaction, which may provide valuable information for future experimental searches.

</details>

*X. H. Mo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文评估了e+e-对撞机中十五种末态的粲偶素电磁截面，采用lS耦合分析方法构建了涉及任意整数自旋两体衰变的协变螺旋度耦合振幅，并基于衰变率最终得到碰撞截面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁截面是重要的可观测量，需要对粲偶素在e+e-对撞中的多种末态衰变进行系统评估。

Method: 采用通用的lS耦合分析方案，构建协变螺旋度耦合振幅，处理任意整数自旋的两体衰变，并由衰变率推导碰撞截面。

Result: 给出了十五种末态的粲偶素电磁截面计算结果，并通过多个示例验证和阐明所推导的一般公式。

Conclusion: 所建立的lS耦合框架适用于一般整数自旋粒子的两体衰变过程，为粲偶素及其他强子电磁过程的截面计算提供了系统方法。

Abstract: As an important measurable quantity, the electromagnetic cross sections of charmonium decay in an e+e- collider are valuated for fifteen final states. A general scheme of lS-coupling analysis is adopted for constructing covariant helicity-coupling amplitudes involving two-body decays with arbitrary integer spins. Based on the decay rate, the collision cross section is obtained finally. A number of illustrative examples is worked out to expound and incarnate the general formulas.

</details>


### [5] [Charm quark energy loss fluctuations in clumpy color string medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19789)
*Daria Prokhorova,Shuzhe Shi,Evgeny Andronov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过模拟粲夸克在质子-质子碰撞中穿越色通量管（flux tube）介质的传播，研究其能量损失机制；发现弦重叠形成的局域能量密度不均匀性主导粲夸克淬火，其中辐射损失远大于碰撞损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高能质子-质子碰撞中粲夸克的能量损失机制，特别是介质空间非均匀性对其淬火行为的影响。

Method: 基于色通量管模型，模拟粲夸克在由反向运动色荷形成的通量管系综中的传播；将叠加色场的局域能量密度转化为理想胶子气体的有效温度，并比较不同能量密度提取方案下弹性和非弹性动量阻尼的响应。

Result: 高通量管密度导致弦簇形成，产生复杂局域能量密度分布；辐射能量损失（最高约1.5 GeV）比碰撞损失（最高约0.15 GeV）大一个数量级；两种阻尼机制均敏感于介质性质，但辐射主导淬火。

Conclusion: 粲夸克在pp碰撞中的介质诱导淬火主要由弦空间重叠所引起的局域能量密度不均匀性决定，辐射过程是能量损失的主要机制。

Abstract: We simulate charm quark propagation through an ensemble of color flux tubes formed between receding color charges in proton-proton collisions. Under the assumption of sharp string boundaries and a localized nature of the overlap regions, high flux tube densities lead to the formation of string clusters. We convert the local energy density of the superposed color field into an effective temperature of an ideal gluon gas and study the dependence of charm energy loss on its spatial inhomogeneities. By comparing different energy-density extraction prescriptions, we show that both elastic and inelastic momentum damping mechanisms are sensitive to the medium properties. However, the collisional channel is an order of magnitude smaller in absolute terms (up to $\sim0.15$~GeV) compared to the radiative loss (up to $\sim1.5$~GeV). Overall, the results demonstrate that the spatial overlap of individual strings generates a complex local energy density pattern, which predominantly governs the medium-induced quenching of charm quarks.

</details>


### [6] [Quark + diquark description of nucleon gravitational form factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19819)
*Peng Cheng,Zhen-Ni Xu,Dong-Yan Fu,Chen Chen,Zhao-Qian Yao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用Poincare协变的夸克+二夸克Faddeev方程，无参数地预测了核子引力形状因子（GFFs），并分解了夸克与胶子贡献；发现标量二夸克主导能量、自旋和力学性质，轴矢量二夸克不可或缺；B(Q²)极小但非零，源于内部结构抵消；c̄(Q²)满足对称性；相比格点QCD，D项和空间半径偏小，主要因忽略介子云对胶子分布的影响；负能量密度中心的存在具有强模型依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确预测核子引力形状因子（GFFs）以揭示其内部能量、自旋、压力与剪切力分布，并厘清夸克与胶子各自贡献，同时检验理论框架的基本对称性与完备性。

Method: 采用Poincare协变的夸克+二夸克Faddeev方程进行无参数计算，完整给出A、J、D和c̄等GFFs，并按二夸克类型（标量/轴矢量）及夸克味（u/d）进行物种分解；结合两种外推方案分析能量密度分布，并与格点QCD结果对比。

Result: 标量二夸克主导核子能量、角动量和机械性质，轴矢量成分关键；B(Q²)极小但非零，源于二夸克与味扇区的精细抵消；c̄(Q²)满足基本对称性；D项和空间半径系统性偏小于格点QCD结果；负能量密度中心存在与否高度依赖于外推模型。

Conclusion: 该Faddeev框架能自洽描述核子GFFs并满足基本对称性，但系统低估D项等反映胶子贡献的量，表明介子云效应对胶子分布影响显著；未来需纳入更完整的动力学自由度（如胶子显式自由度或介子云修正）以提升精度。

Abstract: We present parameter-free predictions for the nucleon gravitational form factors (GFFs) using a Poincare-covariant quark+diquark Faddeev equation. We provide the full GFFs ($A$, $J$, $D$, and $\bar{c}$) along with their species decompositions into quark and gluon contributions. Based on the diquark breakdown, we find that while the scalar diquark component dominates the nucleon's energy, spin, and mechanical properties, the axial-vector component plays an indispensable role. The GFF $B(Q^2)$ is predicted to be vanishingly small but nonvanishing at finite $Q^2$; its physical origin is deeply rooted in the nucleon' s internal structure through cancellations in both the diquark content and the $u/d$ flavor sectors. Furthermore, our results for $\bar{c}(Q^2)$ confirm the fundamental symmetry requirements of the approach. Compared with lQCD, our calculated $D$-term and various spatial radii are somewhat smaller in magnitude. This systematic underestimation is primarily driven by the gluon sector and can be attributed to the omission of meson cloud effects, suggesting that these effects impact the gluon distribution more significantly than the quark distribution. Finally, our analysis of the energy density based on two extrapolation schemes reveals that the presence of a negative central energy density is highly model-dependent.

</details>


### [7] [Exploring X17 Couplings through Meson Dalitz Decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19831)
*Xuan Wang,Tianhong Wang,SiQi Zhang,Guo-Li Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究X17新玻色子在介子Dalitz衰变中的贡献，引入矢量与轴矢量耦合框架，利用Bethe-Salpeter方法计算强子跃迁矩阵元，确定了粲、奇异夸克耦合参数允许区域，并给出四组简并最优解，其中一组为(g_c^V,g_c^A,g_s^V,g_s^A)=(8.32,1.09,-2.62,1.36)×10^{-3}，理论预测D^{*+}→D^+e^+e^-衰变比显著超出标准模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ATOMIC协作组观测到核跃迁中异常的正负电子对产生，被解释为可能的新轻玻色子X17；但仅考虑矢量耦合与现有D*0→D0e+e-、Ds*+→Ds+e+e-及核跃迁分析结果存在张力，需拓展耦合结构。

Method: 构建包含矢量与轴矢量耦合的X17相互作用框架，采用Bethe-Salpeter方法计算介子强子跃迁矩阵元，结合u/d夸克耦合先验，拟合c/s夸克耦合参数。

Result: 确定c/s夸克轴矢量耦合强度|g_{c,s}^A|~10^{-3}；获得四组简并最优解，如(g_c^V,g_c^A,g_s^V,g_s^A)=(8.32,1.09,-2.62,1.36)×10^{-3}；理论预测R^{th}(D^{*+}→D^+e^+e^-)显著高于标准模型。

Conclusion: X17粒子若存在，其与粲、奇异夸克的轴矢量耦合不可忽略；该耦合结构可协调核跃迁与介子衰变实验约束，为X17物理性质提供关键鉴别依据。

Abstract: The ATOMKI collaboration has reported anomalous $e^+e^-$ pair production in a series of nuclear transitions, which has been interpreted as a possible signature of a new light boson, X17. Meson Dalitz decays provide a complementary probe of the X17 interaction structure. However, the widely studied vector coupling scenario is in tension with the combined constraints from existing $D^{*0}\to D^0e^+e^-$, $D_s^{*+}\to D_s^+e^+e^-$ measurements and nuclear transition analyses. In this work, we investigate the X17 contributions to meson Dalitz decays within a framework incorporating both vector and axial-vector couplings, employing the Bethe--Salpeter approach to calculate the hadronic transition matrix elements. With $u$ and $d$ quark couplings taken from previous analyses, we identify allowed regions for the charm and strange quark couplings, which require the axial-vector coupling strength $|g_{c,s}^A|\sim10^{-3}$. We obtain four degenerate best-fit solutions, one of which is $(g_c^V,g_c^A,g_s^V,g_s^A)=(8.32,1.09,-2.62,1.36)\times10^{-3}$, and estimate the decay ratio $R^{\mathrm{th}}(D^{*+}\to D^+e^+e^-)$, which significantly exceeds the Standard Model prediction.

</details>

*Patricia Rebello Teles,David d'Enterria*

TL;DR: 本文探讨了CERN未来环形对撞机（FCC）在多种碰撞模式下利用等效光子近似开展双光子物理研究的潜力，重点聚焦于C偶宇称共振态（如希格斯玻色子、类轴子ALPs和类引力子GLPs）的单态产生与双光子衰变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用FCC在e⁺e⁻、pp、pPb和PbPb等多种运行模式下产生的高亮度等效光子通量，拓展双光子物理的研究范围，特别是对C偶宇称粒子的高灵敏度探测。

Method: 基于等效光子近似（EPA），结合不同对撞模式下的光子通量、能量与虚度特性，计算双光子不变质量谱，并评估希格斯、ALPs和GLPs的产生截面与探测灵敏度。

Result: 预测可在所有FCC对撞模式中观测到光子融合机制产生的希格斯玻色子；对光子耦合的ALPs和GLPs的探测质量上限分别可达FCC-ee的百GeV量级和FCC-hh的多TeV量级，显著优于现有实验限制。

Conclusion: FCC双光子物理项目将为探索标准模型之外的新物理（尤其是轻/重自旋0和自旋2新粒子）提供前所未有的灵敏度和覆盖范围，是其整体物理纲领中不可或缺的重要组成部分。

Abstract: The CERN Future Circular Collider (FCC) offers a unique programme of photon-photon interactions in electron-positron, proton-proton, proton-lead, and lead-lead collisions, by exploiting the high-luminosity equivalent photon fluxes associated with the charged beams. Within the equivalent photon approximation, the electromagnetic fields of relativistic charged particles are described in terms of fluxes of quasireal photons. The complementary photon fluxes, energies, and virtualities available across the FCC operation modes provide access to a broad range of two-photon invariant masses and associated physics measurements. Physics opportunities open to study are highlighted, focusing on the exclusive production of C-even resonances, including the SM Higgs boson and searches for spin-0 axion-like (ALPs) and spin-2 graviton-like (GLPs) particles decaying back into photon pairs. These prospective studies demonstrate the potential to observe photon-fusion production of the Higgs boson for all colliding modes, as well as sensitivity to photon-coupled ALPs and GLPs with masses extending up to the kinematic reach of FCC-ee and into the multi-TeV range at FCC-hh collisions, with significant improvements over existing constraints.

</details>

Abstract: The hadronic vacuum polarization contributions to the muon anomalous magnetic moment $a^{\text{HVP}}_μ$ in spacelike and timelike domains are recapped. The obtained results can be employed in the evaluations of $a^{\text{HVP}}_μ$ in the framework of such methods as lattice studies, MUonE project, and others.

</details>

*Jie Yu,Wan-Li Ju,Qiang Li,Tianhong Wang,Guo-Li Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过求解瞬时Bethe-Salpeter方程研究了重轻2⁻介子的质量谱、D波混合角及OZI允许的两体强衰变，结合PCAC和手征有效拉氏量计算衰变振幅并估算总宽度。

No consensus has emerged on whether difficulties in reproducing them stem from missing physics, simplifying assumptions that break down at the highest redshifts, or model mis-calibration. This is complicated because most galaxy models cannot be compared like-to-like, and models invoking different physical processes can make similar predictions for compressed summaries such as ultraviolet luminosity functions (UV LFs). Here we describe galaxy populations in a universal language: a chain of physically and empirically motivated conditional distributions linking halo mass, stellar mass, star formation rate and UV magnitude, defined consistently across models and data. We build a broad prior from fits to five hydrodynamic simulations, which predict very different high-redshift galaxy populations. We infer the parameters of this flexible galaxy model from $z>6$ JWST observations of UV LFs and angular correlation functions (ACFs). Including the mass-complete ACFs disfavors a large scatter around the stellar-to-halo mass relation (SHMR). With simulation-based priors, the star-forming main sequence (SFMS) is described by a star-formation timescale of about one halo dynamical time, and the scatter around the SFMS increases toward lower stellar masses. The SFMS scatter is the main source of stochasticity in UV magnitude at fixed halo mass, accounting for a median $\sim$61% of the variance at $M_{\rm UV}=-20$ (SHMR scatter: $\sim$36%). Bayesian model selection finds no evidence for redshift evolution of the SHMR or for an SFMS slope different from unity. At $z\sim20$, our fiducial model predicts UV LFs orders of magnitude below estimates from photometric candidates. A consensus on the discrepant UV LF estimates at $z\geq14$ is needed to determine whether model extensions are required.

Our measured torus radius-luminosity slope, $R \propto L^{0.42\pm0.05}$, agrees with literature results. Abstract: We introduce the Gini index (G) as a metric for studying the emergence of brightest cluster galaxies (BCGs) by quantifying the disparity in the light distribution of galaxy cluster cores. The Gini index collapses the skewness of the bright-end luminosity function to a single statistic, and provides a measure for the light dominance of the BCG over other cluster members that is more robust than the magnitude gap. We argue that the Gini index is far less sensitive to short-term changes in the cluster dynamical state than the magnitude gap and solves its known degeneracies by taking the entire cluster membership into account, making G a more sensitive metric to long-term BCG assembly. We apply the magnitude gap and Gini index to a progenitor-selected sample of 92 South Pole Telescope- and Planck-selected cluster cores at 0 < z < 1 with corresponding Hubble Space Telescope F110W and F200LP and Sloan Digital Sky Survey g and r photometry. Based on both parametric and empirical tests, we find no statistical evidence for evolution in G or $\rm M_{1,2}$ over 0 < z < 1, and find that BCGs have not grown significantly (2${\times}$) faster than the cluster population for z < 1 at 3$σ$ confidence. This implies that BCGs achieve dominance and establish defining properties early (z > 1) in massive clusters. In general, we find that the Gini index is a valuable and relatively straightforward measurement, with well-controlled systematics, that could be applied to much larger samples to study BCG dominance and emergence.

Abstract: Magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) turbulence is ubiquitous in astrophysical plasmas and produces scale-dependent anisotropy with respect to the local magnetic field. Because turbulence cascades preferentially perpendicular to the field, its spatial gradients acquire preferred orientations that can be used to probe magnetic-field structure. This property underlies a family of gradient techniques based on spectroscopic, synchrotron-intensity, and synchrotron-polarization observations. In this review, we summarize the theoretical foundation of these techniques, including the anisotropy of MHD turbulence and the corresponding behavior of velocity, magnetic-field, and density fluctuations. We then describe how these fluctuations are encoded in spectroscopic data and synchrotron observables, and review the implementation of Velocity Centroid Gradients, Velocity Channel Gradients, Intensity Gradients, Synchrotron Intensity Gradients, and Synchrotron Polarization Gradients. We also discuss the effects of isotropic fast modes, shocks, self-gravity, stellar outflows, thermal broadening, and self-absorption. Finally, we review applications to the diffuse interstellar medium, star-forming regions, the Galactic center, external galaxies, and galaxy clusters.

Here we report that a new environmental metric, the unbound matter fraction ($f_{\rm un}$) in the $2$--$3\,R_{\rm vir}$ shell around a halo, can largely account for secondary bias. At fixed large-scale environment, $f_{\rm un}$ further indicates the presence of nearby neighbours and the competitive accretion. Our results highlight the role of unbound matter as a mediator of environmental effects on haloes, and reveal the physical mechanisms that shape the CHM pattern through which these effects are mediated.

Abstract: The mass distribution of early-type galaxies (ETGs) encodes key information about baryonic processes and dark-matter assembly. These results are consistent with the redshift trend predicted by Horizon-AGN simulations, in which active galactic nucleus (AGN) feedback, together with stellar assembly and merging, contributes to the structural evolution of ETGs. Abstract: Strong gravitational-lensing systems with multiple source planes are powerful tools for probing the stellar and dark matter content of galaxies. Lens modelling can measure the relative Einstein radii of the two source planes, which encode a ratio of angular-diameter distances determined by the lens and source redshifts and the dark energy equation of state, independently of the Hubble constant. Galaxy-scale double-source-plane lenses (DSPLs) are extremely rare. We present a sample of 84 galaxy-scale DSPL candidates in Euclid Data Release 1 (DR1), of which 80 are newly identified. These systems were selected using machine-learning lens-finding models followed by visual inspection by citizen scientists and experts. We then used lens modelling to test the DSPL interpretation. Among our lens candidates, 11 form the 'Gold' sample: each shows two sets of multiple images at different radii, and the observed configuration is reproduced by a DSPL lens model. The remaining 73 'Silver' candidates have at least two lensed arcs from visual inspection, but either lack multiple images for at least one arc, have a best-fitting DSPL distance ratio close to the single-source-plane limit, remain compatible with a single-plane model with multiple sources, or have not yet been modelled. Even if all 84 candidates are DSPLs, the sample falls below the approximately 90 systems expected from extrapolating the Euclid Quick Data Release 1 (Q1) yield and the approximately 200 obtained by scaling the LensPop forecast to the DR1 area, indicating that many DSPLs remain undetected in DR1. Spectroscopy or higher-resolution imaging is required before we can be confident that the Silver candidates are DSPLs.

Abstract: Normal shock waves are commonly described as mixtures of upstream and downstream Maxwellians. Discrete-velocity solutions of three relaxation models up to Mach 12 and direct simulation Monte Carlo (DSMC) of hard-sphere and variable-hard-sphere gases up to Mach 25 are used to examine the population structure, Mach-number similarity and upstream precursor of their velocity distributions. Three anisotropic Gaussian populations reproduce the streamwise reduced distributions of the Bhatnagar--Gross--Krook (BGK) model and of DSMC to within 4\% up to Mach 12, and of DSMC to within 6\% at Mach 25, whereas two Maxwellians incur errors up to 50\% (BGK) and 37\% (DSMC). Collision histories identify the hard-sphere beam with unscattered stream molecules, which carry half of the peak viscous stress; the other populations are effective descriptions, not collision classes. Above Mach 5, Rankine--Hugoniot-scaled distributions are nearly Mach-independent, with corrections linear in $1/M^2$, consistent with the scaled kinetic equation. At equal normalised density, Mach 8 and 12 give the heat flux and stress at Mach 25 within 1\%, comparable to the accuracy of the reference; with one moment equation for position, physical-space profiles follow within 5\%. Comparing three prediction methods shows that the accuracy comes from the scaling; the Gaussian form adds compactness. Ahead of the shock, the mass of upstream-moving molecules decays as a stretched exponential with effective exponent 0.68--0.87. Collisions regenerate upstream-moving molecules, which explains a decay slower than free-flight survival; a linear cascade model with a downstream-Maxwellian source and no input from the shock interior reproduces the decay rates within 1.2\%.

Abstract: We present a study of the compact-source population associated with the massive galaxy cluster MACS J1149.5+2223 at z=0.544 using deep JWST/NIRCam imaging in F115W, F150W, and F200W. After photometric and morphological selection, we identify 1,202 unresolved compact-source candidates. The observed luminosity distribution samples only the bright side of the expected globular-cluster luminosity function (GCLF), remaining several magnitudes above the turnover. Based on published GCLF turnovers for Abell 2744 and MACS 0417, we adopt an expected turnover magnitude of m_0=33.66 mag for MACS 1149. A forward-modeled fit then yields sigma=1.71 mag, which characterizes the observed bright-side slope under the adopted turnover rather than the intrinsic width of the full GCLF. Comparison with PARSEC simple stellar population models shows that the elevated luminosities of the compact sources are consistent with the expected fading of younger stellar populations as they evolve toward the present epoch. The brightest sources reach luminosities comparable to those of ultra-compact dwarfs. However, stellar-population degeneracies and possible foreground contamination make it difficult to distinguish. Together with recent JWST studies of globular cluster systems across a range of redshifts, MACS 1149 extends the emerging observational sequence of compact stellar systems and supports stellar population fading as an important contributor to their luminosity evolution across cosmic time.

Abstract: Deriving stellar astrophysical parameters from large-scale spectroscopic surveys requires accurate modeling of spectra across a wide wavelength coverage and a broad range of stellar parameter space, which remains a great challenge. In this work, we present \texttt{TDSM}, a physically motivated neural network architecture that self-consistently emulates both the flux and its first-order derivative of the ab initio stellar spectra. The method enables accurate and precise emulation of Kurucz synthetic spectra with severely blended features at $R \simeq 2000$ in a high-dimensional parameter space, achieving a typical pixel-wise flux precision of 0.05\% in the wavelength range $3700<λ<9000$Å over the parameter range of $3000 < T_{\rm eff} < 45,000$~K, $-1 < \log g < 5$, and $-4.0 < \mathrm{[Fe/H]} < 0.5$. The model is applied to LAMOST DR10 to derive stellar parameters for 11 million spectra spanning the full model parameter range. We conduct detailed refinement and calibration efforts to mitigate systematic errors, which are revealed to be significant and complex due to imperfections in the ab initio model atmosphere. The resultant catalog provides eight stellar labels -- $T_{\rm eff}$, $\log g$, $v_{\rm mic}$, [Fe/H], [$α$/Fe], [C/Fe], [N/Fe], and [Mg/Fe] -- for 8 million unique stars (11 million spectra including repeat observations) across the full parameter space. This adds a valuable dataset for studies of Galactic structure and archaeology, stellar populations, and related areas of astrophysics.

Abstract: We present an overview of the LAMOST III Low-resolution Spectroscopic Survey (LRS), including its scientific goals, target selection strategy, and current observing status. Targets in the main survey are selected via weighted random sampling, using the inverse local density in the Hertzsprung-Russell (HR) diagram and in the G-magnitude distribution, with additional enhancement in selected regions of the HR diagram. The main survey is complemented by 35 dedicated add-on catalogs (including very metal-poor star candidates, halo stars, hot/young stars, wide binaries, extragalactic sources and so on) and a time-domain program providing multi-epoch spectroscopy of binaries and other peculiar systems. LAMOST III LRS is designed to map the structure and evolution of the Galactic disk, reconstruct the assembly and early chemical-enrichment history of the Galactic halo, improve stellar-parameter calibration, and characterize rare and variable objects. By June 2025, the LAMOST III LRS had obtained 1.53 million low-resolution spectra from 658 plates, corresponding to 1,264,052 unique sources. Relative to LAMOST I+II, the current sample is enriched in O/B stars, subgiants, main-sequence turn-off stars, metal-poor stars, and other previously underrepresented populations. In particular, the fractions of metal-poor and very metal-poor stars increase from 3.8% and 0.4% in LAMOST I+II to 9.0% and 1.1% in LAMOST III LRS, respectively, demonstrating the improved efficiency of the revised target-selection strategy for Galactic archaeology and stellar astrophysics.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究星系在不同演化阶段（蓝云、绿谷、红序）的引力势特征如何反映其熄火过程，特别是圆周速度参数与恒星形成率偏离主序程度（ΔSFR）之间的定量关系。

Conclusion: Vc,max（示踪内引力势）比Vc,flat（更受外围质量和暗物质晕影响）对星系熄火参数ΔSFR更敏感，暗示星系中心区域的动力学演化在熄火过程中起主导作用。

Abstract: The first generation of metal-free Population III (Pop~III) stars provides a possible formation channel for the black hole (BH) seeds in the early Universe. LISA pinpoints more massive central BHs ($\sim10^3$-$10^4 {\rm M_\odot}$), grown rapidly through mutual mergers alongside efficient gas accretion ($f_{\rm Edd}=1$), and a relatively top-heavy IMF ($α=0.8$). In contrast, detectability with ET favors lower-mass BHs, with less efficient accretion ($f_{\rm Edd}=0.01$) and a steeper IMF ($α=1.5$). These results highlight the potential of LISA and ET to probe Pop~III BH formation, growth, and mergers across different regions of parameter spaces, jointly constraining Pop~III star formation and early BH seed evolution.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Radio antennas are often used to form arrays, such as phased arrays and interferometer arrays. For most applications the beam patterns of these instruments must be accurately known. For phased arrays the array patterns are formed by coherently adding the embedded element patterns (EEPs) of the individual antennas in the array. For interferometers, the fundamental measurement is of visibilities, which depend on the products of pairs of EEPs. Unfortunately, in either case, for closely-spaced arrays, the EEPs can be distorted by mutual coupling between the antennas and can be very different from the isolated element patterns (IEPs), measured when the antennas are far apart. Mutual coupling is characterized by the scattering parameters for the array. Full-wave electromagnetic simulations can simulate the EEPs, including mutual coupling effects, but these simulations can be computationally expensive and require accurate knowledge of the design of the array elements, their placement, and the environment in which they are deployed. Directly measuring either the EEPs or the array patterns for instruments in the field is often impractical as well. In this paper we show that in cases where the scattering parameters between array elements can be measured, they can be used to infer the array patterns of a phased array. We demonstrate the training of a deep neural network to simulate array patterns when given: the design of the antennas, the locations of the antennas, the frequency of operation, and measurements of the scattering parameters of the array. As a demonstration for a simple model, we apply this approach to the simulation of the array patterns for a small phased array of three dipole antennas and evaluate the sensitivity of this procedure to uncertainties in measurements of the antenna positions and scattering parameters.

Abstract: Existing calendars, in particular the Gregorian calendar, have a complex structure which requires constant adjustment in the form of leap days and leap seconds in order to remain synchronized to the position of a planet in the Solar System. In addition, mean solar days can deviate up to 16 minutes from true solar days on Earth. On other planets, this discrepancy can be far greater: on Mars, it can be up to 51 minutes. Numerous Martian proposals ranging from Aitken (1936) to Gangale (1986) to Silin (2025) mimic the Gregorian calendar and do not address these issues. Moreover, many do not include a system for telling the time of day, or stretch the second beyond the standard SI second. This paper proposes a calendar mechanism that uses years and days with a variable duration, in order to accurately represent true solar years and days. In this mechanism, time of day is expressed as a proportion, rather than a number of fixed units, such as hours and minutes. This results in a simple and consistent structure that can universally be applied to any planet, moon or space station with a human presence. Furthermore, it allows analog missions on Earth to use the same calendar mechanism as future missions in space. This paper will outline the principles and details of this mechanism, using Earth and Mars as illustrative examples. Also discussed is the implementation of the mechanism on actual computers, which have a Unix time system clock based on discrete milliseconds.

Abstract: A satellite in low Earth orbit normally passes through Earth's shadow on every revolution. A dawn-dusk sun-synchronous orbit largely avoids it: J2 precession keeps the orbit plane facing the Sun all year, but it follows only the Sun's average position, the mean Sun. In reality the Sun wanders up to 23 degrees around this average, enough to push the plane into shadow for part of the year. We introduce MSSI, a coordinate frame that rotates with the mean Sun. In it, a sun-synchronous orbit is a fixed ring and the real Sun traces the analemma, so the yearly eclipse question becomes static geometry. From that picture we derive how much of each orbit is in shadow, the yearly eclipse for every plane orientation, maps of which directions are never, sometimes or always eclipsed, and the margin a mission has. Fixed planes become eclipse-free above about 1400 km, within a window of node offsets that widens with altitude, and the 06:00 and 18:00 lines turn out to be equivalent. The closed form is checked against a full-force-model numerical propagation and against the published eclipse seasons of three flying missions; the model, its code and an interactive 3-D visualizer are published as open source.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate the demographic evolution of the Japanese astronomy and astrophysics publication community during 1996-2025 using bibliometric data from 23,274 publications in major journals in the field. Participation was highly heterogeneous, with 46% of authors observed in only one publication year within the study period and a smaller core showing long observed participation periods. We define the Community Renewal Rate (CRR) as a community-level measure of renewal, calculated as the fraction of active authors not observed during a specified preceding interval. The 5-year CRR typically remained near one quarter of the active community, with annual values ranging from approximately 21% to 33%, indicating persistent renewal despite substantial community growth. An order-of-magnitude comparison suggests that newly entering students account for only part of the renewal-author population. The image appeared as a ring-like structure, representing the gravitationally lensed and redshifted emission around the central supermassive black hole, M87*, located at the galactic nucleus. However, application of various data analysis and imaging heuristics resulted in divergent representations of M87* fine-scale features, with some external analyses even challenging the fundamental ring-like morphology of the source. Such difficulties arise as a consequence of the inherent sparsity of the very-long baseline interferometry (VLBI) measurements, rendering interferometric image reconstruction an inadequately constrained problem. Moreover, the solution is sensitive to instrument calibration, post-processing algorithms, and imaging hyperparameters. Here, we apply a newly-developed independent imaging algorithm to the M87* dataset, which is free from prescriptive morphological priors and founded on observables immune to instrument calibration errors. We reconstruct the black hole shadow image, and unequivocally reproduce a ring-like morphology, with angular diameter 42.2 +/- 1.5 microarcseconds corresponding to a black hole mass of 6.5 +/- 0.5 billion solar masses assuming nominal scaling parameters, observed at an inclination angle of ~20 deg from the approaching jet.

In the mid-infrared, the most viable solution has been shown to be a formation-flying nulling space interferometer. Aims. In this work, we present the initial prototype for an inter-spacecraft metrology system. This system is a sub-system of the Pyxis/SEIRIOS interferometer, a formation-flying interferometer which serves as a technology demonstrator for the LIFE mission. Methods. We have designed a multi-stage metrology system using three interferometric measurement techniques, covering varying distance resolution ranges. Digital Interferometric (DI) ranging provides the coarsest measurement, while a differential phase read-out provides sub-nm precision. Multi-wavelength interferometry is employed to overcome the phase ambiguity associated with the differential phase readout. Results. A single-wavelength electronic noise floor below 20 pm RMS over 10 s was measured by applying a common photodetector signal to both readout channels, a factor of 28 below the 560 pm requirement. For the multi-wavelength readout, the obtained electronic noise RMS was 556 nm at 10 second integration, decreasing to 200 nm below 3 second integration. The full optical measurement in laboratory air is currently limited by environmental path fluctuations. Conclusions. We demonstrate an initial prototype system for precision ranging between satellites for formation-flying nulling interferometers.

Conclusion: ARSA是一种鲁棒、无参数、局域自适应的改进方案，有效克服RSLA在光学厚区的核心缺陷，适用于多数非相对论RHD模拟，但其优势受限于辐射与气体热容的相对占比。

Abstract: The reduced speed of light approximation (RSLA) is a common approach in radiation-hydrodynamic (RHD) simulations of non-relativistic systems where the gas speed $v_{\rm gas} \ll c$. In the RSLA one eases the severe time step constraint imposed by the speed of light $c$ by replacing it with a reduced value $\hat{c}$ chosen so that $v_{\rm gas} \ll \hat{c} \ll c$. This approach works well in flows with low or moderate optical depth, but fails in optically thick media, where it makes thermal relaxation too slow and gives incorrect RHD wave modes. Here we propose an Adaptive Reduced Speed of Light Approximation (ARSA) that removes these problems by replacing the global speed reduction factor $φ_0 \equiv \hat{c}/c$ with a local, cell-by-cell reduction factor $φ$ derived from a von Neumann stability analysis of the implicit-explicit update used by modern two-moment RHD codes. The resulting limiter has no tunable parameters, can be computed using only local operations, and improves on the classic RSLA by allowing $φ\to 1$ in cells where a reduced speed of light is not required because the exchange with the gas damps unstable modes, allowing recovery of the correct thermal relaxation and wave-damping rates in highly-opaque regions. We compare ARSA to the classic RSLA in a range of standard RHD test problems, and show that in opaque regions where gas holds most of the heat capacity the ARSA is much more accurate than the RSLA at the same time step, or that it can achieve the same accuracy as the RSLA with up to 100 times fewer time steps. However, gains are limited in regions where radiation holds a large share of the heat capacity, as it does in some astrophysical systems.

</details>

Abstract: Gaia has observed every detected source on the sky 50 to 100 times, each time through a window of about 1 by 2 arcseconds at a different scan angle. The resulting one-dimensional flux profiles to be released in Gaia DR4 encode the two-dimensional arrangement of nearby point sources at ~0.1 arcsecond resolution. Repeated observations also constrain their relative motion. We present a method to model the scene with a mixture of moving point sources. Complementing Gaia DPAC's source-environment reconstruction, our approach focuses on close, blended companions and models their relative motion. We first fit each window with a few point sources and obtain their along-scan positions. We then triangulate these positions from different scan angles to identify candidate sources on the sky. A joint forward model of all windows measures positions, fluxes, relative proper motions, and parallax differences, even for blended components. In simulations, we recover companions at separations smaller than the instrumental profile width. For a moving companion at ~75 mas separation with a flux ratio of ~0.2, we obtain a separation precision of ~0.1 mas and a relative proper-motion precision of ~0.1 mas/yr per component, even in a scene contaminated by a third source. These precisions assume a known instrumental profile and independent Gaussian noise. We also recover differential parallax for a chance-aligned source. Modeling nearby contaminants prevents significant biases. With a Gaia-like instrumental profile, sampling and scan law, we show a sensitivity of ~5 mag in contrast at separations of 50-150 mas, and we discuss its dependence on the color term of Gaia's line-spread function. The method could extend the census of close companions and provide relative astrometry, and so dynamical information, along short orbital arcs. External measurements could also guide retrospective searches in Gaia's windows.

Abstract: We investigate superradiant cloud level transitions in stellar-mass black hole binaries with milli-Hertz and multiband gravitational-wave observations. Under the adopted cloud formation and survival conditions, the $\lvert322\rangle\rightarrow\lvert31-1\rangle$ fine transition can imprint phase corrections in the LISA band, while Bohr transitions occurring after the milli-Hertz band observation can be probed through shifts in the merger time. Modeling the coupled cloud-orbit evolution, we find that cloud depletion can begin before resonance and that ionization can advance rather than delay coalescence. For a representative nearby binary, a vacuum injection-recovery analysis yields a cloud-mass-fraction posterior concentrated at $M_c^i/M\lesssim10^{-6}$. Comparing the LISA-predicted coalescence time with a subsequent ground-based measurement provides a complementary test of later cloud evolution. These results motivate complementary searches for ultralight bosons with milli-Hertz and multiband gravitational-wave observations.

### [88] [Drift of a Charged Particle in a Black Hole Magnetosphere: the Effect of Black Hole Spin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19098)
*Ye Shen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协变形式的导心近似方法，用于研究黑洞磁层中带电粒子在强引力场（特别是黑洞自旋）影响下的漂移运动，并推导出适用于强场和高速情形的协变漂移公式，验证了其在弱场低速极限下退化为牛顿结果。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导心近似多基于牛顿框架，缺乏对强引力场（尤其是黑洞自旋）下带电粒子漂移的协变描述；黑洞磁层中稀疏等离子体需碰撞less处理，亟需协变导心理论。

Method: 采用协变导心近似方法，在Kerr时空下建模黑洞磁层中的带电粒子运动；引入引力电磁学语言分析牛顿极限下的旋转引力效应；定义并计算协变漂移率以量化黑洞自旋对漂移的影响。

Result: 推导出协变形式的漂移公式，验证其在弱场低速极限下回归牛顿结果；发现黑洞自旋对漂移的影响在某些极端情况下可与其他因素（如磁场梯度、曲率）相当。

Conclusion: 黑洞自旋可通过协变导心机制显著影响带电粒子漂移，该效应在强场高速条件下不可忽略，为理解黑洞磁层动力学提供了新视角。

Abstract: Sparse plasma immersed in a strong magnetic field is common in astrophysical scenarios, for which a collisionless description is more appropriate. The guiding-center approximation serves as an effective tool for describing the motion of charged particles in a strong magnetic field, revealing the drift, a sub-leading order motion that causes particles to deviate from the magnetic field lines. It is widely accepted that the charged particles drift under the induction of gravity in Newtonian framework. However, a covariant formulation of the guiding-center approximation, as well as the drift of a charged particle induced by strong gravitational field, has yet to be fully explored. As a preliminary study, we aim to analyze the drift of a single charged particle in a black hole magnetosphere via a covariant approach. Particularly, we focus on the effects of black hole spin. We first examine the drift caused by the rotation of a massive object in the Newtonian regime using the language of gravitoelectromagnetism. A covariant formula of drift is derived, which is confirmed to reduce to the Newtonian results in the weak gravitational field and low speed limit. The impact of BH spin on the drift is analyzed in detail in Kerr spacetime specifically, for which we define the drift rate whose value quantifies the significance of the drift. Our results indicate that the impact of BH spin on the drift of a charged particle in a BH magnetosphere can be comparable to that of other factors in some extreme cases.

</details>


### [89] [Chaotic motion of spinning particles in nonlocal gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19191)
*Jianhui Lin,Xiangdong Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在修正的Deser--Woodard非局域引力（NLG）理论中，围绕静态球对称黑洞的自旋诱导混沌行为，发现随着背景偏离广义相对论（GR）程度增加（参数ζ增大），轨道混沌比例显著下降；混沌与规则轨道的引力波谱存在可分辨差异，但混沌本身未必能提升对NLG背景的识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非局域引力理论中极端质量比旋进（EMRI）系统的轨道动力学是否呈现可观测的混沌特征，以检验其能否作为区分NLG与广义相对论的潜在探针。

Method: 采用保守性测试粒子近似建模轨道运动，利用庞加莱截面、旋转曲线和快速李雅普诺夫指标（FLI）量化混沌程度，并对比NLG与GR背景下的引力波形（特别是质量四极矩波形）及其匹配度（mismatch）。

Result: 随着NLG变形参数ζ从0增至0.1，非坠入轨道中被FLI判为混沌的比例由37.5%降至0；混沌与规则轨道的波形在谱平坦度上存在明显局部差异；当调整初始半径使NLG（ζ=1e-6）与GR波形匹配度提升时，原属混沌的NLG轨道仍保持较高最小失配。

Conclusion: 背景几何变形可抑制混沌，且混沌轨道的辐射谱具有独特性，但混沌动力学本身并未被证实能增强对NLG背景的识别能力；轨道动力学变化与辐射特征存在关联，但尚不足以确立混沌为有效甄别工具。

Abstract: We study spin-induced chaos around a static, spherically symmetric black hole in revised Deser--Woodard nonlocal gravity (NLG), using the conservative test-body approximation relevant to extreme-mass-ratio inspirals. The deformation parameter $ζ$ describes departures from Schwarzschild spacetime. Poincaré sections, rotation curves, and fast Lyapunov indicators (FLIs) reveal changes in resonant structures and a decreasing fraction of non-plunging trajectories classified as chaotic within the sampled family, from $37.5\%$ at $ζ=0$ to zero at $ζ=0.1$. The FLI classifications of selected trajectories are reflected in their approximate mass-quadrupole waveforms: neighboring chaotic and regular trajectories show a marked local spectral-flatness contrast, which weakens in backgrounds where both are classified as regular. To assess whether a transition from chaotic to regular motion caused by the background deformation helps distinguish NLG from GR, we compare NLG waveforms at $ζ=10^{-6}$ with GR waveforms generated at different initial radii. For a trajectory whose classification changes between the backgrounds, this adjustment substantially reduces the mismatch. The selected NLG waveforms from chaotic orbits retain larger minimum mismatches than those from regular orbits, although sensitivity to initial conditions may also hinder signal recovery. These results connect background-dependent orbital dynamics with radiation spectra, but do not establish that chaos offers an advantage for identifying the background geometry.

</details>


### [90] [Detectability of Eccentric Intermediate Mass-Ratio Inspiral in the Decihertz Band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21323)
*Devesh Giri,Sajal Mukherjee,Apratim Ganguly,Sanjit Mitra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在decihertz引力波频段（约0.01–1 Hz）中，中间质量比旋进（IMRI）系统因轨道偏心率导致的圆形模板匹配失效问题；发现当初始偏心率e₀超过约0.03时，圆形模板拟合因子下降至97%以下，且参数估计产生数个百分点偏差；即使极小偏心率（如5×10⁻⁴）也可被分辨，表明未来探测器需采用含偏心率的波形模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: decihertz频段引力波探测器（如DECIGO、LGWA、IndIGO-D）将观测动力学形成的具有显著偏心率的中间质量比旋进（IMRI）；而当前主流搜索与参数估计流程依赖于圆形轨道模板，其适用性亟待检验。

Method: 基于黑洞性质微扰理论波形（FastEMRIWaveforms），对典型IMRI（M=10⁴ M⊙, q=100，最后10小时观测）计算偏心信号与圆形模板的拟合因子（Fitting Factor），在B-DECIGO、LGWA-Si、IndIGO-D噪声曲线及白谱假设下，用差分进化算法最大化匹配，同时分析失配来源（径向Teukolsky谐波激发与轨道衰减变慢）。

Result: 拟合因子随初始偏心率e₀增大而下降，在e₀≈0.03时突破3%失配阈值；e₀=0.1时总质量与质量比估计偏差达数个百分点；结合信噪比依赖的可分辨性判据，B-DECIGO和IndIGO-D可分辨e₀≈5×10⁻⁴、LGWA-Si可分辨e₀≈5×10⁻³的偏心率；失配主因是圆形模板无法表征偏心轨道激发的侧带功率及更慢的轨道衰减。

Conclusion: 为保障decihertz频段IMRI的可靠探测与参数估计，必须发展并使用含偏心率的波形模型及相应模板库，不能继续依赖圆形模板。

Abstract: The proposed decihertz gravitational-wave (GW) band $(\sim 10^{-2}-1\,\Hz)$ will observe the late inspiral of intermediate mass-ratio inspirals (IMRIs), binaries with mass ratios $10^2 \lesssim q \lesssim 10^4$ that assemble dynamically in dense stellar environments and are expected to retain measurable eccentricity. Search and parameter-estimation pipelines are predominantly built around circular templates, and we test this assumption. Using black-hole perturbation theory waveforms from \textsc{FastEMRIWaveforms} for a representative IMRI ($M=10^4\,\modot$, $q=100$, observed for its final $10\,\hr$), we compute fitting factors between eccentric signals and circular templates, maximizing over the template's total mass and mass ratio with a differential-evolution search, for the B-DECIGO, LGWA-Si, and IndIGO-D noise curves and a flat PSD. The mismatch grows with initial eccentricity $e_0$ and crosses the standard $3\%$ threshold at $e_0\simeq3\times10^{-2}$, well within the range allowed by the waveform model and expected from dynamical formation. The best-fitting circular templates also bias the total mass and mass ratio by several percent at $e_0=0.1$. Combining the mismatch with the signal-to-noise-dependent distinguishability criterion, we find that for a source at $1\,\mathrm{Gpc}$, eccentricities as small as $e_0\simeq5\times10^{-4}$ (B-DECIGO and IndIGO-D) and $5\times10^{-3}$ (LGWA-Si) are resolvable, one to two orders of magnitude below where detection efficiency degrades. The loss of match arises from the excitation of radial Teukolsky harmonics, which spread power into sidebands that circular templates cannot represent, and from the slower orbital decay of eccentric binaries at fixed initial semi-latus rectum. Robust detection and parameter estimation of IMRIs with DECIGO, LGWA, and IndIGO-D will require eccentric waveform models and template banks.

</details>


### [91] [Numerical evidence for the validity of the inverse hoop conjecture for black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19240)
*Shahar Hod*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文为非稳态黑洞构型提供了逆环（IH）猜想成立的证据，验证了CGP最近构造的所有非稳态黑洞解均满足IH关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证逆 hoop (IH) 猜想在非稳态黑洞构型中的适用性，拓展其在更广泛黑洞场景下的有效性。

Method: 分析Croft、García-Moreno 和 Pani (CGP) 最近构造的非稳态黑洞解，检验其是否满足 IH 关系 ${\cal M}_{\text{irr}}\leq {\cal C}_{\text{s}}/4π$。

Result: 所有 CGP 构造的非稳态黑洞解均满足 IH 关系，为该猜想在非稳态情形下的成立提供了支持性证据。

Conclusion: IH 猜想不仅适用于稳态黑洞，也适用于非稳态黑洞构型，增强了其作为普适几何约束的可信度。

Abstract: The inverse hoop (IH) conjecture, ${\cal M}_{\text{irr}}\leq {\cal C}_{\text{s}}/4π$, originally discussed in the context of stationary black-hole configurations, suggests that the surface area of a black hole cannot undergo unconstrained geometric wrinkling (here ${\cal M}_{\text{irr}}=\sqrt{{\cal A}/16π}$ is the irreducible mass associated with the horizon area ${\cal A}$ of the black hole and ${\cal C}_{\text{s}}$ is the circumference length of the smallest ring that can engulf the horizon of the black hole in every direction). In the present compact paper we provide evidence supporting the validity of the IH conjecture for non-stationary black-hole configurations. In particular, we show that all the non-stationary black-hole solutions recently constructed by Croft, García-Moreno, and Pani (CGP) [arXiv:2611.13266] satisfy the IH relation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在统一若干重要几何构造并为广义相对论中各类无穷远边界（空间、时间、类光）提供一致的共形紧化与环境描述框架。

Method: 构建由两个权为1的共形标量组成的双尺度系统，满足近爱因斯坦方程及特定正则性条件，以此作为共形几何基础框架。

Result: 实现了Fefferman-Graham环境度量、Brinkmann空间与超静态真空时空的共形紧化统一；给出了Spi/Ti的新环境构造，其中Spi/Ti与类光无穷远分别自然对应于环境空间的边界面与角；该系统可作为研究场在各类无穷远处渐近行为的背景几何。

Conclusion: 双尺度共形系统为理解广义相对论中多类无穷远结构及其物理场渐近行为提供了统一、自然且几何清晰的框架。

Abstract: We show that certain geometric constructions, including the Fefferman-Graham ambient metric, Brinkmann spaces, and ultrastatic vacuum spacetimes, admit natural conformal compactifications and can be unified within a single framework arising naturally from conformal geometry. The framework is based on a two-scale system comprising two weight-one conformal scalars satisfying almost-Einstein equations and certain regularity conditions. As a particular instance of this unifying framework, we obtain a new ambient construction for Spi and Ti, the extended spatial and temporal infinities of Ricci-flat spacetimes. A remarkable feature of this construction is that Spi/Ti and the null infinity of the corresponding spacetimes are naturally realised as a boundary face and the corner of the two-scale ambient space, respectively. We also demonstrate how the two-scale system can serve as a background ambient geometry for studying the asymptotic behaviour of (gauge) fields at spatial, temporal, and null infinity.

</details>

Conclusion: 该模型表明，在适当构造的高阶矢量-标量耦合理论中，可实现既满足基本物理条件（如能量条件）、又具有良好扰动性质（稳定且有界）的平滑宇宙反弹，为奇点回避提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We construct a geodesically complete bounce in cubic generalised Proca theory with a free canonical scalar as the matter field. The temporal vector evolves along its algebraic constraint and supports the transition from contraction to expansion while matter obeys the null energy condition. This evolution permits nonzero derivative mixing at the bounce. The kinetic coefficients are positive at every finite time for all nonzero comoving wavenumbers, and short-wavelength perturbations are stable throughout the solution. Following the linear equations to both infinite-time limits also shows that local perturbations are bounded for all nonzero comoving wavenumbers.

</details>


### [94] [Black holes in Myrzakulov $F(R,T)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19500)
*Alexandre Landry,Ratbay Myrzakulov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了度规-仿射Myrzakulov F(R,T)引力理论中的真空黑洞解，其中T是二次挠率不变量而非物质能量动量张量的迹；通过变分方法并限定于度规相容且完全反对称挠率的几何，发现非零轴向挠率下存在特殊分支，使度规满足含有效宇宙常数的爱因斯坦方程，并构造了光滑穿过类时Killing视界的挠率化Schwarzschild-(反)de Sitter解；进一步在球对称轴向假设下分类Ricci对称仿射联络，并验证了相关恒等式与退化特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索度规-仿射框架下包含挠率不变量T的F(R,T)引力理论中真空黑洞解的存在性与结构，弥补传统广义相对论与纯Ricci标量f(R)理论在描述挠率自由度方面的不足。

Method: 对度规和独立仿射联络全部分量进行无约束变分，再施加度规相容性与完全反对称挠率约束；采用球对称轴向挠率ansatz，分析Einstein方程与仿射曲率不变量；通过坐标水平的曲率收缩与分量检验验证变分推导。

Result: 发现非零轴向挠率下存在特殊参数分支α+β+3γT_*²=0，此时度规满足带有效宇宙常数Λ_*=γT_*³/α的Einstein方程；构造出光滑穿过Killing视界的挠率化Schwarzschild-(anti-)de Sitter解；识别出轴向二次型的零方向，并区分其与完整度规-仿射稳定性分析的差异。

Conclusion: 在局部范围内证实了MG-I理论中挠率化黑洞解的存在性与可构造性，但未证明全局可延拓性、所有挠率构型的物理不等价性或动力学稳定性。

Abstract: We investigate vacuum black-hole solutions in metric-affine Myrzakulov $F(R,T)$ gravity (MG-I), where $R$ is the affine Ricci scalar and $T$ is a quadratic torsion invariant, not the trace of the matter energy-momentum tensor. For the polynomial model $F(R,T)=αR+βT+γT^3$, with $α\neq0$, we first vary the metric and all components of the independent affine connection, and only then specialize to metric-compatible geometry with totally antisymmetric torsion. Nonzero axial torsion admits an exceptional branch $α+β+3γT_*^2=0$, on which the metric satisfies the Einstein equations with effective cosmological parameter $Λ_*=γT_*^3/α$. We establish a local existence result and construct torsionful Schwarzschild-(anti-)de Sitter representatives that are smooth through a selected future Killing horizon. Within the spherically symmetric axial ansatz, we characterize Ricci-symmetric affine connections by the condition $(fV-U)'=0$ and compare a constant-$W=fV-U$ family with a constant-$U$ family. Although these connections share the same metric and torsion scalar, their affine curvature invariants generally differ. Independent coordinate-level curvature contractions and component tests of the unrestricted metric and connection variations corroborate the identities used in the construction; these checks complement the analytic variational derivation. Finally, we identify null directions of the restricted axial quadratic form and distinguish this degeneracy from a complete metric-affine stability analysis. Our conclusions are local and do not establish global extendibility, physical inequivalence of all torsion profiles, or dynamical stability.

</details>

*A. C. Khunt*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解中子星内部物态方程（EOS）对强引力场中轨道动力学及QPO现象的影响，特别是通过RPM模型将观测到的QPO频率与中子星质量、自转和四极矩联系起来，以区分中子星与黑洞时空。

Conclusion: RPM结合真实中子星外部时空可有效约束EOS相关参数；观测QPO频率对中子星四极矩敏感，不能用Kerr度规简单替代；部分源的数据暗示极高自转频率或最大质量极限，挑战现有EOS模型。

Abstract: We study the relativistic precession model (RPM) of quasi-periodic oscillations (QPOs) in the spacetimes of slowly rotating neutron stars computed from the equation of state (EOS). For four EOSs (WFF1, G3, FSUGarnet and IOPB-I) we solve the Hartle--Thorne equations to second order in the angular velocity and compute the orbital and epicyclic frequencies of the exterior metric without expanding in the spin or the quadrupole. At $1.9\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$ the spin-induced quadrupole is 2--4 times that of a black hole with the same spin; it lowers the nodal precession frequency by up to 25 per cent near the innermost stable circular orbit and limits the nodal frequency that any spin can produce. An eccentric, tilted geodesic around a $1.92\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$ star spinning at 363~Hz precesses at the epicyclic rates, and the power spectrum of a hot spot on it peaks at the orbital, periastron and nodal precession frequencies. Applied to the atoll sources 4U 1636$-$53 and 4U 1728$-$34, the RPM gives masses of 2.02--$2.15\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$ and 1.88--$1.92\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$. For 4U 1728$-$34 the low-frequency QPO favours stars with large moments of inertia, $I\simeq2.5\times10^{45}$~g\,cm$^{2}$. Without an EOS the QPOs give $M=1.920^{+0.014}_{-0.012}\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$, $j=0.162^{+0.023}_{-0.011}$ and $\bar Q=2.2^{+1.1}_{-0.8}$; a Kerr spacetime with the same nodal frequency would underestimate the moment of inertia by 22 per cent. For the Z sources GX 5$-$1 and GX 340+0 the horizontal-branch oscillations cannot be twice the nodal precession frequency for any of the four EOSs: the fits are driven to spins of 730--745~Hz and to the maximum mass.

</details>

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了Starobinsky标量子（scalaron）在外场中的荷（charge）的普适公式，适用于任意静态球对称物质源；发现中子星的标量荷由物质压强显著调制，且与致密度呈准普适关系；该荷与潮汐形变率、转动惯量高度相关；黑洞在真空下标量荷为零，但吸积盘存在时可产生非零荷，其大小由盘外缘决定；研究还结合NICER和LIGO/Virgo观测对理论参数进行了约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Starobinsky引力模型中的标量子（scalaron）作为额外自由度，其外部‘荷’不源于黑洞真空几何，而由物质能动张量的迹（ρ−3p）决定；需建立普适公式以量化该荷，并连接中子星结构、引力波观测与实验室约束。

Method: 推导静态球对称源下标量子外场荷的主公式；结合中子星内部物态方程（6种 realistic EOS）与Eardley敏感性分析；自洽求解Einstein帧结构方程；将理论荷与NICER半径、GW170817潮汐形变、GW230529无质量偶极子限制及未来Einstein Telescope/Cosmic Explorer灵敏度进行Fisher分析；显式对比实验室对纯R+αR²模型的约束。

Result: 获得标量荷q的解析主公式；中子星q由致密度主导，压强起减项作用；q与潮汐形变率Λ、转动惯量I分别达~0.1%和~1%精度关联；自洽计算下q在固定致密度下偏离测试场值<4%；吸积黑洞的q = sinh(m_φ r_d)/(m_φ r_d)，由盘外缘r_d主导；光子无法在领头阶探测该荷，高阶修正可忽略；基于GW230529给出有限标量子质量m_φ的Fisher灵敏度预测。

Conclusion: 标量子荷是连接恒星结构、强场引力波与超越广义相对论检验的关键可观测量；中子星提供高精度标定，吸积黑洞提供新机制；理论与多信使观测一致，且可被下一代探测器有效约束；真空黑洞严格q=0，故该荷可作为区分真实黑洞与致密星体的潜在探针。

Abstract: The Starobinsky scalaron is sourced by the trace of the stress--energy, so its exterior charge is set by the matter around an object and not by the object's vacuum geometry. We derive a master formula for that charge for any static, spherically symmetric source, evaluate it first for neutron stars, where the interior is known, and then for accreting black holes, where it is not. For a star the pressure subtracts: on the interior metric with the Eardley sensitivity, and the charge is a quasi-universal function of the compactness. Six realistic equations of state confirm the relation but lie above the coefficient calibrated on polytropes; the refit reproduces their mean to 0.007. The trace $ρ-3p$ changes sign in the cores of massive stars, and the charge also tracks the tidal deformability and the moment of inertia to a few parts in 1000 and 100. Solving the Einstein-frame structure equations self-consistently moves a star along this relation and leaves its charge at fixed compactness within 4 per cent of the test-field value. We compare the charge with NICER radii and the GW170817 tidal deformability, and use the published massless dipole constraint from GW230529 to calibrate a Fisher estimate of the sensitivity of a similar binary to a finite scalaron range, projected to the Einstein Telescope and Cosmic Explorer; the range is a free parameter, and the laboratory bound on pure $R+αR^2$ is confronted explicitly. A vacuum black hole has q=0; a hole with a disc has $q=\sinh(m_φr_d)/(m_φr_d)$, controlled by the disc's outer edge, which makes it the counterpart in which the same integral is dominated by matter far from the centre; light is blind to the charge at leading order, and the correction beyond it is negligible for any self-consistent disc.

</details>

Conclusion: Dymnikova型各向异性物质可支持具有良好几何与热力学性质的负宇宙常数黑弦解，为研究正则黑洞类模型在高维或非球对称背景下的适用性提供了新例证。

Abstract: We obtain a new static black string solution of Einstein's equations with a negative cosmological constant sourced by a Dymnikova-type anisotropic matter distribution. At large radial distances, the geometry approaches the Lemos black string, while the behavior near the central region is characterized by an effective curvature parameter. We examine the weak and strong energy conditions, the horizon structure, and the curvature invariants. The Ricci and Kretschmann scalars remain finite at the origin. We also investigate the Hawking temperature and heat capacity, with particular attention to the dependence of the thermodynamic behavior on the Dymnikova scale.

</details>


### [98] [Multi-throat generalized Ellis--Bronnikov wormholes with asymptotic string-cloud behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19808)
*M. S. Cunha,C. R. Muniz,V. H. U. Borralho,G. Alencar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类通过局部变形广义Ellis-Bronnikov径向轮廓并引入渐近角亏的球对称超静态可穿越虫洞，其喉部数量（1–4个）由变形参数决定；作者重构了包含标量场与非线性电磁场耦合的广义相对论物质源，并分析了能量条件、光子环稳定性及NEC积分性质。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索具有多喉结构的正则、渐近圆锥虫洞几何，理解其拓扑变化（分岔）、物质源构造及能量条件满足区域，特别是非线性电动力学耦合如何消除单值性障碍。

Method: 基于解析变形的areal-radius度规构造多喉虫洞；通过求解爱因斯坦方程反演物质源（含规范标量、移位对称幻标量与磁型非线性电磁场）；分析能量条件（特别是径向零能量条件NEC）、Nandi-Zhang-Kumar体积积分、光子环存在性与稳定性。

Abstract: We study an ultrastatic, spherically symmetric family of traversable wormholes obtained by locally deforming a generalized Ellis--Bronnikov areal-radius profile in a spacetime with an asymptotic angular deficit. Depending on the deformation parameters, the same analytic family contains one, two, three, or four throats. The corresponding bifurcation curves are obtained in dimensionless form, and the resulting geometries are regular and asymptotically conical. We also reconstruct a source in General Relativity using a canonical scalar, a shift-symmetric phantom scalar, and a magnetic nonlinear electromagnetic field coupled to the canonical scalar. In the reference multi-throat geometry, the coupling removes a branch obstruction of an uncoupled single-valued nonlinear electrodynamics (NED); the same obstruction occurs whenever equal values of the magnetic invariant require different values of $\mathcal G_F$. For $\mathcal G(F)=F+λ\sqrt F$, the NED sector carries the leading asymptotic stress, which scales as $r^{-2}$ and approaches the string-cloud equation of state. The total stress tensor satisfies pointwise energy conditions in finite belly-centered regions of representative two- and four-throat solutions, while the throats violate the radial null energy condition (NEC) and the violation extends into integrable asymptotic tails. The Nandi--Zhang--Kumar covariant volume integral of $ρ+p_r$ is quadratic in the deformation amplitude; its zero is distinct from the bifurcation curves. Away from saddle-node boundaries, each throat supports a radially unstable background-metric light ring, and each intermediate belly supports a radially stable one, with an internally trapped null family in the double-throat case. These results place the topology, source reconstruction, integrated NEC content, and background-metric circular-orbit structure in a common parameter-space description.

</details>


### [99] [Quasinormal Modes and Environmental Trapping around Charged Letelier Black Holes with Perfect-Fluid Dark Matter and Quintessence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19935)
*Semra Gurtas Dogan,Abdullah Guvendi,Hassan Hassanabadi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了被对数完美流体暗物质（PFDM）和精质（quintessence）环绕的带电Letelier黑洞的无质量标量和电磁场拟正则模（QNMs），分别在零宇宙常数（Λ=0）和渐近反德西特（AdS）时空下；结果表明，不同暗物质/能量成分及黑洞参数显著影响振荡频率与衰减率，AdS情形需用切比雪夫伪谱法求解，并发现大量长寿命模式，但未发现不稳定的增长模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解暗物质与暗能量如何影响黑洞动力学及稳定性，特别是其拟正则模谱特性，为引力波天文学和黑洞热力学提供理论依据。
