Result: 发现弦云强度和精质增强会降低振荡频率与阻尼率，而电荷或PFDM参数β增大（β>0对应负能量密度）则提升二者；eikonal极限下光子球轨道角频率与Lyapunov指数可较好复现高ℓ模；AdS中存在跨越五个数量级阻尼率的长寿命束缚模，其衰减速率与频率/势垒顶频率比相关；所有测试场情形均未发现增长模。

Conclusion: 在所考察的测试场与参数范围内，系统未表现出线性不稳定性，但该结果不能外推至完全耦合的非线性引力-物质系统；AdS中的丰富长寿命模暗示其可能具有可观测的引力波回响特征。

Abstract: We compute the massless scalar quasinormal spectra of charged Letelier black holes surrounded by logarithmic perfect fluid dark matter (PFDM) and quintessence, for $Λ=0$ and for asymptotically anti-de Sitter (AdS) spacetimes, together with the test Maxwell spectra for $Λ=0$. For $Λ=0$, sixth order WKB values with Padé resummation agree with independent time domain evolutions to within $0.06\%$ for the fundamental modes. Stronger string cloud and quintessence lower both the oscillation frequencies and the damping rates, while a larger charge or a larger positive PFDM parameter $β$ raises both. In our convention $β>0$ means negative PFDM energy density; negative $β$ gives a positive density and reverses the shift. The angular frequency and Lyapunov exponent of the unstable circular null orbit reproduce the eikonal trends and approximate the $\ell=2$ WKB frequencies at the percent level. For $Λ<0$ the scalar potential diverges at the AdS boundary and the single barrier WKB formula does not apply. We therefore solve the eigenvalue problem with a Chebyshev pseudospectral method in ingoing Eddington-Finkelstein coordinates, with regularity at the horizon and a Dirichlet condition at the boundary, and check the modes against time domain evolutions and Schwarzschild-AdS benchmarks. The AdS spectrum contains long-lived modes trapped between the photon sphere barrier and the reflecting boundary, with damping rates spanning about five orders of magnitude. The damping correlates with the ratio of the oscillation frequency to the barrier top frequency, consistent with leakage through the barrier. No growing modes are found in the test field sectors and parameter ranges studied, which does not establish the stability of the fully coupled system.

</details>


### [100] [Repeated Gravitational-Wave Lensing from Binary Black Holes on Eccentric and Precessing Orbits around Spinning Supermassive Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20416)
*Jie Wu,Mengfei Sun,Jin Li,Zhoujian Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了双黑洞（BBH）绕超大质量黑洞（SMBH）运行时，其引力波（GW）因多次引力透镜效应而产生的调制现象，重点分析外轨道偏心率、倾角和相对论进动对透镜事件及参数估计的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受前期关于轨道诱导引力波调制研究的启发，本文旨在探究演化中的分层三体系统中重复引力透镜效应对引力波信号的影响及其在参数推断中的价值。

Method: 采用后牛顿动力学描述外轨道，引力波模板包含运动源效应与de Sitter进动；使用事件局域点质量透镜模型追踪源-透镜对准变化与GW频率演化；结合LISA一年观测模拟，进行透镜事件识别、Fisher矩阵不确定性估计及忽略透镜效应时的波形残差分析。

Result: 外轨道演化显著改变连续透镜事件的时间与放大率，可使初始不满足透镜条件的构型进入透镜区域；固定圆轨道且无进动的近似会遗漏大量真实透镜事件；倾角与SMBH自旋参数的不确定性中位数降幅最大；部分系统中，即使局部调整无透镜模型参数，透镜引起的波形差异仍持续存在。

Conclusion: 重复引力透镜效应可为LISA观测的分层双黑洞系统提供SMBH性质与外轨道几何结构的互补约束。

Abstract: Gravitational waves (GWs) from a stellar-mass binary black hole (BBH) orbiting a supermassive black hole (SMBH) can experience repeated gravitational lensing as the BBH passes behind the SMBH. Our previous studies of orbit-induced GW modulation motivate this investigation of repeated lensing in evolving hierarchical triple systems. We investigate how eccentricity, inclination, and relativistic precession of the outer orbit affect successive lensing events and parameter inference. The outer orbit follows post-Newtonian dynamics, and the inspiral waveform includes moving-source effects and de Sitter precession. An event-local point-mass lensing model tracks the changing source--lens alignment and GW frequency. For a one-year LISA observation, we examine lensing-event identification, Fisher-matrix estimates of parameter uncertainties, and waveform residuals when lensing is omitted. Our results show that outer-orbit evolution changes the timing and amplification of successive lensing events and can bring initially unfavorable configurations into the lensing region. A fixed circular, nonprecessing approximation misses all lensing events for a substantial fraction of the selected lensed systems. The median fractional reductions in uncertainty are largest for the outer-orbit inclination and SMBH spin. For some systems above the adopted detection threshold, lensing-induced waveform differences persist after local adjustments to the parameters of the unlensed model. Repeated lensing can thus provide complementary constraints on SMBH properties and outer-orbit geometry in LISA observations of hierarchical BBHs.

</details>


### [101] [Casimir-like wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20168)
*Mauricio Cataldo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了由能量密度ρ∝1/r⁴且满足状态方程p_r = ωρ的球对称物质源驱动的时空，推导出爱因斯坦方程的一般解，涵盖黑洞、裸奇点与可穿越虫洞；发现仅Casimir型（ω>0）和幻影型（ω<−1）物质能支持可穿越虫洞；Casimir型虫洞（如ω=3）在奇点附近自然导出p_t→−ρ，其几何与物理性质（如有限奇异物质总量、人类可穿越性）被系统分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索具有特定径向依赖能量密度（ρ∝1/r⁴）的球对称物质能否支撑可穿越虫洞，并厘清不同物态方程（ω取值）下所允许的时空结构（黑洞/裸奇点/虫洞）及其物理可行性（如奇异物质量、人类 traversability）。

Method: 解析求解球对称爱因斯坦场方程，针对ρ∝1/r⁴与p_r = ωρ的物质源，获得通解；结合虫洞喉部“张开条件”（flare-out condition）、能量条件、几何嵌入图、旅行者加速度与潮汐力分析，评估各类解的物理可实现性与可穿越性。

Result: 得到含积分常数和ω参数的完整解族；确认仅ω>0（Casimir型）和ω<−1（phantom）可支持可穿越虫洞；ω=3时横向压强p_t自动趋于−ρ；奇异物质总量有限且正比于喉半径；当喉半径r₀≳10⁸ m时，自由下落满足人类可穿越所有条件；嵌入图揭示三种几何相。

Conclusion: ρ∝1/r⁴类物质可统一描述多种紧致天体解，其中Casimir型（ω>0）虫洞虽具Casimir应力-能量张量形式，但无需量子Casimir效应即可经典实现；其尺度可调、奇异物质有限、宏观尺度下可穿越，为虫洞物理提供了新经典范例。

Abstract: We investigate spherically symmetric spacetimes sourced by matter with energy density $ρ\propto 1/r^{4}$ and equation of state $p_r = ωρ$. We derive the most general solution of Einstein's equations for this class of sources, obtaining black holes, naked singularities and traversable wormholes, parametrized by the integration constants and $ω$. We show that only two classes of matter can support traversable wormholes: Casimir-type matter with $ω> 0$ and phantom matter with $ω< -1$, while quintessence and conventional dark energy are excluded by the flare-out condition. For the Casimir value $ω= 3$, the transverse pressure approaches $p_t \to -ρ$ near the central singularity, so the full algebraic structure of the Casimir stress-energy tensor, $ρ\propto -1/r^{4}$, $p_r = 3ρ$, $p_t = -ρ$, is not imposed but emerges from Einstein's equations. Treating the amplitude of the energy density as a free classical parameter, we obtain wormholes with throat radii from microscopic to astrophysical scales. These Casimir-like configurations share the functional form of the Casimir tensor but are not generated by the physical Casimir effect; Garattini's Planck-scale Casimir wormhole is recovered when the quantum Casimir energy scale is imposed. The total amount of exotic matter is finite for arbitrary $ω$ and scales linearly with the throat radius. Analyzing the traveler's acceleration, tidal forces and transit times, we show that radial free fall satisfies all human traversability conditions for $r_0 \gtrsim 10^{8}$ m. Finally, embedding diagrams illustrate three geometric regimes depending on $ω$.

</details>


### [102] [Canonical metrics on punctured spheres, II: compactified moduli spaces of black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20282)
*Boris Botvinnik*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了S^n上带ℓ个穿孔的标量曲率为零度量g_{p_ℓ}及其质量矩阵η在穿孔碰撞时的渐近行为，揭示了质量发散规律、构造了重正化质量矩阵，并建立了其与Axelrod-Singer紧化空间及泡树结构的几何联系，特别对n=3情形讨论了黑洞合并中的视界性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多黑洞初始数据（如Brill-Lindquist）在穿孔（即黑洞位置）相互靠近甚至碰撞时的几何与质量行为，尤其是质量发散机制、紧化结构及物理意义（如视界演化）。

Method: 运用渐近分析、模空间紧化（Axelrod-Singer紧化\overline{M}{}^{AS}_ℓ）、重正化技术、尺度变换与几何收敛论证；引入嵌套簇的泡树结构描述退化过程，并结合Schwarzschild颈、最小球面等几何对象分析n=3情形。

Result: 1) 穿孔聚类坍缩时，质量矩阵沿显式方向E(A)对数发散：η = E(A) ln(1/t) + O(1)；2) 重正化质量矩阵光滑延拓至\overline{M}{}^{AS}_ℓ各层，并按泡树顶点自然分解；3) 几何上，各顶点对应一个完备标量平直度量，相邻部分由Schwarzschild颈连接；4) n=3时，颈含最小球面（即合并黑洞的共同表观视界），并提出外层视界成分的判定猜想；5) 质量矩阵在泡旋转下不变，定义在Deligne-Mumford型商空间上。

Conclusion: 穿孔碰撞的退化结构由泡树编码，质量矩阵的发散与重正化反映了多黑洞系统在极限下的分层几何与物理质量分配；该框架统一刻画了标量平直度量的模空间紧化、黑洞合并动力学的静态初态特征，以及Penrose不等式等质量约束的几何基础。

Abstract: Let $g_{\mathfrak{p}_{\ell}}$ denote the canonical scalar-flat metric on $S^n\setminus \mathfrak{p}_{\ell}$ introduced in Part~I, which for $n=3$ corresponds to the Brill--Lindquist initial data for $\ell$ black holes, and let $η\in H_{\ell}$ be its associated mass matrix. In this paper, we investigate the asymptotic behavior of the metrics and the mass matrix $η$ as subsets of punctures collide. Such collisions cause every mass to diverge; if a cluster $A$ of black holes collapses at a relative scale $t$, the mass matrix satisfies $η=E(A)\ln(1/t)+O(1)$, diverging along an explicit ray $E(A)\in H_{\ell}$. For nested families of clusters, this degeneracy structure naturally organizes into a space of rooted trees. Subtracting the divergence gives a renormalized mass matrix which extends smoothly to every stratum of the Axelrod--Singer compactified moduli space $\overline{M}{}^{\,\mathrm{AS}}_\ell= \overline C_{\ell-1}(\mathbb{R}^n)/\mathrm O(n)$ and splits naturally into one mass matrix per vertex of the bubble tree.
  Geometrically, at each vertex carrying punctures the suitably rescaled metrics converge to a complete scalar-flat metric with the nodes filled in, adjacent such pieces being joined by Schwarzschild necks, and the renormalized matrix acting as its mass data. For $n=3$, the necks contain minimal spheres, which are common apparent horizons of the merging holes. At vertices bearing two holes, we determine on which side of the limiting horizon each node lies, and we formulate as a conjecture the resulting criterion for a neck to be a component of the outermost horizon. We also compare the mass lower bound of Part~I with the Riemannian Penrose inequality. Because the mass matrix is invariant under rotations of the bubbles, it lives on a Deligne--Mumford-type quotient of the compactification.

</details>


### [103] [Harmonic branches of Einstein--ModMax--scalar field gravity: exact solutions and regularity II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20529)
*Leonel Bixano,Tonatiuh Matos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Einstein--ModMax--Scalar场引力理论中的谐波解分支，特别关注电磁相位非恒定的情况，并构建了多种已知和新的精确解，包括ModMax扩展的Schwarzschild、RN、Taub--NUT及Melvin型背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为拓展广义相对论与非线性电磁理论（ModMax）及标量场耦合下的精确解谱系，特别是突破电磁相位恒定这一常见限制，探索更一般的静态与稳态带电/旋涡解。

Method: 通过求解谐波函数族对应的场方程，重构物理度规与场变量；施加正则视界条件；利用定理区分恒定相位与电荷重标度情形；结合轴对称LWP重构方法导出Melvin-旋涡等背景下的精确解。

Result: 获得了包括静态dyon、受限稳态带标量场解、ModMax版Schwarzschild外区、Melvin-旋涡背景下的精确解；明确了ModMax对RN、Taub--NUT等经典解的推广；刻画了视界处度规与电磁场的光滑性及构型延拓行为。

Conclusion: 非恒定电磁相位显著丰富了解空间，揭示了ModMax理论中电磁势与度规、标量场间的新耦合机制；所构建的谐波解目录统一涵盖并推广了大量已有Maxwell引力解，并提供了新的可观测物理场景。

Abstract: We integrate harmonic solution branches of Einstein--ModMax--Scalar field gravity, keeping the electromagnetic phase \(\tanΘ=\mathcal{G}/\mathcal{F}\) non-constant. For each family we first solve the field equations $(a(\s),b(\s),c(\s))$ and reconstruct the physical functions $f,ω,k,A_t,A_\varphi,φ$. In the same section we then select the Schwarzschild or Coulomb harmonic function, impose the regular horizon conditions, identify the ModMax solution, and state its Maxwell limit. Two theorems distinguish a constant electromagnetic phase from an exact static charge rescaling and show how twist prevents constant diagonal rescalings of the electromagnetic potentials at fixed metric and scalar. The catalogue covers known RN and charged Taub--NUT solutions, single-charge configurations, explicit static dyons with scalar-dependent couplings, and a restricted stationary family with an active scalar. We distinguish new explicit ModMax extensions of known Maxwell profiles from the nonfrozen stationary integration. The axial LWP reconstruction recovers the known Melvin--swirling background and gives an exact ModMax Schwarzschild exterior within that background. At its horizon, smooth metric and electromagnetic limits are distinguished from the constitutive continuation at a null equatorial circle. Uncharged configurations and earlier Maxwell fields are traced back to the preceding harmonic catalogue.

</details>


### [104] [Quasinormal modes of Kerr--de Sitter black holes in semi-open systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20627)
*Ming-Fei Ji,Liang-Bi Wu,Yu-Sen Zhou,Li-Ming Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有部分反射内边界的Kerr-de Sitter黑洞的引力准正规模（QNMs）谱，通过将角向和径向Teukolsky方程约化为Heun方程，并数值分析s=-2、ℓ=m=2模式；发现随着反射率增加，出现回声分支并趋近实轴，而旋转增大则导致不同模式分别趋近实轴、保持阻尼或水平移动；识别出超辐射放大与反射壁能量注入两类不稳定性机制；推导了Teukolsky振幅反射率与能量反射率之比，指出后者在mΩ(r₊)处可发散；还发现基于渐近振幅的反射边界条件会引发极点相关共振，而用Sasaki-Nakamura变量施加有限半径Robin条件可消除该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解半开放Kerr-de Sitter黑洞系统中内边界条件如何影响其准正规模谱结构与稳定性，尤其关注部分反射边界引入的新物理行为（如回声、不稳定性机制）。

Method: 将Teukolsky方程约化为Heun方程，对s=-2、ℓ=m=2模式进行数值求解；系统变化反射率R₀和自转参数a；分析QNM频率分布、实部与虚部演化；推导Teukolsky振幅与能量反射率关系；比较不同边界条件（渐近振幅 vs. 有限半径Robin）对频谱的影响。

Result: 1) QNMs随反射率升高出现趋近实轴的等间距回声分支；2) 随自转增强，部分模态趋近实轴或保持有限阻尼，另一些实部追踪mΩ(r₊)；3) 发现超辐射与反射壁能量注入两种不稳定性机制；4) 能量反射率可在mΩ(r₊)发散，即使Teukolsky振幅反射率很小；5) 渐近振幅边界条件导致极点诱导共振，而Sasaki-Nakamura变量的有限半径Robin条件可消除之。

Conclusion: 内边界的具体数学表述（如反射率大小、是否基于渐近振幅、是否采用有限半径Robin条件）深刻决定半开放Kerr-de Sitter黑洞系统的准正规模谱形态与稳定性，为黑洞模拟及量子引力模型中的边界物理提供关键理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate the gravitational quasinormal mode spectrum of Kerr--de Sitter black holes with a partially reflective inner boundary near the event horizon, by reducing the angular and radial Teukolsky equations to Heun equations. We vary different parameters and study the $s=-2$, $\ell=m=2$ modes numerically. As the reflectivity $\mathcal{R}_0$ increases, QNMs develop echo branches approaching the real axis with approximately regular spacing, while others approach $\operatorname{Re}ω=mΩ(r_+)$. As the rotation $a$ increases, some QNMs approach the real axis or retain finite damping, while others exhibit approximately horizontal shifts with real parts tracking $mΩ(r_+)$. Two mechanisms driving the instabilities are identified in complementary regimes, namely superradiant amplification and energy injection from the reflective wall. We derive the ratio between Teukolsky amplitude and energy reflectivities, finding that the latter can diverge at $mΩ(r_+)$ even when the former is small, which accounts for an active wall in this regime. We also find that formulating the reflective boundary condition in terms of asymptotic amplitudes gives rise to resonances near each pole of the ingoing connection coefficient, extending to arbitrarily large positive imaginary parts. An exact finite radius Robin condition imposed on the Sasaki--Nakamura variable removes these pole-born frequencies. These results show how the inner boundary prescription controls the spectrum structure and stability of semi-open Kerr--de Sitter systems.

</details>


### [105] [Boson Stars with Ayón--Beato--García nonlinear electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20634)
*Long-Xing Huang,Yong-Qiang Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了爱因斯坦引力与Ayón--Beato--García非线性电动力学及质量复标量场耦合下的球对称玻色子星解，发现仅当电荷低于临界值时解存在；小电荷下出现引力蓝移和局部负径向质量；大电荷下当标量场频率趋于零时出现‘冻结’玻色子星，其度规在某特殊半径处趋近于极值黑洞，并显著影响类时测地线行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非线性电动力学与引力、标量场耦合下新型致密天体解的存在性与特性，特别是电荷与标量场频率对结构和时空几何的影响。

Method: 求解球对称情形下爱因斯坦-非线性电动力学-复标量场耦合方程组，分析不同电荷q和标量场频率ω参数下的静态解性质，包括质量函数、度规行为、ADM质量及类时测地线运动。

Result: 1) 解仅存在于电荷q小于某临界值的范围内；2) 小q时出现引力蓝移和局部负径向质量（ADM质量仍正）；3) 大q且ω→0时出现‘冻结’玻色子星，度规在r_ch附近极度压缩，类黑洞；4) 该区域类时轨迹强烈偏折，穿越时间显著增长。

Conclusion: Ayón--Beato--García非线性电动力学可支持新型玻色子星构型，其物理行为随电荷与频率演化，展现出从常规致密星到类极值黑洞过渡的丰富现象，为理解强场引力与非线性场相互作用提供了新视角。

Abstract: In this paper, we study spherically symmetric boson star solutions in Einstein gravity minimally coupled to Ayón--Beato--García nonlinear electrodynamics and a massive complex scalar field. We find that these solutions exist only when the electric charge $q$ is below a critical value. Within this range, solutions with relatively small charges can exhibit a gravitational blueshift, and the radial mass function becomes negative in some regions while the ADM mass remains positive. For larger charges, frozen boson-star solutions emerge as the scalar-field frequency $ω$ approaches zero. In these configurations, the metric function $1/g_{rr}$ and $-g_{tt}$ become very small near a special position $r_{\mathrm{ch}}$, and the spacetime resembles an extremal black hole from the perspective of an observer at infinity. Moreover, timelike trajectories in these frozen boson stars are strongly deflected near $r_{\mathrm{ch}}$, and particles require a longer coordinate time to traverse the same radial distance than they do in ordinary boson stars.

</details>


### [106] [Simultaneous tests of post-Newtonian inspiral using GW250114 and GW250207](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20738)
*Saurabh Kumar,Parthapratim Mahapatra,Ish Mohan Gupta,M. Saleem,B. S. Sathyaprakash,K. G. Arun*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过同时改变3.5PN阶的八个相位系数，对双黑洞并合过程进行广义相对论后牛顿（PN）理论的高维检验，利用GW250114和GW250207两个强信号事件，结合改进的IMRPhenomXPHM_SpinTaylor波形模型与参数化相位偏差，开展贝叶斯推断，并通过主成分分析识别数据最强约束的相位组合，结果支持广义相对论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验广义相对论在强引力场下的有效性，尤其是双黑洞并合过程中后牛顿相位演化的准确性，需突破以往低维或单参数检验的局限，开展更一般、更高维的PN检验。

Method: 采用IMRPhenomXPHM_SpinTaylor波形模型并引入参数化PN相位偏差，对GW250114和GW250207两个高信噪比事件进行贝叶斯参数估计；随后在八维PN相位变形空间中实施主成分分析，提取数据最敏感的线性组合。

Result: 数据显著约束了变形空间中的前三个主成分，且所有三个主成分均与广义相对论预测一致；表明强引力波事件可实现鲁棒的高维PN检验，并直接揭示可观测的PN变形子空间。

Conclusion: 当前最一般的八参数PN并合检验结果与广义相对论高度一致，证实了利用明亮引力波事件开展高维理论检验的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: We perform the one of the most general tests of post-Newtonian (PN) inspiral in binary black holes to date by allowing all eight phase coefficients up to 3.5PN order to vary simultaneously from their general-relativistic values. Using two of the loudest binary black hole events observed to date, GW250114 and GW250207, we carry out Bayesian inference with the IMRPhenomXPHM_SpinTaylor waveform model augmented by parametrized deviations in the post-Newtonian phasing. We then identify the combinations of phase coefficients most strongly constrained by the data through a principal component analysis of the eight-dimensional deformation space. We find that the data constrain the three leading principal components beyond their priors, and that all three are consistent with the predictions of general relativity. These results demonstrate that exceptionally loud gravitational-wave events enable robust, high-dimensional tests of inspiral dynamics and provide direct access to the measurable subspace of post-Newtonian deformations.

</details>


### [107] [Cartan geometry and the Hamiltonian analysis of gravitational actions in terms of forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20850)
*J. Fernando Barbero G.,Juan Margalef-Bentabol,Valle Varo,Eduardo J. S. Villaseñor*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文综述了以微分形式表示的广义相对论相关作用量的哈密顿表述，并利用嘉当几何简化哈密顿分析，获得统一而清晰的几何理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为系统理解广义相对论相关作用量的哈密顿结构，需借助更有力的几何工具。

Method: 采用嘉当几何方法，对以微分形式表达的若干广义相对论相关作用量进行哈密顿分析。

Result: 成功简化了哈密顿分析过程，获得了统一、连贯且具有清晰几何意义的哈密顿表述框架。

Conclusion: 嘉当几何是处理此类问题的有效工具，能显著提升广义相对论作用量哈密顿分析的几何直观性与系统性。

Abstract: The purpose of the paper is to review the Hamiltonian formulation for several actions related to general relativity that can be written in terms of differential forms. As we will show the powerful ideas of Cartan geometry can be used to simplify the Hamiltonian analysis and arrive at a coherent and comprehensive geometrical understanding of it.

</details>


### [108] [Gravitational collapse in Lorentz-violating gravity and the spin-0 mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21034)
*Emma Albertini,Massimo Vaglio,Enrico Barausse*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过球对称标量场坍缩的全非线性数值模拟，验证了Einstein-Aether理论中自旋-0模式是真实传播的能量携带波，其行为符合线性理论预测；同时发现强耦合下可能形成裸奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期Taherasghari和Will的后牛顿分析质疑Einstein-Aether理论中自旋-0模式是否为真实传播、携能的波动，因其辐射反作用计算中未体现该模式传播速度；本文旨在通过非线性动力学直接检验其物理本质。

Method: 采用球对称条件下的全非线性数值相对论模拟，研究标量场在Einstein-Aether引力理论中的坍缩过程，聚焦于仅由自旋-0模主导的辐射行为，并分析能量输运、波速、残余aether构型及黑洞/白洞形成条件。

Result: 确认自旋-0模以线性理论预言速度传播，振幅按1/r衰减，携带正能至无穷远；静态aether构型具负能量，致视界质量大于ADM质量；大耦合下出现持续陡化的坍缩壳层，无陷俘面遮蔽，暗示裸奇点可能形成。

Conclusion: 自旋-0模确为真实、动力学的携能引力自由度；Einstein-Aether理论在强场下可呈现新奇时空结构（如裸奇点），挑战经典黑洞唯一性图景。

Abstract: Einstein-Aether theory is a theory of gravity with a preferred frame, in which Lorentz symmetry is broken by a dynamical unit timelike vector field. The theory is closely related to Horava gravity, and in spherically symmetric collapse the two produce the same dynamics. Both propagate a scalar (spin-0) mode besides the tensor mode and, in Einstein-Aether theory, the vector mode. The physical nature of the spin-0 mode has recently been called into question following the post-Newtonian analysis of Taherasghari and Will, whose post-Newtonian calculation of radiation reaction in compact binaries finds no trace of its speed in the energy loss, suggesting that the mode may not be a dynamical, energy-carrying wave. We test this with fully nonlinear evolutions of scalar-field collapse in spherical symmetry, where the spin-0 mode is the only radiative degree of freedom. The collapse emits an outgoing spin-0 wave that falls off as the inverse of the radius, travels at the speed predicted by the linear theory, and carries positive energy to infinity. The static aether configuration left around the black hole has negative energy instead, so that the horizon mass exceeds the ADM mass. For large couplings, collapse does not produce a black hole: we recover the transient white holes found by Akhoury, Garfinkle and Gupta, but at higher resolution the collapsing shell keeps steepening, not hidden behind any trapped surface, which may signal a naked singularity.

</details>


### [109] [Maximum entropy reconstruction of the black hole greybody factors from its Korteweg-de Vries integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21043)
*Noah Alberca,Michele Lenzi,Carlos F. Sopuerta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最大熵原理的新方法，用于求解由KdV层级对称性导出的黑洞灰体因子矩问题，并在Pöschl-Teller势和Schwarzschild黑洞Regge-Wheeler势上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以精确计算无解析解的黑洞灰体因子；而KdV积分虽提供约束，但需有效矩问题求解策略。

Method: 采用最大熵原理重构满足有限个KdV积分约束、且天然保证正性的传输概率分布；在不同截断阶数下实现渐进逼近。

Result: 该方法在可解的Pöschl-Teller势上验证准确，在Schwarzschild黑洞Regge-Wheeler势下结果与四阶WKB近似高度一致。

Conclusion: 最大熵法是求解黑洞灰体因子矩问题的有效新工具，仅需少量KdV积分即可高精度重建灰体因子。

Abstract: The dynamics of perturbed non-rotating black holes (BHs) can be described by master wave equations with a potential barrier. Recent work has shown that these equations possess an infinite number of symmetries generated by the Korteweg-de Vries (KdV) hierarchy, leading to conserved quantities (KdV integrals) that fully determine the BH greybody factors via a moment problem. In this paper, we introduce and validate a new method to solve this moment problem using the maximum entropy principle. The method reconstructs, at each order of truncation, the least-biased transmission probability compatible with a finite set of KdV integrals, guaranteeing positivity by construction. We first validate the technique against the exactly solvable Pöschl-Teller potential. We then apply it to the Regge-Wheeler potential governing axial perturbations of a Schwarzschild BH, where no exact solution is available; the resulting reconstruction agrees closely with the fourth-order Wentzel-Kramers-Brillouin (WKB) approximation across several multipoles. This is the first application of the maximum entropy method to the reconstruction of BH greybody factors, demonstrating that a modest number of KdV integrals is sufficient to recover them accurately.

</details>


### [110] [On Chen's Extended Action for $κ(R,T)$ Gravity: Gravity in a Polarizable Medium, Material Folds and a No-Bounce Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21093)
*Ginés R. Pérez Teruel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过Chen的局部伴随作用从局部变分原理推导出κ(R,T)引力理论的场方程，并在平直FLRW宇宙中利用Dirac约化分析其哈密顿结构；发现耦合参数κ通过第二类物质约束进入系统，物理应力张量通常不守恒但‘修饰后的粒子数’守恒；提出极化介质物理解释，将κ=0、斥力相和物质映射折叠分别对应完全屏蔽、过屏蔽与饱和；线性耦合尘埃模型重现圈量子宇宙学（LQC）的有效Friedmann方程，但守恒律不同；引入有效压强−ρ²/ρ_c可恢复均匀LQC动力学，且折叠密度恰为LQC变形代数方法中的符号改变阈值；最后，约化尘埃约束支持Koopman–von Neumann输运量子化，伴随场作为响应变量，暗示开放系统诠释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为κ(R,T)引力理论建立坚实的变分基础与物理诠释，揭示其在宇宙学背景下的动力学行为、守恒性质及与圈量子宇宙学的深层联系，特别是探索其是否支持宇宙反弹以及如何理解非守恒应力与有效介质类比。

Method: 采用Chen的局部伴随作用构建变分原理；在平直FLRW度规下实施Dirac约束约化以获得哈密顿形式；对物质约束进行分类（第一类/第二类），分析其对哈密顿量的影响；引入有效源分解并类比极化介质；研究线性κ(T)耦合与尘埃情形下的精确解与奇点结构；对比分析与圈量子宇宙学（LQC）的有效动力学及守恒律差异；应用Koopman–von Neumann方法对约化约束进行输运式量子化。

Result: 1) κ(R,T)理论场方程可由局部变分原理导出；2) 在正则分支上，哈密顿量不显含κ(T)，κ仅通过第二类物质约束起作用；3) 物理应力张量不守恒，但存在守恒的‘修饰粒子数’；4) 提出极化介质解释：κ=0为完全屏蔽，斥力相为过屏蔽，折叠对应饱和；5) 线性尘埃模型在低密度膨胀支上存在过去端点（体积最小、密度达ρ_c/2），Dirac一致性禁止光滑穿越该折叠；6) 平直FLRW宇宙中，κ>0、ρ>0、w>−1时严格排除光滑反弹；7) 该模型重现LQC有效Friedmann方程，但需添加有效压强−ρ²/ρ_c才能匹配其均匀动力学；8) 折叠密度等于LQC变形代数方法中的时空符号改变阈值；9) 约化尘埃约束支持KvN输运量子化，伴随场表现为响应变量。

Conclusion: κ(R,T)引力在FLRW背景下展现出丰富而独特的动力学结构：其哈密顿实现依赖于非平凡约束体系，物理可观测量需重新定义（如修饰粒子数），有效源可统一诠释为极化介质响应；虽与LQC共享部分有效宇宙学行为，但微观机制与守恒结构迥异；折叠奇点构成不可逾越的边界，排除了常规反弹可能，暗示该理论更适合作为描述有效开放量子引力系统的框架。

Abstract: Chen's local adjoint action reproduces the field equations of $κ(R,T)$ from a local variational principle, and we show that it does physical work. In flat FLRW, Dirac reduction gives a Hamiltonian independent of $κ(T)$ on regular branches, where the coupling enters through a second-class matter constraint. The physical stress tensor is not generally conserved, but a dressed particle number is. An exact decomposition of the effective source motivates a polarizable-medium interpretation: $κ=0$ corresponds to total screening, the repulsive phase to overscreening, and a fold of the matter map to saturation. For linear coupling and dust, the expanding low-density branch has a past endpoint at the fold, where the volume is minimal and the density reaches $ρ_c/2$ at finite curvature. Dirac consistency rules out smooth Hamiltonian continuation through the fold. More generally, a flat-FLRW bounce is excluded for $κ>0$, positive density and $w>-1$. The linear dust model reproduces the effective Friedmann equation of loop quantum cosmology (LQC), but the two models obey different conservation laws. An effective pressure $-ρ^2/ρ_c$ restores the homogeneous LQC dynamics, and the fold density coincides with the signature-change threshold in the deformed-algebra approach to LQC. The reduced dust constraint admits an exactly solvable Koopman--von Neumann transport quantization and the adjoint field acts as a response variable, motivating an open-system interpretation.

</details>


### [111] [Critical curves and throat degeneracy in black bounces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21355)
*Francisco S. N. Lobo,Diogo Mesquita,Luís F. Dias da Silva*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了静态球对称时空在径向形变下光子球轨道动力学的变化规律，指出即使临界曲线相同，零测地线的动力学行为也可能不同；关键发现包括临界冲击参数和轨道频率保持不变，而Lyapunov指数和强偏转系数受径向Jacobian影响，并给出了光学简并阶数与临界行为的普适标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解径向形变如何影响光子球的轨道稳定性与强引力透镜效应，区分哪些零测地线信息由临界曲线决定、哪些由局部几何决定。

Method: 基于静态球对称时空的径向（areal-radius）形变模型，分析光学潜力极值结构、Lyapunov指数、强偏转角渐近行为，结合Simpson–Visser时空族进行具体验证。

Result: 1) 光子球临界冲击参数与轨道频率在形变下保持不变；2) Lyapunov指数乘以径向Jacobian，强偏转系数取其倒数；3) 在反射对称喉部，稳定性由原光学势导数与喉部曲率因子化决定；4) 一般情况下，m阶势极值与q阶半径极小值组合导致p=mq阶光学简并，给出普适临界趋近与偏转角标度律；5) Simpson–Visser族中a<3M时临界冲击参数保持Schwarzschild形式，a=3M处发生四阶合并，分隔稳定/不稳定喉分支。

Conclusion: 径向形变下，零测地线的关键全局量（如b_c, Ω）由原始临界曲线决定，而局部动力学稳定性（如λ, ar{a}）则敏感依赖于形变引入的局部几何特征；该工作建立了临界曲线与局部几何对光线动力学分工的清晰图景。

Abstract: Identical critical curves need not imply identical null-orbit dynamics. For a class of static spherical areal-radius deformations, we show that a surviving original photon sphere retains its critical impact parameter and orbital frequency, while its coordinate-time Lyapunov exponent is multiplied by the radial Jacobian. The logarithmic strong-deflection coefficient acquires the inverse factor. At a reflection-symmetric throat, stability factorizes into a derivative of the original optical potential and the throat curvature. More generally, an order-$m$ extremum of the original potential composed with an order-$q$ areal-radius minimum produces an optical degeneracy of order $p=mq$, with critical approach $|x|\propto|t|^{-2/(p-2)}$ and reflected strong deflection $Δφ\propto(b/b_{\rm c}-1)^{-(p-2)/(2p)}$ for $p>2$. For the Simpson--Visser family with mass $M$ and bounce parameter $a$, the critical impact parameter remains Schwarzschild-like for $a<3M$, although the instability varies with $a$. The merger at $a=3M$ is quartic, separating stable and unstable throat branches. These results isolate the null-orbit information preserved by a radial deformation from that encoded in its local geometry.

</details>


### [112] [The Stability of the Higgs vacuum inside a Schwarzschild Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21356)
*Paul G. Abel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了希格斯场在史瓦西黑洞内部的行为，考虑了希格斯场与克雷特施曼标量（Kretschmann scalar）之间的普朗克压制耦合。结果表明，在黑洞内部，希格斯场保持在对称破缺相，直到曲率达到普朗克尺度才显著偏离真空值，且该行为与黑洞质量无关；一环量子修正仅使希格斯凝聚略微减弱（约10%），电弱对称性在整个黑洞内部仍保持破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子场（特别是希格斯场）在强引力、高曲率时空（如黑洞内部）中的行为，检验标准模型与广义相对论在极端条件下的相容性，并澄清静态近似下可能产生的误导结论。

Method: 在史瓦西黑洞内部（径向坐标类时区域）求解含曲率耦合项的希格斯场运动方程；利用渐近分析将奇点附近的方程简化为贝塞尔方程；并引入希格斯真空涨落的一圈修正，精确求解带源项的贝塞尔方程。

Result: 希格斯场在黑洞内部随固有时演化，其解为零阶贝塞尔函数H = v J₀(x)；即使在大质量黑洞中，场也保持破缺相，仅当曲率达普朗克量级时才明显偏离真空期望值；一圈修正导致凝聚最多减弱约10%，电弱对称性在整个黑洞内部有效保持破缺。

Conclusion: 在有效场论适用范围内，电弱对称性在史瓦西黑洞内部始终处于破缺态，不因黑洞质量或经典几何演化而恢复；这支持了标准模型希格斯机制在强引力背景下的稳健性。

Abstract: I investigate the behaviour of the Higgs field inside a Schwarzschild black hole, including a Planck-suppressed coupling between the Higgs field and the Kretschmann scalar, the leading curvature invariant in a Ricci-flat geometry. Because the radial coordinate is timelike in the interior, the Higgs condensate evolves in time rather than tracking the minimum of its potential. Near the singularity the interior field equation reduces to Bessel's equation, with solution $H=vJ_0(x)$, where $x\propto\sqrtξ\,t_p/τ$ and $τ$ is the proper time to the singularity. Contrary to static analyses, the field remains frozen in its broken phase while the potential changes shape, departing from its vacuum value only when the curvature becomes Planckian, independently of the black-hole mass. Including the one-loop correction from Higgs vacuum fluctuations yields the same Bessel equation with a source term, which I solve exactly; the condensate is reduced by at most about ten per cent. The electroweak symmetry therefore remains broken throughout the interior within the regime of validity of effective field theory.

</details>


### [113] [Geometry of squeezed states. The phase-space metric, relative vacua and cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21426)
*Alejandro Corichi,Jerónimo Cortez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了量子理论中几何结构的作用，特别是相空间上的度量g如何作为核心对象，描述线性可观测量的涨落、压缩态的性质以及二次演化对度量的影响，并将该框架应用于场论和暴胀宇宙学中的真空选择问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态空间具有辛结构和度量结构，而经典相空间在选定真空后也自然获得一个兼容度量g；本文旨在以该相空间度量g为基本出发点，统一理解量子涨落、压缩态、真空比较及幺正可实现性等概念。

Method: 通过将线性系统量子化等价于选取相空间上与辛结构兼容的复结构（即兼容度量g），建立g与量子度量、哈密顿矢量场范数、相干态、压缩态之间的精确对应；分析二次哈密顿演化对g的作用，并推广至量子场论，用度量距离刻画真空间的幺正等价性。

Result: 证明线性可观测量的涨落等于其哈密顿矢量场关于g的范数；沿相干态，g/2ℏ即为量子度量；压缩真空对应另一度量，压缩参数r_i由两度量比L=g₀⁻¹g的本征值e^{±2r_i}决定；二次演化中仅相对于参考度量自伴的部分改变r_i；对de Sitter空间无质量场，Bunch-Davies真空相对于不同参考真空的压缩率不同，且仅当度量距离有限时幺正可实现。

Conclusion: 相空间度量g是理解量子涨落、压缩、真空选择与幺正性的几何基础；该几何视角统一了量子力学与量子场论中的诸多现象，并为暴胀宇宙学中真空比较提供了清晰判据。

Abstract: Geometry enters quantum theory in two places. The space of quantum states carries a symplectic form, which generates unitary evolution, and a metric, which governs uncertainties and transition probabilities. The classical phase space acquires a metric of its own when a vacuum is chosen: quantizing a linear system amounts to choosing a compatible complex structure, equivalently a compatible metric $g$. We take this second metric as the primary object and show that it plays, on phase space, the role that the quantum metric plays on the space of states: fluctuations of linear observables are $g$-norms of Hamiltonian vector fields, and along coherent states $g/2\hbar$ \emph{is} the quantum metric. Squeezed states then have a simple description. A squeezed vacuum is the vacuum of another metric, and squeezing is a property of a pair of metrics, a relative vacuum, whose invariants are the eigenvalues $e^{\pm2r_i}$ of $L=g_0^{-1}g$. We work out the harmonic oscillator in detail, including why a rotated squeezed state no longer minimizes the product of the displayed uncertainties. Quadratic evolution deforms the metric, and only the part of the generator that is self-adjoint with respect to the reference changes the $r_i$, at a rate we give exactly. For fields, a vacuum is unitarily implementable relative to a reference exactly when it lies at finite distance from it in the space of vacua. For a massless field in de Sitter space we revisit the two canonical charts compared by Grain and Vennin: relative to the reference of the standard squeezing formalism the Bunch--Davies state has $\sinh r_k=1/(2|kη|)$, and relative to the vacuum that a radiation era beginning at time $η$ would select it has $\sinh r_k=1/(2k^2η^2)$. The second comparison squeezes twice as fast and is unitarily implementable; the first is not.

</details>


### [114] [Pulsar timing response and observational constraints on continuous-spin gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21512)
*Jing-Hong Han,Zhi-Chao Zhao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续自旋粒子（CSP）在引力波探测中的表现，特别是利用纳秒脉冲星计时阵列（NANOGrav）数据对连续自旋尺度ρ_g设定了新的上限，并发现其对引力波谱指数γ_GW的拟合更接近双黑洞并合的理论预期值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续自旋粒子是庞加莱群最一般的无质量整数螺旋度不可约表示，具有由非零Pauli-Lubanski不变量决定的连续自旋尺度ρ_g；其效应在纳赫兹频段引力波中显著，脉冲星计时阵列对此天然敏感。

Method: 从线性化连续自旋引力的世界线规范不变耦合出发，推导出单向脉冲星计时红移响应及各整数螺旋度下各向同性随机背景的重叠减小函数，并在太阳系质心框架下对NANOGrav 15年数据进行联合拟合（含脉冲星红噪声）。

Result: 在均匀与对数均匀先验下，分别获得ρ_g的95%上限为7.03×10⁻²³ eV和4.79×10⁻²³ eV；在均匀先验下得到引力波谱指数γ_GW = 4.89⁺⁰·⁸⁵₋₀·₉₆，更接近SMBHB理论值13/3。

Conclusion: 连续自旋引力模型可被当前脉冲星计时数据有效约束，且其对γ_GW的改善暗示该新物理可能缓解标准广义相对论在解释NANOGrav信号时的张力。

Abstract: Continuous-spin particles form the most general massless integer-helicity irreducible representations of the Poincaré group, characterized by a dimensionful continuous-spin scale $ρ_g$ set by a nonzero Pauli--Lubanski invariant. A single continuous-spin representation contains an infinite tower of integer helicities $h\in\mathbb Z$ that mix under Lorentz boosts. Since continuous-spin effects are controlled by $α\equivρ_g/(\hbarω)$ at gravitational-wave angular frequency $ω$, nanohertz pulsar timing arrays are naturally sensitive to ultralow values of $ρ_g$. Starting from the gauge-invariant worldline coupling of linearized continuous-spin gravity, we derive the one-way pulsar timing redshift response and the overlap reduction function of an isotropic stochastic background for arbitrary integer helicity. We show that while photon propagation is modulated across all helicities, only the vector modes ($h=\pm1$) perturb the four-velocity of a static observer, and that the tensor modes ($h=\pm2$) smoothly recover the Hellings--Downs correlation of general relativity as $ρ_g/(\hbarω)\to 0$. Modeling an unpolarized $h=\pm2$ background in the solar-system barycentric frame and jointly fitting the NANOGrav 15-year data with pulsar red noise, we obtain the $95\%$ upper limits $ρ^{U}_g<7.03\times10^{-23}\,\mathrm{eV}$ and $ρ^{\log {U}}_g<4.79\times10^{-23}\,\mathrm{eV}$ under uniform and log-uniform priors, respectively. Under the uniform prior, we obtain $γ_{\mathrm{GW}} = 4.89^{+0.85}_{-0.96}$, which brings the spectral index closer to the canonical value $γ_{\rm GW}=13/3$ expected for circular SMBHBs than standard General Relativity.

</details>


### [115] [On the Asymptotic Expansion of Massive Fields in Spherically Symmetric Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21557)
*Robin Fynn Diedrichs,Iris van Gemeren*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文挑战了大质量实标量场在中心物体作用下通常呈Yukawa衰减的传统假设，指出该假设在一般情况下不成立；同时揭示了远距离提取的标量场系数既不具备坐标不变性，也不等同于点粒子作用量中的标量荷，并通过匹配渐近展开和数值方法解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上假设大质量实标量场在中心物体作用下呈Yukawa衰减，且远距离系数可表征标量荷，但该假设缺乏严格验证，且其坐标不变性和物理含义存疑。

Method: 采用匹配渐近展开法进行微扰分析，并结合数值方法研究非微扰结构。

Result: 证明大质量实标量场（即使最小耦合）通常不满足Yukawa衰减；远距离提取的场系数既非坐标不变，也不等于点粒子作用量定义的标量荷。

Conclusion: 需谨慎诠释远距离标量场行为及其与源荷的关系；标量荷的坐标不变定义应基于点粒子作用量，而非远场拟合系数。

Abstract: The behavior of massive real scalar fields, when sourced by a central object, is usually assumed to follow a Yukawa falloff towards radial infinity. In this paper, we show that this is generically not the case, even for minimally coupled fields. Further, while the point-particle action provides a coordinate-invariant definition of the central object's scalar charge, we show that the coefficient one usually extracts at asymptotically large radii in the case of massive scalar fields is neither coordinate invariant nor identical to the scalar charge appearing in the point-particle action. We resolve this issue perturbatively by constructing matched asymptotic expansions and investigate the non-perturbative structure using numerical methods.

</details>


### [116] [Separation of gravitational perturbations of Myers-Perry black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21591)
*Jun-Kun Zhao,Hong-Da Lyu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了五维渐近平坦 Myers-Perry 黑洞（具有两个不等自旋）的线性化引力扰动，利用 Killing-Yano 对称性构造了五个耦合的规范不变极化模式的分离解，并建立了完备的角向谐函数系与局域度规重构方法，为研究双自旋五维旋转黑洞的准正规模和引力稳定性提供了理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究五维渐近平坦、具有两个不等自旋的 Myers-Perry 黑洞的引力扰动，以理解其稳定性与动力学行为，填补多自旋高维黑洞微扰理论的空白。

Method: 基于 Killing-Yano 对称性构造一阶角向算子，结合对易的二阶算子确定所有五个引力极化模式的角向结构；利用 Carter 互换关系关联角向与径向方程；证明实频率下角向谐函数的完备性，并实现局域度规重构。

Result: 得到了任意方位角模数下五个规范不变极化模式的分离解；确立了统一的径向振幅描述五个潮汐分量；证明了角向谐函数在实频率下的完备性；实现了物理扰动的局域度规重建；在单自旋极限下复现 Santos 的结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为五维双自旋旋转黑洞的准正规模计算与引力稳定性分析提供了系统、完备且可计算的微扰理论框架。

Abstract: We study linearized gravitational perturbations of five-dimensional asymptotically flat Myers--Perry black holes with two unequal spins. We construct separated solutions for the five coupled gauge-invariant polarizations for arbitrary azimuthal mode numbers. The key observation is that the Killing--Yano symmetry induces a first-order angular operator that, together with a commuting second-order operator, determines the angular profiles of all five gravitational polarizations. The Carter interchange relates the scalar angular and radial equations, and the five tidal components of each separated solution share a single radial amplitude. We prove completeness of the angular harmonics at real frequencies and show that the resulting physical perturbations admit local metric reconstruction. In the singly spinning limit, we recover Santos's angular operators and radial equation for perturbations with $m_ψ=0$ and even parity under $ψ\to-ψ$. These results provide a framework for studying quasinormal modes and gravitational stability of five-dimensional rotating black holes with two independent spins.

</details>


### [117] [A separation criterion for chiral systems and its application to Plebański--Demiański perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21603)
*Hideo Furugori,Daisuke Yoshida*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种构造性判据，用于判断一类耦合复二重态的一阶手性系统能否局域分离变量，并将其应用于旋转带电Plebański-Demiański黑洞背景下的爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦辐射扰动，导出了分离的径向与角向方程，并分析了角向算子的谱性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为处理复杂弯曲时空（如含加速、NUT荷、宇宙常数的Plebański-Demiański度规）中耦合场扰动的可分离性问题，需超越传统Killing矢量或对称性方法，建立更普适、可构造的分离判据。

Method: 结合源项的Gram分解（要求其具有可分离的坐标依赖结构）与微分输运检验；在模无关、行列式为1的复矩阵变换类下，对可恢复且非退化的源族给出充要条件；进一步分析源项唯一确定厄米系数矩阵的条件。

Result: 建立了局部变量分离的构造性充要判据；为Einstein-Maxwell辐射扰动构造了显式代数变换，导出径向与角向分离方程；证明在正则性假设下，角向自伴实现具有离散谱和完备标准正交特征基；给出Bonnor与Misner拓扑识别下的角向端点指标。

Conclusion: 该判据统一处理了广义旋量耦合系统的可分离性问题，不仅适用于高对称背景，也拓展至缺乏全局对称性的加速、NUT、Λ等物理情形，为强引力场中波传播与稳定性分析提供了严格数学工具。

Abstract: We give a constructive criterion for local separation of first-order chiral systems coupling two complex doublets. It combines a Gram decomposition of the coupling sources with separate coordinate dependence and a differential transport test. For recoverable, nondegenerate source families, the criterion is necessary and sufficient within the specified class of mode-independent complex matrix transformations with unit determinant. For a fixed differential operator and admissible Gram decomposition, we identify conditions under which the sources uniquely determine the Hermitian coefficient matrix required for separation. As an application, we construct an explicit algebraic transformation and derive separated radial and angular equations for coupled radiative Einstein--Maxwell perturbations of rotating, charged Plebański--Demiański backgrounds with acceleration, NUT charge, and a cosmological constant, on specified local patches. Its Gram decomposition is unique under additional generic assumptions. We also derive angular endpoint indices for Bonnor and Misner identifications with conical deficits or excesses. Under the stated regularity assumptions, each specified self-adjoint angular realization at fixed real mode parameters has a discrete spectrum and a complete orthonormal eigenbasis.

</details>


### [118] [Self-interacting $p$-forms and the shear-free anisotropic cosmological model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07288)
*Nicolas Hernandez,Ben D. Normann,Yeinzon Rodriguez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了扩展的弗里德曼-勒梅特-罗伯逊-沃克（eFLRW）宇宙学模型，重点研究非阿贝尔p-形式场（p=0,…,4）在各向异性空间曲率与各向异性应力平衡中的作用，发现仅3-形式场在特定势下无需微调即可自然实现该平衡，并兼具暗能量与类曲率暗物质行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统宇宙学原理要求各向同性膨胀且无涡度，但观测可能并不要求如此强的条件；本文旨在探索更弱条件（即仅需各向同性膨胀、零剪切、非零各向异性空间曲率）下，何种场能通过与各向异性应力的动态平衡实现eFLRW结构。

Method: 基于eFLRW度规框架，将分析从自由微分p-形式推广至含自相互作用的非阿贝尔p-形式场（p=0–4），结合各向异性应力按a^{-2}衰减的要求与规范场连续性方程，系统考察各类势函数对曲率-应力平衡的影响，并特别分析Bianchi A类中带/不带荷的3-形式场。

Result: 一般非阿贝尔p-形式需对势函数进行精细微调才能平衡各向异性曲率；唯独3-形式场H^a在势V(H²)=A+B H²下（A、B为常数），无论是否带荷（后者需适配联络），均可自然实现零剪切、各向同性膨胀的eFLRW解，且无需任何参数微调。

Conclusion: 3-形式场配二次势是目前唯一无需微调即可自洽支撑eFLRW宇宙学的非阿贝尔场候选者，兼具解释宇宙常数与类曲率暗物质的双重潜力，为弱化宇宙学原理提供了新路径。

Abstract: The cosmological principle may require more than observations demand: what is needed is isotropic expansion without vorticity. Extended Friedmann-Lemaitre-Robertson-Walker (eFLRW) cosmologies realise this with a non-vanishing anisotropic spatial curvature that is balanced by an anisotropic stress while the shear vanishes. Thorsrud and others investigated such scenarios for free differential $p$-forms. Here we extend the analysis to non-Abelian $p$-forms ($p=0,\dots,4$) with self interactions. The balance requires the anisotropic stress to decay as $a^{-2}$, $a$ being the cosmological scale factor, which we combine with the gauge-field continuity equation. The general results indicate that, in the presence of a potential, fine-tuning of the same is needed in order to balance the curvature. Notably, this is not the case for a $3$-form field $\underline{H}^a$ with a potential term $V(\underline{H}^2)=A+B\,\underline{H}^2$, where $A$ and $B$ are constants: in Bianchi class A, an uncharged 3-form balances the curvature with this potential without any tuning, and a charged one does so for a suitably chosen connection. This is the only candidate we have found where the potential does not require tuning. It is also an interesting candidate as it can be interpreted as a cosmological constant in combination with a dark-matter sector mimicking curvature.

</details>

*A. Coerver,M. Rahimi,D. Anbajagane,E. Anderes,A. J. Anderson,B. Ansarinejad,M. Archipley,L. Balkenhol,D. R. Barron,P. S. Barry,K. Benabed,A. N. Bender,B. A. Benson,F. Bianchini,L. E. Bleem,S. Bocquet,F. R. Bouchet,E. Camphuis,M. G. Campitiello,J. E. Carlstrom,J. Carron,C. L. Chang,P. M. Chichura,A. Chokshi,T. -L. Chou,T. M. Crawford,C. Daley,T. de Haan,K. R. Dibert,M. A. Dobbs,M. Millea,J. Montgomery,Y. Nakato,T. Natoli,A. Ouellette,Z. Pan,P. Paschos,K. A. Phadke,K. Prabhu,W. Quan,A. Rahlin,C. L. Reichardt,M. Rouble,J. E. Ruhl,A. C. Silva Oliveira,A. Simpson,J. A. Sobrin,A. A. Stark,J. Stephen,C. Tandoi,Q. Taylor,C. Trendafilova,J. D. Vieira,A. G. Vieregg,A. Vitrier,Y. Wan,N. Whitehorn,W. L. K. Wu,M. R. Young,J. A. Zebrowski*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用SPT-3G两年数据搜寻各向异性宇宙双折射，未发现显著信号，给出目前最严格的尺度不变双折射功率谱振幅上限（A_CB < 0.027），并据此约束轴子耦合常数和原初磁场强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测理论预言的真空偏振依赖性折射率在宇宙尺度上的各向异性变化（即各向异性宇宙双折射），该效应会因CMB光子传播过程中偏振角旋转而引起E/B模式混合。

Method: 基于SPT-3G两年观测数据，测量旋转角α的功率谱，在20 < L < 2048范围内分六个角多极 bins 进行分析，并对尺度不变和白噪声型尺度相关模型设定上限。

Result: α功率谱与零一致，未发现宇宙双折射证据；获得尺度不变模型上限A_CB < 0.027（95%置信度）；同时给出白噪声模型上限，并导出轴子光子耦合常数g_αγ < 1.7×10⁻²/H_I 和原初磁场B_1Mpc < 9.6 nG（95%置信度）。

Conclusion: 当前SPT-3G数据未支持各向异性宇宙双折射存在，所设上限为迄今最强；未来完整巡天数据有望将限制提升数倍。

Abstract: We search for anisotropic cosmic birefringence using two years of data from the SPT-3G instrument on the South Pole Telescope. Anisotropic cosmic birefringence refers to a theorized dependence of the refractive index of the vacuum on the polarization of light that varies across the Universe in a statistically isotropic manner. Such an effect would manifest as a direction-dependent mixing of the $E$ and $B$ polarization modes of the cosmic microwave background (CMB), caused by rotation of the polarization angle as photons travel from the CMB last scattering surface to us. We present a measurement of the power spectrum of the rotation angle $α$ in six bins across the angular multipole range $20 < L < 2048$, finding no evidence for cosmic birefringence with the spectrum consistent with zero. At 95% confidence, we set a new best upper limit on the amplitude of a scale-invariant birefringence power spectrum of $A_{\rm CB}<0.027$. Additionally, we place upper limits on a scale-dependent white noise model. We translate the scale-invariant upper limit to constraints on the axion coupling constant and primordial magnetic field strength, finding 95% confidence limits of $g_{αγ} < 1.7\times 10^{-2}/H_I$ and $B_{\rm 1\,Mpc} < 9.6\,{\rm nG}$ respectively. We expect these limits to improve several-fold with the complete SPT-3G survey.

</details>


### [120] [Cosmography with gravitational waves II: Padé approximants in an extended redshift regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19006)
*Jonathan Morais,Rodrigo Gonçalves,Jailson Alcaniz*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用下一代引力波探测器（如LISA、爱因斯坦望远镜和DECIGO）结合电磁亮标准汽笛事件，采用Padé逼近法扩展宇宙学参数的高红移宇宙学分析，显著提升对哈勃常数H₀等参数的约束精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统泰勒展开的低红移宇宙学分析受限于适用红移范围，难以充分利用高红移标准汽笛事件；需发展更稳健的高红移宇宙学参数重建方法。

Method: 采用Padé逼近法替代传统泰勒展开，对宇宙学膨胀历史进行高阶参数化（H₀, q₀, j₀, s₀, l₀），并基于不同探测器（LISA、ET、DECIGO）模拟的标准汽笛事件数量与信噪比–红移分布进行参数约束分析。

Result: LISA可实现H₀的百分比级约束，爱因斯坦望远镜和DECIGO在大样本下可达亚百分比精度；q₀约束达几十个百分点，而更高阶参数j₀、s₀、l₀约束依次减弱；Padé方法显著拓展了有效红移范围和可用事件数。

Conclusion: Padé逼近法显著提升了高红移宇宙学分析能力，使下一代引力波探测器有望成为独立、高精度测量宇宙学参数（尤其是H₀）的重要工具，并支持更高阶宇宙膨胀历史重建。

Abstract: We evaluate the capability of next-generation gravitational-wave detectors, including LISA, the Einstein Telescope, and DECIGO, to probe cosmographic parameters using electromagnetically bright standard sirens. Adopting Padé approximants, we extend the cosmographic analysis to substantially higher redshifts and constrain the parameters $H_0$, $q_0$, $j_0$, $s_0$, and $l_0$. We examine the constraints primarily as a function of the number of detections and, in a complementary analysis, across correlated SNR--redshift regimes. LISA provides percent-level constraints on $H_0$, while the Einstein Telescope and DECIGO reach subpercent precision as the catalogue size increases. The parameter $q_0$ can be constrained at the tens-of-percent level, whereas the higher-order parameters $j_0$, $s_0$, and $l_0$ remain progressively less determined. Compared with the low-redshift Taylor analysis, the Padé approach extends the usable redshift range, increases the accessible event population and enables a higher-order reconstruction of the expansion history.

</details>


### [121] [Testing General Relativity by Reconstructing Dark Energy and the Growth Index with Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19117)
*Andronikos Paliathanasis*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Cosmo-PINN，利用多模块物理信息神经网络，无模型地重构暗能量状态方程w_DE(z)和物质扰动增长指数γ(z)，并结合多种宇宙学观测数据与哈密顿蒙特卡洛采样进行不确定性量化与一致性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现对暗能量状态方程w_DE(z)和增长指数γ(z)的模型无关、高精度重建，并检验广义相对论线性微扰框架下不同重建路径的一致性。

Method: 构建包含背景模块、物质增长模块和增长指数模块的Cosmo-PINN；背景模块通过能量守恒残差重建w_DE(z)；后两模块分别直接求解扰动方程或经γ(z)间接重建f(z)和fσ₈(z)；使用DESI DR2 BAO、CC、SNe Ia和RSD数据训练，并采用哈密顿蒙特卡洛（HMC）获取后验不确定性。

Result: w_DE,0后验中位数为[-0.930, -0.867]，幽灵穿越红移z≈0.41–0.53；γ₀后验中位数为0.60–0.62，表明宇宙结构增长被抑制。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了对关键动力学函数的稳健、无先验模型约束的重建，且双路径重建结果的一致性支持当前ΛCDM框架下广义相对论线性扰动理论的有效性。

Abstract: We extend the Cosmo-PINN, a physics-informed neural network for model-independent cosmological reconstruction, to reconstruct the dark energy equation of state parameter $w_{\mathrm{DE}}(z)$ and the growth index $γ(z)$ of matter perturbations. The reconstruction is performed through three modules: the background module, the matter growth module, and the growth index module. The background module learns the dark energy density $ρ_{\mathrm{DE}}(z)$ from cosmological observations, and $w_{\mathrm{DE}}(z)$ is reconstructed through the residual of the conservation equation. The reconstructed background is then passed to the other two modules, which reconstruct the observables $f(z)$ and $fσ_{8}(z)$ either directly from the linear perturbation equation of General Relativity or through an independently reconstructed growth index $γ(z)$. Comparing the two reconstructions on the same background provides a consistency test of the underlying physical framework. The network is trained on the DESI DR2 Baryon Acoustic Oscillations, Cosmic Chronometers, Type Ia Supernovae, and redshift-space distortion data. Posterior uncertainties for the reconstructed cosmological parameters and functions are obtained through Hamiltonian Monte Carlo sampling. For the different data combinations, the posterior median of the present-day dark energy equation of state lies within the range $w_{\mathrm{DE,0}}\in \lbrack -0.930,-0.867]$, while the phantom divide crossing takes place at $z\simeq 0.41$--$0.53$. The posterior median of the present-day growth index reconstructed from the data lies within $γ_{0}\simeq 0.60$--$0.62$, indicating suppression of the growth of cosmic structure.

</details>


### [122] [Initial Conditions Dictate Gas-Stellar Counter-Rotation in Galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19294)
*Lin Zhu,Hao-Ran Yu,Min Bao,Ming-Jie Sheng,Jian Qin,Shuai Lu,Min Du,Yanmei Chen,Yipeng Jing*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于宇宙原初扰动潮汐力尺度依赖性的新机制，解释星系中气体与恒星反向旋转的物理起源，并预测其分量角动量方向，为ΛCDM范式提供可观测检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释星系中气体与恒星反向旋转（gas-stellar counter-rotation）的物理起源，并超越传统晚时形成场景，将其与宇宙学原初条件联系起来。

Method: 基于冷暗物质（CDM）等级成团理论，分析拉格朗日空间中不同半径区域物质的吸积时序与对应尺度的原初潮汐扭矩关系，建立恒星（早期内区）与气体（晚时外区）角动量来源的尺度分离机制。

Result: 揭示了反向旋转源于小尺度（恒星）与大尺度（气体）原初潮汐扭矩的差异；该机制可被观测检验，并能预测各成分角动量方向。

Conclusion: 气体-恒星反向旋转是原初密度扰动尺度依赖性的直接表现，其成分分辨的角动量可作为探测原初扰动和重构宇宙初始条件的新探针。

Abstract: We present a physical origin of gas-stellar counter-rotation in galaxies. Scale-dependent partitions of tidal torques from cosmic primordial perturbations source angular momentum for distinct baryonic components. This scale dependence maps to radial hierarchy in Lagrangian space clustering: stellar components, assembled at earlier times from the inner protohalo, retain persistent memory of small-scale tidal torques, whereas gas, accreted later from outer Lagrangian regions, is governed by large-scale perturbations and can develop counter-rotation. This mechanism is observationally testable and offers a direct probe of the hierarchical cold dark matter (CDM) paradigm. Unlike conventional late-time scenarios that only explain the existence of counter-rotation, our framework further predicts its component-resolved direction, enabling component-resolved angular momentum to serve as a new probe of primordial density perturbations and improving cosmological initial condition reconstruction.

</details>


### [123] [Baryon Acoustic Oscillations from the all-sky Wen & Han cluster catalog and the 2MASS Redshift Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19308)
*Martín G. Richarte,Diego G. Lambas*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用Wen和Han（2024）全天域星系团目录中0.1<z<0.7范围内的\vNclus个具有光谱红移的星系团，首次高显著性（\vsigpostσ）探测到重子声学振荡（BAO）信号，并通过密度场重建获得精确的距离测量结果\DV/\rd=\vDVpost±\vDVpoststat(stat)±\vDVpostsys(sys)，统计误差比此前最优星系团BAO测量小\vratiostat倍；该结果与DESI DR2一致，但略低于Planck宇宙学参数预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管星系和类星体的BAO已实现约1%精度的距离测量，星系团BAO信号此前仍十分微弱；本文旨在利用最新大样本星系团巡天数据提升BAO探测显著性与距离精度。

Method: 基于Wen & Han (2024)全天域星系团样本（\vNclus个，0.1<z<0.7）计算两点相关函数，并采用密度场重建技术增强BAO峰信号；同时对2MASS红移巡天的\vTNgal个星系也进行了BAO分析作为对比。

Result: 星系团样本经重建后BAO峰显著性达\vsigpostσ；测得\DV/\rd=\vDVpost±\vDVpoststat(stat)±\vDVpostsys(sys)，在z_eff=\vzeffv处达到\vfracposttot%精度；统计误差较先前最优星系团BAO测量降低\vratiostat倍；与DESI DR2结果在\vnsdesipostσ内一致，但比Planck低\vnsplanckpostσ；2MASS星系样本重建前测得\aiso=\vTpreA^{+\vTpreAup}_{-\vTpreAlo}。

Conclusion: 本工作首次在星系团样本中实现高显著性BAO探测，验证了其作为独立宇宙学探针的潜力，为未来更大规模星系团巡天（如eROSITA、Rubin LSST）开展高精度BAO测量奠定基础。

Abstract: Baryon acoustic oscillations (BAO) in the clustering of galaxies and quasars now measure distances to precision of about one percent although galaxy clusters have so far given only marginal signals. We measure the BAO scale in the correlation function of \vNclus\ galaxy clusters with spectroscopic redshifts at $0.1<z<0.7$ from the Wen and Han (2024) all-sky catalog. After reconstruction of the density field, the BAO peak has a significance of $\vsigpostσ$ against a model without it, and gives $\DV/\rd=\vDVpost\pm\vDVpoststat\,({\rm stat})\pm\vDVpostsys\,({\rm sys})$ at an effective redshift $z_{\rm eff}=\vzeffv$, a \vfracposttot\% distance. We find that the statistical uncertainty is \vratiostat\ times smaller than that of the best earlier cluster measurement. The distance agrees with the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) Data Release 2 within $\vnsdesipostσ$ and lies $\vnsplanckpostσ$ below Planck. We also measure the BAO scale in the \vTNgal\ galaxies of the 2MASS Redshift Survey with a mean effective redshift (weighted by $n\,w^2$ with FKP weights), $z_{\rm eff}=\vTzeff$ For this all-sky galaxy sample we obtain $\aiso=\vTpreA^{+\vTpreAup}_{-\vTpreAlo}$ before reconstruction.

</details>


### [124] [Constraining neutrino masses with localized fast radio bursts: Current bounds and future prospects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19416)
*Yin-Long Cao,Ji-Guo Zhang,Guo-Hong Du,Tian-Nuo Li,Jing-Fei Zhang,Xin Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用快速射电暴（FRB）的色散量-红移关系，结合宇宙微波背景（CMB）数据，研究其对中微子质量总和∑mν的限制能力；结果表明，大样本FRB（如10000个事件）可提供比当前CMB+DESI更严格的上限（0.035 eV），但暗能量模型选择（如w0waCDM）仍主导约束强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用FRB的DM-z关系提供独立的低红移宇宙学信息，以改进中微子质量总和∑mν的限制，弥补高红移CMB与低红移巡天（如DESI）之间的信息空缺。

Method: 联合分析CMB数据与120个实测定位FRB及更大规模模拟FRB样本（1000和10000个事件），在ΛCDM+∑mν和w0waCDM+∑mν模型下进行贝叶斯参数估计，评估样本量与暗能量模型对∑mν上限的影响。

Result: 在ΛCDM+∑mν模型中，CMB+120 FRB给出∑mν < 0.21 eV；模拟1000和10000 FRB分别给出0.12 eV和0.035 eV上限，后者优于当前CMB+DESI结果；在w0waCDM+∑mν模型中，所有样本均得∑mν < 0.3–0.4 eV，显示暗能量模型为关键系统误差源。

Conclusion: 大样本、精确定位的FRB有望成为独立且有力的低红移探针，用于收紧中微子质量限制，但需同步提升DM各成分建模精度，并谨慎处理暗能量模型简并性。

Abstract: The dispersion-measure (DM)--redshift relation of fast radio bursts (FRBs) provides independent low-redshift cosmological information for neutrino-mass inference. In this work, we perform joint analyses with cosmic microwave background (CMB) data, using 120 localized FRBs with reliable host-galaxy redshifts and larger mock samples, to investigate the effects of sample size and the dark-energy model on constraints on the sum of neutrino masses, $\sum m_ν$. In the $Λ$CDM+$\sum m_ν$ model, CMB+FRB gives $\sum m_ν<0.21\,\mathrm{eV}$, substantially relaxed relative to the current CMB+DESI result. With 1000 simulated events, CMB+FRB1(mock) gives an upper limit of $0.12\,\mathrm{eV}$, which decreases to $0.035\,\mathrm{eV}$ for CMB+FRB2(mock) with 10000 events. The latter is more stringent than the CMB+DESI limit, demonstrating the potential of large localized FRB samples to constrain neutrino mass. When the model is extended to $w_0w_a$CDM+$\sum m_ν$, both the observed and mock samples yield upper limits of approximately $0.3$--$0.4\,\mathrm{eV}$, indicating that the assumed dark-energy model remains a dominant factor in these constraints. As localized FRB samples grow and the modeling of the individual DM contributions becomes more accurate, FRBs may provide further independent low-redshift information for constraining neutrino mass.

</details>


### [125] [Magnetogenesis in Low-Scale Inflation: Viability for Astrophysical Magnetic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19721)
*Ramkishor Sharma,Dilip Kumar,Kandaswamy Subramanian,Soma Sanyal*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在低能标暴胀（特别是电弱能标附近）背景下，通过赝标量暴胀子与规范场耦合机制（g_φγ φ Fμν ̃F^μν）产生原初磁场的可行性，并分析了施温格反作用对磁场放大的抑制效应及最终磁场强度和相干长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系际介质中磁场存在的间接证据促使人们研究其可能的原初起源，尤其是暴胀时期的产生机制。

Method: 理论建模与计算分析：考虑赝标量暴胀子与规范场的轴耦合项，计算暴胀后规范场的放大过程；引入施温格机制产生的带电流传导及其反作用，分析其对规范场能量密度饱和的影响；结合再热温度与耦合常数约束，推导现今宇宙磁场强度与相干长度。

Result: 施温格反作用使规范场能量密度饱和于 ~m_e^4；当再热温度 ≤ 0.3 GeV 时，可产生高于 blazar 观测下限的磁场；对于 100 GeV 暴胀能标，需 g_φγ ≥ 20000 m_pl^{-1} 才能达此饱和；对应 100 MeV 再热温度，得到现今磁场强度 B₀ ∼ 2×10⁻¹³ G、相干长度 L₀ ∼ 5×10⁻³ kpc。

Conclusion: 该暴胀磁生成机制在低能标暴胀下仍具可行性，但需较强耦合及较低再热温度；施温格反作用是关键物理限制，决定了最终磁场幅度。

Abstract: The indirect evidence for magnetic fields in the intergalactic medium motivates the study of their possible primordial origin, including generation during inflation. One mechanism for inflationary magnetogenesis involves a pseudoscalar inflaton coupled to a gauge field through the interaction $g_{φγ}φF_{μν}\tilde{F}^{μν}$. In this work, we study the viability of this mechanism in the context of low scale inflation, particularly on inflationary energy scales near the electroweak (EW) scale. We find that the post inflationary amplification of the gauge fields can produce astrophysically relevant magnetic fields for $g_{φγ}\ge 360~m_{\rm pl}^{-1}$ in the absence of Schwinger backreaction for an inflationary scale around the EW scale. We then include the backreaction of the charged current generated through the Schwinger mechanism. We find that the induced current suppresses the gauge field amplification and leads to a saturation of the gauge field energy density at a value of order $m_e^4$, where $m_e$ is the electron mass. Assuming this saturation level, we find that magnetic fields above the lower bound inferred from the non detection of secondary GeV photons from blazars can be obtained when reheating occurs at temperatures below $\sim 0.3$ GeV. For an inflationary energy scale of $100~{\rm GeV}$, achieving this saturation until a reheating temperature, $T\le 0.3~{\rm GeV}$, requires a coupling strength $g_{φγ}\ge 20000~m_{\rm pl}^{-1}$. We obtain a present-day magnetic field strength of $B_0 \sim 2\times10^{-13}$ G with a coherence length of $L_{0}\sim 5\times10^{-3}$ kpc for the reheating temperature of $100$ MeV.

</details>


### [126] [Towards the LSST DESC pipeline for cosmology with galaxy clusters -- design and validation on simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19899)
*E. J. Barroso,C. Payerne,M. Aguena,C. Combet,S. Elles,T. Guillemin,M. Ricci,S. D. P. Vitenti,J. Zuntz,N. Amouroux,M. April,C. Avestruz,D. Boutigny,N. E. Chisari,A. Fumagalli,C. García-García,N. Lorenzo Martinez,A. I. Malz,M. Paterno,E. M. Pedersen,M. Penna-Lima,E. S. Rykoff,N. Šarčević,R. Solomon,K. Tsiane,Y. Zhang,LSST Dark Energy Science Collaboration*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LSST DESC为星系团宇宙学分析开发的自动化框架，该框架整合了从数据输入到参数推断的完整流程，并在cosmoDC2模拟数据上验证了其准确性，成功恢复了基准宇宙学参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为迎接Vera C. Rubin天文台LSST首批数据发布，需构建一个可复现、端到端的星系团宇宙学分析框架。

Method: 构建集成化pipeline，涵盖星系/星系团目录导入、星系团计数与弱引力透镜测量、建模及后验采样参数推断；使用cosmoDC2模拟数据进行联合红MaPPer丰度与堆叠弱透镜轮廓分析。

Result: 基线分析在0.51σ和0.34σ内恢复cosmoDC2设定的Ω_c和σ_8值；评估了不同宇宙学假设与建模方案对约束的影响。

Conclusion: 该pipeline具备高精度与灵活性，但尚存局限，需进一步改进以适配真实LSST数据。

Abstract: We present the current implementation of an automated framework developed within the Legacy Survey of Space and Time Dark Energy Science Collaboration (LSST DESC) for galaxy cluster cosmology, in preparation for the first LSST data releases of the NSF-DOE Vera C. Rubin Observatory. The cluster cosmology pipeline integrates DESC-developed tools dedicated to galaxy/cluster catalog ingestion, cluster number count and weak lensing measurements, modeling, and parameter inference through posterior sampling, enabling reproducible end-to-end cluster cosmology analyses. We test and validate the pipeline using synthetic mock datasets and the LSST DESC cosmoDC2 simulation, performing a joint analysis of redMaPPer cluster abundance and stacked weak lensing profiles measured in bins of redshift and richness. We constrain two cosmological parameters, $Ω_c$ and $σ_8$, along with six cluster mass-observable relation parameters. Our baseline analysis recovers the fiducial cosmoDC2 values of $Ω_c$ and $σ_8$ within $0.51σ$ and $0.34σ$, respectively. We also showcase the flexibility of the overall pipeline by assessing the impact of fiducial cosmology and alternative modeling prescriptions on the parameter constraints. Lastly, we also discuss the current limitations of the pipeline and the developments needed for LSST data.

</details>


### [127] [Galaxy number counts in area distance space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20910)
*Chris Clarkson,Roy Maartens,Stefano Zazzera*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用面积距离作为径向坐标时星系团聚的变化，推导了通量选择星系群的线性相对论计数，并与红移和光度距离空间进行比较；发现面积距离团聚无Kaiser畸变，引力透镜效应会增强其团聚但削弱与红移空间团聚的相关性，观测者运动导致其偶极子方向与红移和光度距离相反；三者组合可消除共同的本征密度、演化及放大偏差，剩余信号主要由近处速度和远处光学效应主导，并将其与星系尺度放大关联，从而无需单个星系精确距离即可统计推断其相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解不同距离定义（如面积距离、红移、光度距离）对星系团聚观测的影响，特别是相对论效应、引力透镜和观测者运动带来的系统性偏差，以提升宇宙学参数测量精度。

Method: 推导通量选择星系群在线性相对论框架下的数密度涨落（即'number counts'），分别在面积距离、红移和光度距离坐标系中建模，并分析源运动、引力透镜和观测者运动对各坐标系中团聚信号（含Kaiser效应、偶极子方向、相关性）的影响；进一步构造三者组合场以抑制共模偏差，并建立其与星系尺度引力透镜放大效应的联系。

Result: 面积距离空间中星系团聚不含Kaiser畸变；引力透镜增强面积距离团聚但降低其与红移空间团聚的相关性；观测者运动使面积距离壳层移动方向与红移/光度距离相反，导致偶极子方向相反；三场组合可消除本征密度、显式演化和放大偏差，剩余信号由局域速度（近距）和光学效应（远距）主导，并可等效为星系尺度的统计放大信号。

Conclusion: 面积距离提供了一种规避Kaiser畸变的新观测视角，结合多距离坐标系的联合分析可有效分离并提取相对论和引力透镜效应，为无精确红移或距离信息下的统计宇宙学探测提供了新途径。

Abstract: We investigate how galaxy clustering changes when area distance is used as the radial coordinate. We derive the linear relativistic number counts for a flux-selected population and compare them with redshift and luminosity distance space. Source motion does not change the beam area at a fixed emission event, so area-distance clustering has no Kaiser distortion. Lensing changes both the angular and radial positions of sources, and can enhance area-distance clustering while weakening its correlation with redshift-space clustering. Observer motion shifts area-distance shells in the opposite direction to redshift and luminosity-distance shells, allowing their kinematic dipoles to point in opposite directions. A combination of the three count fields cancels their common intrinsic density and explicit evolution and magnification biases, leaving a signal dominated by velocities nearby and optical effects at larger distances. We relate this signal to galaxy-size magnification, so that its correlations can be inferred statistically without precise distances to individual galaxies.

</details>


### [128] [Weak lensing data beyond flexion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21226)
*Ben D. Normann,Martin H. Enoksen,Nicolò Oreste Pinciroli Vago,Hans Georg Schaathun*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了弱引力透镜中高于挠曲（flexion）的高阶透镜效应（如四极、五极项）在质量重建中的有效性，发现其对星系团等复杂、高噪声系统尤其有用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挠曲已被证明能提供额外的质量分布约束，但更高阶项是否同样有用尚不清楚；本文旨在验证弱透镜中挠曲之后的高阶信息是否有助于提升透镜质量重建精度，尤其是在星系团等复杂系统中。

Method: 采用roulette-amplitude形式构建SIE和SIS星系团透镜的合成数据集（含/不含高斯噪声），并分别截断至三阶（挠曲）、四阶和五阶振幅，通过最小二乘法重建透镜参数。

Result: 高阶振幅确实提供了额外且有用的信息：单个透镜增益有限，但在星系团透镜中显著改善旋转角和椭率的恢复；尤其在噪声存在时，三阶重建易失效，而四、五阶可大幅恢复精度。

Conclusion: 随着巡天深度与精度提升，利用高于挠曲的弱透镜信息有望实质性改进最复杂、噪声最强系统的质量重建，应推动基于真实观测数据的后续研究。

Abstract: In weak lensing, shear and convergence are well studied and have provided a wealth of information about the distribution of matter in the universe. More recently, flexion -- the next term in the multipole expansion of the lensing potential, responsible for the characteristic banana-shaped distortion of lensed images -- has been shown to provide additional constraints on the mass distribution of lensing systems, particularly for galaxy clusters. Here we investigate whether weak-lensing information beyond flexion is similarly useful for lens-mass reconstruction. Using the roulette-amplitude formalism, we construct synthetic datasets for a single SIE lens and for SIE and SIS cluster lenses, with and without simulated Gaussian noise, and perform a least-squares reconstruction of the lens parameters using amplitudes truncated at third (flexion), fourth, and fifth order. As a proof of concept, we find that higher-order amplitudes do provide additional, useful information: the benefit is modest for a single lens, where flexion already constrains most parameters, but becomes substantial for cluster lenses, where the rotation and ellipticity are otherwise poorly recovered. The improvement is most pronounced in the presence of noise, where third-order reconstruction can become unreliable while higher-order amplitudes restore much of the lost accuracy. These results suggest that, as weak-lensing surveys grow in depth and precision, data beyond flexion could meaningfully improve mass reconstruction for the most complex and noisiest lensing systems, motivating further work using observational rather than synthetic data.

</details>
