### [129] [Nonperturbative lattice simulation of the nucleation rate in thermal first-order phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18794)
*Jing Zhou,Ligong Bian*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过非微扰格点方法研究了三维实标量场理论在有限温度下的真空衰变过程，结合扩展系综多重正则混合蒙特卡洛与实时朗之万动力学，计算了气泡成核率，并与一阶微扰结果比较发现约8%的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解有限温度下真空衰变的非微扰机制，检验微扰理论在强耦合或临界区域的适用性。

Method: 采用扩展系综多重正则混合蒙特卡洛方法采样临界气泡构型，并结合实时朗之万动力学模拟气泡后续演化，最终外推至连续极限。

Result: 得到了真空衰变的气泡成核率，并在连续极限下与一阶微扰预言相比存在约8%的偏差。

Conclusion: 一阶微扰理论虽大致有效，但在定量精度上存在不可忽略的非微扰修正，需更精确的非微扰方法进行校准。

Abstract: We present a nonperturbative lattice study of vacuum decay through bubble nucleation in a three-dimensional real scalar field theory at finite temperature. The vacuum decay rate is computed by combining an expanded-ensemble multicanonical hybrid Monte Carlo approach, which samples the critical bubble configurations, with real-time Langevin dynamics, which follows the subsequent bubble evolution. We extrapolate the nucleation rate to the continuum limit and compare it with the corresponding one-loop perturbative prediction, finding a discrepancy of about \(8\%\).

</details>


### [130] [Spatiotemporal K-Shape: Reconstructing inhomogeneous condensate profiles on the lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19306)
*Keita Horie,Rin Kagami,Chiho Nonaka*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Spatiotemporal K-Shape的新方法，用于从格点蒙特卡洛系综中无先验假设地提取空间调制手征凝聚态的代表性实空间形状，并验证其在(1+1)维Gross-Neveu模型中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统两体关联函数无法保留傅里叶相位信息，因而无法确定空间构型的具体形状；需发展能直接刻画非均匀序实空间结构的新分析工具。

Method: 扩展K-Shape聚类算法至周期性格点场，支持平移对齐（及可选的整体符号/相位对齐），从中提取聚类质心作为代表性形状；通过多组聚类比较判断单一形状是否足够表征整体多样性。

Result: 在Gross-Neveu模型中成功提取出含两个符号翻转的非正弦轮廓，并在某一化学势下识别出多个形状族；引入手征符号对称性对齐后，可将畴长涨落映射为不对称质心，而无需假定系综本征不对称性。

Conclusion: 该方法弥补了关联函数诊断的不足，提供了实空间代表性形状及其多样性的定量信息，为QCD相图中非均匀序的研究提供了新工具和测试平台。

Abstract: Spatially modulated chiral condensates can affect the phase structure of dense QCD effective models. Understanding such order requires characterizing its real-space structure as well as detecting a modulation. Spatial two-point correlators retain the ensemble-averaged power spectrum but discard relative Fourier phases, leaving the configuration shapes undetermined in general. We propose the Spatiotemporal K-Shape method to extract representative shapes from lattice Monte Carlo ensembles without assuming a functional form. The method extends K-Shape clustering to periodic lattice fields, aligning configurations modulo translations and, optionally, a global sign or phase before extracting cluster centroids. Comparisons across clusterings assess whether one representative shape suffices. Controlled tests recover distinct profiles with identical correlators and separate mixtures of different shapes. Applied to the $(1+1)$-dimensional Gross-Neveu model with $N_f=8$, the method extracts non-sinusoidal profiles with two sign changes at two chemical potentials and separates multiple shape families at a third, where a single centroid is insufficient. Including the discrete chiral sign symmetry in the alignment can fold domain-length fluctuations into an asymmetric centroid without implying a preferred ensemble asymmetry. The method thus complements correlator diagnostics with representative real-space shapes and information on their diversity. The application to the Gross-Neveu model provides a testing ground for configuration-based studies of inhomogeneous order relevant to the QCD phase diagram.

</details>


### [131] [Fate of chiral and $U(1)_A$ symmetries in hot QCD matter under background magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19758)
*Dan Zhang,Heng-Tong Ding,José Javier Hernández Hernández*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了背景磁场下格点QCD中手征对称性和单态U(1)_A对称性破缺行为，发现弱磁场增强对称性破缺，而强磁场在近相变区反而抑制破缺，且对U(1)_A破缺的抑制较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究背景磁场如何影响QCD中手征对称性和U(1)_A对称性的破缺行为，特别是由于夸克电荷差异导致的非单态味对称性显式破缺。

Method: 采用固定尺度的(2+1)味HISQ格点QCD组态，轻夸克质量取m_l = m_s^phys/10（零场π介子质量约220 MeV），计算中性通道磁化率差χ_π⁰−χ_σ（表征手征对称性破缺）和χ_π⁰−χ_δ⁰（表征U(1)_A对称性破缺）。

Result: 低温下磁场增强两个磁化率差；在近交叉温度区域，足够强的磁场反而抑制两者；U(1)_A相关抑制起始磁场更强、程度更弱。

Conclusion: 磁场对QCD对称性破缺的影响具有温度与强度依赖性：低温增强破缺，高温近相变区强磁场则削弱破缺，且U(1)_A破缺比手征破缺更稳健。

Abstract: We summarize our recent lattice QCD study of chiral and singlet $U(1)_A$ symmetry breaking in background magnetic fields. The magnetic field explicitly reduces the non-singlet flavor symmetry through the unequal light-quark electric charges. We therefore study two neutral-channel susceptibility differences: $χ_{π^0}-χ_σ$ for chiral symmetry breaking and $χ_{π^0}-χ_{δ^0}$ for singlet $U(1)_A$ symmetry breaking. The calculation uses fixed-scale $(2+1)$-flavor HISQ ensembles with $m_l=m_s^{\mathrm{phys}}/10$, corresponding to a zero-field pion mass of $M_π\simeq220\,\mathrm{MeV}$. Magnetic fields enhance both susceptibility differences at low temperatures. Near the crossover, sufficiently strong fields instead suppress both differences. The suppression sets in at larger magnetic fields and is milder for the singlet-axial susceptibility difference than for the neutral chiral susceptibility difference.

</details>


### [132] [Canonical partition function and equation of state in the heavy quark region of finite-density lattice gauge theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20178)
*Shinji Ejiri,Hideya Hashizume,Masanari Koiida*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限温密度下格点规范理论的相结构和状态方程，提出了一种基于粒子数分解大正则配分函数以缓解符号问题的算法，并在SU(2)规范理论中首次验证；通过引入微小外场解决中心对称性导致的奇粒子数扇区配分函数为零的问题，最终重构大正则配分函数并计算热力学观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决有限密度格点QCD中由夸克行列式复相位引起的严重符号问题，特别是重夸克、高密度区域下的计算困难。

Method: 采用按粒子数分解大正则配分函数的方法构建正则配分函数；针对SU(Nc)中心对称性导致奇粒子数扇区配分函数为零的问题，引入微小外场加以调控；结合蒙特卡洛模拟计算正则配分函数，并重构大正则配分函数以提取热力学量。

Result: 在SU(2)理论中验证该方法：偶粒子扇区符号问题较轻，奇粒子扇区严重；引入外场后成功计算全密度范围的热力学量；低温度下物态方程符合强子共振气模型特征，未发现有限密度下QCD物质软化现象。

Conclusion: 基于粒子数分解的正则方法可有效缓解重夸克区域的符号问题；中心对称性约束需在外场下谨慎处理；结果支持低温强子相图像，且未见致密QCD物质出现软化，对理解QCD相结构具有重要意义。

Abstract: We investigate the phase structure and equations of state in lattice gauge theory at finite temperature and finite density, focusing on the canonical partition function obtained by decomposing the grand partition function with respect to particle number. We demonstrate that when quark masses are heavy, the complex phase of the quark determinant, which causes the sign problem, can be controlled by decomposing the grand partition function according to particle number. In this study, we propose an algorithm to calculate the canonical partition function for heavy-quark, high-density effective theory and test it for the first time using one-flavor $SU(2)$ gauge theory, a case where the sign problem arises. When the grand partition function of $SU(2)$ is decomposed into canonical partition functions, the sign problem is serious in odd-particle sectors, while it is mild in even-particle sectors. The odd-particle sectors also expose a fundamental issue: center symmetry makes the canonical partition function of $SU(N_c)$ vanish unless the particle number is an integer multiple of $N_c$. Although appropriate in the confinement phase, this restriction must be treated carefully in the deconfinement phase. We resolve the issue by introducing an infinitesimal external field, calculate the canonical partition functions by Monte Carlo simulation, reconstruct the grand partition function, and determine thermodynamic observables over a wide range of temperature and density. Furthermore, we find that the chemical-potential dependence of the equation of state at low temperatures exhibits behavior characteristic of the hadron resonance gas model, and that no softening of the QCD matter occurs at finite density.

</details>


### [133] [Transferable neural-network preconditioners for Wilson--Clover propagator inversions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20990)
*Shiyi Zhong,Yuanyi Zhang,Jian-Hui Zhang,Dian-Jun Zhao*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种单层神经网络架构，用于加速四维Wilson-Clover传播子求逆计算，并能在不同格点参数间无需重训练直接迁移应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决格点QCD中四维Wilson-Clover传播子求逆计算耗时长的问题，探索高效、可迁移的神经网络加速方法。

Method: 提出两种单层神经网络：一是规范协变的平行输运卷积（PTC）网络，直接减少广义共轭残差（GCR）迭代次数；二是类CNN网络，结合多重网格启发式算法；二者可联合使用。

Result: PTC网络可在不同格点体积、格距和π介子质量间无重训练迁移并保持加速效果；类CNN网络可从小体积直接迁移到大体积格点并实现强加速；联合使用可进一步减少GCR迭代；整体方案在大体积格点上更具成本优势。

Conclusion: 所提神经网络方法具备良好泛化性和可迁移性，尤其适用于大规模格点QCD计算，显著提升传播子求逆效率。

Abstract: We investigate two single--layer neural-network architectures for accelerating four-dimensional Wilson--Clover propagator inversions on lattice ensembles in practical use; both transfer directly without retraining. The first is a gauge-covariant parallel transport convolution (PTC) network that directly reduces the number of generalized conjugate residual (GCR) solver iterations. The second is a CNN-like network used together with a multigrid-inspired algorithm. The two approaches can be combined to further reduce GCR iterations. PTC transfers without retraining across lattice volumes, lattice spacings, and pion masses while maintaining comparable acceleration, whereas the CNN-like network transfers directly from small- to large-volume ensembles with strong acceleration. A cost comparison accounting for both basis construction and solves shows that the proposed scheme is more advantageous for large-volume ensembles than for small-volume ones.

</details>


### [134] [Global analysis of the $ρ$ resonance in lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21112)
*Luka Jevsenak,Luka Leskovec,Stefan Meinel,John Negele,Srijit Paul,Andrew Pochinsky*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过格点QCD研究ρ共振态，采用全局分析四个N_f=2+1组态，创新性地将离散化效应参数化在格点能级而非散射振幅中，并使用K矩阵参数化描述连续谱中的散射振幅，同时纳入轻夸克和奇异夸克质量依赖；最终通过模型平均得到物理极限下ρ介子极点位置为744(11)(7) − i 69(7)(3) MeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确提取ρ共振态的物理极点位置，需系统处理格点QCD中夸克质量外推和格距离散化效应带来的系统误差。

Method: 对四个N_f=2+1格点QCD组态进行全局分析；将离散化效应参数化于格点能级（而非散射振幅）；在连续极限下用K矩阵参数化散射振幅，并显式包含轻、奇异夸克质量依赖；通过改变质量与格距依赖形式评估系统误差，并做模型平均。

Result: 得到物理极限下ρ介子极点位置为√s_ρ = 744(11)(7) − i 69(7)(3) MeV，其中第一误差为统计误差，第二为系统误差。

Conclusion: 该方法有效分离并控制了格距与夸克质量外推的系统不确定性，提高了ρ共振态极点位置的精度与可靠性，为强子谱学中宽共振态的格点研究提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present a lattice-QCD study of the $ρ$ resonance in which we address the quark-mass and lattice-spacing dependence in a novel way using a global analysis of four $N_f=2+1$ ensembles. We parametrize discretization effects in the lattice energy levels and not in the scattering amplitude, which is defined in the continuum. We describe the amplitude using a $K$-matrix parameterization, which contains the light- and strange-quark mass dependence. By varying the parameterizations of quark-mass and lattice-spacing dependence, we examine the sensitivity of the physical-limit $ρ$ pole position to these choices and obtain the result $\sqrt{s}_ρ= 744(11)(7) - i\,69(7)(3)~\text{MeV}$ from a model average.

</details>


### [135] [Finite temperature Sp(4) lattice gauge theory with matter fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21493)
*Chris Allton,Ed Bennett,Luigi Del Debbio,Ryan C. Hill,Deog Ki Hong,Jong-Wan Lee,C. -J. David Lin,Biagio Lucini,Maurizio Piai,Davide Vadacchino,Alexis Verney-Provatas*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文首次在有限温度下对Sp(4)规范理论耦合两个Wilson-Dirac费米子进行了格点研究，旨在探索其在复合动力学与暗物质SIMP范式中的应用，并评估其可能产生的可探测引力波背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Sp(4)规范理论在有限温度下的相变行为，特别是其是否产生强一阶热相变，从而在早期宇宙中生成可观测的随机引力波背景，以支持SIMP暗物质模型。

Method: 采用精细网格扫描格点耦合参数，对不同时间维大小、空间体积和费米子质量进行模拟；测量Polyakov圈及其磁化率，结合重加权与有限体积分析来判定相变性质并估算临界耦合；用纯规范Sp(4) Yang-Mills理论验证方法可靠性。

Result: 在较重费米子质量下首次发现一阶热相变的证据；而中等轻费米子情形则呈现明显的交叉行为，未见相变奇点；纯规范理论验证成功，确认了一阶相变。

Conclusion: Sp(4)理论在大费米子质量区域可能存在一阶热相变，为后续在连续极限下开展大规模格点研究提供了关键动机，也拓展至更一般的Sp(2N)规范理论。

Abstract: We perform the first finite-temperature lattice study of the Sp(4) gauge theory coupled to two Wilson-Dirac fermions transforming in the fundamental representation. Its continuum limit plays an important role in many extensions of the Standard Model for particle physics with composite dynamics. For example, it provides a compelling realisation of the strongly interacting massive particle (SIMP) paradigm for dark matter. If realised in nature, it might lead to a first-order thermal phase transition in the early universe, and produce a detectable relic stochastic background of gravitational waves (GW). Assessing whether future experiments are sensitive to the resulting GW power spectrum requires determining the strength of the transition. We scan the parameter space of this lattice theory with a fine mesh of choices of lattice coupling, for two small values of the temporal extent of the lattice, and several choices of spatial volume and fermion mass. We measure the Polyakov loop and its susceptibility on the ensembles generated, implement reweighting, and perform a finite volume analysis, both to classify the nature of the phase transition, and to estimate the critical coupling. We validate our algorithms and analysis procedures on the better understood Sp(4) Yang-Mills theory, by confirming the presence of a first-order phase transition. Upon coupling the theory to matter fields, in the presence of moderately light fermions, the Polyakov loop shows a clear crossover behaviour and does not detect singularities associated with a phase transition. We also uncover the first evidence of a first-order thermal phase transition in the case of the largest fermion masses available. This finding motivates future, large-scale studies aimed at characterising the transition in the continuum limit, for this and closely related theories with Sp(2N) gauge group.

</details>


### [136] [Hadron structure on the 16-cell honeycomb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21543)
*Sandor Katz,Daniel Nogradi*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了在16胞蜂窝晶格上进行非微扰QCD计算，相较于传统立方晶格，该晶格具有更大的时空对称性，从而显著减小截断效应，并提升对强子结构函数的格点计算精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统立方晶格存在较大的截断效应，限制了强子结构函数等物理量的精确格点计算；而16胞蜂窝晶格具有更大的对称性，有望改善这一问题。

Method: 基于16胞蜂窝晶格的对称性分析，系统研究其群表示理论，对比立方晶格，分析极化/非极化、正向/非正向、扭度2与扭度3算符矩阵元中不可约表示的数量与混合行为，并评估其对重整化及计算效率的影响。

Result: 16胞蜂窝晶格提供更多不可约表示，抑制算符混合、减少幂律发散；更多表示适用于静止强子，提升计算效率；可比立方晶格在连续极限下确定更多矩。

Conclusion: 16胞蜂窝晶格为强子结构函数的高精度格点计算提供了更优离散化方案，具备更小截断效应、更好对称保护和更高计算可行性。

Abstract: In previous work we started a program of studying non-perturbative QCD on the 4-dimensional 16-cell honeycomb lattice instead of the usually adopted cubic lattice. It was shown that in several observables much smaller cut-off effects are observed, undoubtedly a result of the larger space-time symmetry of this discretization. In the present work further consequences are worked out, relevant for the lattice determination of hadronic structure functions. The key ingredient is the larger set of irreducible representations of the 16-cell honeycomb symmetry group relative to the cubic lattice, leading to more protection from mixing and thus less power divergences. A second ingredient is that more irreducible representations are available for a hadron at rest, which also leads to computational gain in practice. The analysis is given in full detail for both polarized and unpolarized and both forward and off-forward matrix elements of twist-2 and twist-3 operators. It is shown that a larger set of moments can be determined with continuum-like renormalization and at rest, than on the cubic lattice.

</details>

### [137] [Schwarzschild scattering from CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18826)
*Paolo Arnaudo,Benjamin Withers*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文展示了如何利用AdS4中描述小黑洞的CFT相关函数来推导渐近平直Schwarzschild时空的散射矩阵元素及准正规模谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立AdS/CFT对偶与渐近平直时空物理之间的联系，特别是通过CFT计算获取黑洞散射和准正规模信息。

Method: 识别一类在高频率下于AdS边界表现为平面波的算符，建立CFT关联函数与反射系数之间的解析关系，并取AdS半径L→∞的平直空间极限。

Result: 成功导出渐近平直Schwarzschild时空的偏波S矩阵元及准正规模谱；该方法适用于自旋1、自旋2及共形耦合标量场。

Conclusion: Retarded CFT相关函数可作为桥梁，将AdS中的小黑洞物理映射到平直空间黑洞散射问题，揭示了耗散准正规模可在有效厄米系统中短时涌现。

Abstract: We show that retarded CFT correlators on $\mathbb{R}\times \mathbb{S}^2$ in a state described by the small black hole in AdS$_4$ can be used to derive asymptotically flat Schwarzschild partial-wave S-matrix elements, including its spectrum of quasinormal modes. This is achieved by identifying a class of operators for which the dual bulk fields behave as plane waves near the AdS boundary for sufficiently high frequencies. For such operators there is a simple analytic relation between CFT correlators and reflection coefficients which behaves gracefully in the flat space limit, $L\to \infty$. These include spin-1, spin-2, and conformally coupled scalars. We provide a simple toy model that captures how dissipative QNMs can appear for short times inside Hermitian systems, closely resembling the small black hole in AdS.

</details>


### [138] [Gravitational Memory Correlators and BMS Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18827)
*Enrico Herrmann,Ian Moult,Brett Oertel,Julio Parra-Martinez,Biswajit Sahoo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文使用in-in形式主义在微扰量子引力的领头阶下计算了标量粒子衰变为引力子过程中的n点记忆效应和混合能量-记忆关联函数，并验证了记忆、能量与Bondi质量探测器之间的超平移流平衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究红外有限可观测量如何满足渐近对称性约束，特别是超平移对称性在引力记忆效应中的体现。

Method: 采用in-in形式主义，在微扰量子引力的领头阶下计算标量衰变到引力子过程的n点记忆及混合能量-记忆关联函数。

Result: 得到了任意n点的紧凑解析结果，并显式验证了记忆、能量与Bondi质量探测器之间的超平移流平衡关系。

Conclusion: 引力波记忆效应与能量辐射受渐近超平移对称性严格约束，该约束在红外有限可观测量中得以实现。

Abstract: We compute the n-point memory and mixed energy-memory correlators for scalar decay into gravitons using the in-in formalism at leading order in perturbative quantum gravity. We obtain compact results for arbitrary n and explicitly verify the supertranslation flux balance relation between memory, energy, and Bondi mass detectors, realizing asymptotic symmetry constraints on infrared-finite observables.

</details>


### [139] [Rational conformal interfaces between the Ising and tricritical Ising models from a tetrality web](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18831)
*António Antunes,Jin Chen,Junchen Rong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了Ising模型与三临界Ising（TIM）模型之间的有理共形界面，通过不同手征对称性构造折叠边界态，并利用‘上移’与‘下移’方法揭示了一个关联四个理论的‘四元性网络’；从中导出18个可分解界面和6个透射界面，后者对应RG墙等重要物理对象，并系统计算了其能量传输系数、边界熵、体关联及算符谱等可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建Ising与三临界Ising模型之间具有明确共形结构和物理意义的有理界面，以深入理解不同临界相之间的RG流、对偶性及边界/界面统一描述。

Method: 采用折叠边界态方法，结合基于S₃自同构群的‘上移’（扩展至辅助coset）与‘下移’（约化至S₃不变代数）两种构造策略，建立四元性网络；再将最小理论中的Cardy边界态拉回至物理张量积系统，分类并识别界面类型，并计算各类可观测量。

Result: 发现18个因子化界面和6个透射界面；6个透射界面承载TIM融合环的正则作用，具有相同能量传输系数35/48，但边界熵、体关联函数和算符谱互异；完整计算了24个边界间的环面振幅。

Conclusion: Ising与TIM模型间存在由S₃对称性组织的深层结构——四元性网络，不仅统一描述多种已知界面（如Gaiotto RG墙），还揭示了透射界面在融合代数、能量输运与量子纠缠等方面的丰富区分特征，为共形界面分类与RG流几何提供了新范式。

Abstract: We construct rational conformal interfaces between the Ising and tricritical Ising (TIM) models by using different chiral symmetries to prepare their folded boundary states. We use a moving up procedure which extends the product theory to an auxiliary coset with an $S_3$ automorphism group, where permutations give twisted Cardy states. We complement this with a moving down approach which reduces to the $S_3$ invariant algebra. Overall, these procedures uncover a tetrality web relating four different theories. Pulling back ordinary Cardy states of the smallest theory in the web to the physical tensor product system gives eighteen factorized interfaces and six transmitting interfaces. The latter agree with four transposition and two 3-cycle twisted boundaries in the largest theory. They include Gaiotto's RG wall and the interface obtained by fusing an Ising duality line into it. The six interfaces carry the regular action of the TIM fusion ring and have the same energy transmission coefficient, $35/48$. Their boundary entropies, bulk correlations and operator spectra distinguish them. We calculate these observables and the complete annulus amplitudes between the twenty four boundaries.

</details>


### [140] [Constants and Nonperturbative Corrections in $V^{5,2}$ Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18832)
*Seyed Morteza Hosseini*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了与非环面Sasaki-Einstein流形V^{5,2}的自由和定点循环商相关的两个三维N=2超对称理论的扭结超势的微扰与指数小修正，揭示其模结构共性及非微扰系数律的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非环面Sasaki-Einstein背景下三维N=2理论的精确全阶超势结构，特别是微扰、指数小项与模性质之间的关系，并统一处理自由与定点商两类几何情形。

Method: 结合超共形化学势下的解析延拓、Clausen函数与ζ(3)显式求值、Eichler积分构造权为4的Eisenstein级数、高精度Bethe方程数值验证，以及M理论相对局域化框架下的球面配分函数b→0极限分析。

Result: 获得两类理论（双节点Chern-Simons与单节点带味理论）的收敛积分表示；在整数参数下给出含Clausen函数和ζ(3)的显式常数；提出k=1,3与N_f=1,3时由Eichler积分表征的完整指数余项；确认Eisenstein生成元的最小模水平及原函数的收敛性与自然边界；并通过球面配分函数极限复现Chern-Simons位移秩与常数。

Conclusion: 两类理论共享非微扰系数的模结构，但其微扰位移与指数修正不同，从而解释了它们在主导N^{3/2}行为下看似相同、实则本质有别的物理。

Abstract: We study perturbative and exponentially small corrections to the on-shell effective twisted superpotentials of two three-dimensional $\mathcal N=2$ theories associated with free and fixed-locus cyclic quotients of the non-toric Sasaki-Einstein manifold $V^{5,2}$. These are the two-node Chern-Simons theory at levels $(k,-k)$ and the one-node flavored theory with $N_f$ fundamental pairs, respectively. At superconformal chemical potentials, we propose the Chern-Simons counterpart of the all-order perturbative expression for the flavored theory and obtain convergent integral representations for the rank-independent constants of both theories. At integer parameters, these constants admit explicit evaluations involving finite sums of Clausen functions and $ζ(3)$, while their large-parameter expansions Borel sum to the defining integrals. At $k=1,3$ and $N_f=1,3$, we conjecture complete exponential remainders expressed through Eichler integrals of weight-four Eisenstein series. Supported by high-precision Bethe solutions, the resulting completions coincide at unit quotient order but differ at order three. We determine the minimal modular levels of the Eisenstein generators and establish convergence and natural boundaries for their primitives. Within the relative M-theory localization prescription, a complementary bulk analysis recovers the Chern-Simons shifted rank and constant in the $b\to0^+$ limit of the squashed-sphere partition function. We propose a generalization incorporating fixed-locus contributions that yields the corresponding flavored-theory result. The two theories therefore share a modular structure in their nonperturbative coefficient laws, but distinct perturbative shifts and exponential corrections resolve differences invisible in their common leading $N^{3/2}$ behavior.

</details>


### [141] [Quadratic Sum Rules for Scalar Amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18836)
*Karol Kampf,Jiří Novotný,David Podivín,Jonah Stalknecht*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发现了一类新的二次求和规则，连接不同重数的标量振幅，并将其推广到非线性σ模型、其弦启发扩展、KLT矩阵对角元及具有非导数相互作用和平凡传播子的平面标量理论中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Kleiss-Kuijf、BCJ关系及其弦单值性推广在约束部分振幅方面成功启发，作者试图寻找新的振幅约束关系。

Method: 结合递归方法、运动学位移和符号回归技术来建立这些二次求和规则。

Result: 发现了适用于非线性σ模型及其弦启发扩展的新二次求和规则，并进一步推广至KLT矩阵对角元以及更一般的平面标量理论。

Conclusion: 该工作拓展了振幅关系的研究范畴，为理解不同理论间振幅结构的普适性提供了新工具和视角。

Abstract: Kleiss--Kuijf and Bern--Carrasco--Johansson relations, and their string monodromy generalizations, have long provided some of the most powerful and useful constraints on partial amplitudes. Motivated by their success, we uncover a new class of quadratic-sum rules that connect scalar amplitudes at different multiplicities. Combining recursion, kinematic shifts, and symbolic regression, we establish them for the nonlinear sigma model and its string-inspired extensions. We then extend these relations to diagonal elements of the inverse Kawai--Lewellen--Tye matrix and subsequently to planar scalar theories with nonderivative interactions and general propagators.

</details>


### [142] [Generalised global symmetries of 6d SCFTs on a circle from the blow-up equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18837)
*William Harding,Noppadol Mekareeya*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了6维(1,0)超共形场论（SCFT）在圆周紧化（可能带扭转）下的性质，通过其5维描述的爆破方程，建立了6维与5维框架间的显式映射，并揭示了高阶形式对称性、混合反常及2-群结构如何由 blown-up C² 中例外曲线上的通量决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解6维(1,0) SCFT在圆周紧化（含扭转）下如何诱导5维理论的对称性结构（如1-形式、2-形式对称性、混合反常和2-群），并建立6维与5维物理量（如KK质量与瞬子质量之比r）之间的精确对应。

Method: 利用6d SCFT在圆上紧化后的5d有效描述，结合爆破方程（blow-up equations），分析例外曲线上的通量（fluxes）；将通量解释为5d 't Hooft扇区，并通过6d Green–Schwarz耦合确定通量权重；据此提取高阶对称性、混合反常及2-群结构。

Result: 发现6d高阶对称性结构（包括2-形式对称性与U(1)_KK/引力/味/ SU(2)_R 的混合反常、2-群）完全由例外曲线上通量决定；具体示例中识别出Z₄非分裂2-群、瞬子对称性与味中心锁定导致仅存混合反常等现象；该方法成功推广至E₁理论多拷贝SU(2)规范化的各类情形。

Conclusion: 6d (1,0) SCFT经圆紧化后，其对称性拓扑结构（高阶形式对称性、反常、2-群）可统一由5d爆破几何中的通量编码；该几何—物理字典为理解高维SCFT降维后的对称性演生与约束提供了系统框架。

Abstract: We study 6d $(1,0)$ SCFTs on a circle, possibly twisted, through the blow-up equations of their 5d descriptions. For every theory we consider, we construct the explicit map between the 6d and 5d frames, including the ratio $r$ of the Kaluza--Klein (KK) mass to the instanton mass, which depends on the frame and not only on the twist. Our main result is that the higher-form symmetries of these theories, their mixed anomalies and 2-groups can all be read off from the fluxes through the exceptional curve of the blown-up $\mathbb{C}^2$. The flux sectors are the 't$~$Hooft sectors of the 5d 1-form symmetries. On the tensor fluxes, the 6d Green--Schwarz couplings fix the weights, whose fractional exponents we read as the mixed anomalies of the 6d 2-form symmetry with the KK $\mathrm{U}(1)$, gravity, flavour and $\mathrm{SU}(2)_R$. The fluxes forced in each sector decide between a 2-group and a mixed anomaly. For instance, the twisted frames $\mathfrak{su}(4)_{0,4}+4Λ^2$ carry a non-split $\mathbb{Z}_4$ 2-group, while, in $\mathfrak{su}(4)_2+4Λ^2$ and $\mathfrak{su}(4)_6+2Λ^2$, the instanton symmetry is locked to the flavour centre and only a mixed anomaly remains. The same method applies to the $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ gauging of two, three and four copies of the $E_1$ theory, which describes, respectively, $\mathrm{SU}(4)_0$, pure $\mathrm{Spin}(8)$ and the untwisted $\mathfrak{so}(8)$ KK theory. There, the fluxes exhibit the extensions of 1-form symmetries inherited from the $E_1$ 2-groups, identify the states that break each accidental 1-form symmetry and the background to which it is locked, and give the 5d 2-group to which the $S_3$-invariant 6d 3-group reduces.

</details>


### [143] [Non-Topological Chiral Anomaly: The Chiral Magnetoelectric Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18841)
*Rémy Larue,Amaury Marchon,Jérémie Quevillon,Diego Saviot*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了热介质中的手性反常，发现除已知的拓扑项外，还存在一个此前被忽略的非拓扑贡献，该贡献与电磁场无关，可导致新型输运效应；并据此提出在韦尔半金属中可观测的‘手性磁电效应’。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示热介质中手性反常的完整结构，特别是针对非规范轴向对称性的非拓扑部分，以拓展反常诱导输运现象的理解与应用。

Method: 理论推导热介质中的手性反常，分析其拓扑与非拓扑成分，并结合相对论流体力学与凝聚态物理模型，预测新输运效应。

Result: 发现手性反常包含独立于电磁场的非拓扑项；提出并命名‘手性磁电效应’——即无外加电场时由非拓扑反常诱导、正比于磁场的电场响应；建立适用于凝聚态与高能系统的非拓扑手性反常流体动力学框架。

Conclusion: 非拓扑手性反常是真实且物理可观测的，它不仅修正了手性连续性方程，更催生新的输运机制，为韦尔半金属等体系提供新实验探针，并推动跨领域反常输运研究。

Abstract: We derive the chiral anomaly in a thermal medium and show that, in addition to the well-established topological piece, the anomaly acquires a previously overlooked non-topological contribution, relevant for non-gauged axial symmetries. Remarkably, this modification of the continuity equation is independent of the electromagnetic fields, thereby giving rise to novel transport effects in relativistic hydrodynamics. We further propose an experimentally accessible new effect in Weyl semimetals which we call the Chiral Magnetoelectric Effect: an electric field proportional to the magnetic field is generated by the non-topological anomaly, even in the absence of an externally applied electric field. Beyond this concrete prediction, our results establish a broader hydrodynamic framework for non-topological chiral anomalies, opening the door to a range of previously unexplored anomaly-induced transport phenomena across condensed-matter and high-energy systems.

</details>


### [144] [A Bound on Wormhole Probing Time from Holographic Multi-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19035)
*Takanori Anegawa,Norihiro Iizuka*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了多部分纠缠熵探测永恒虫洞增长的能力，证明对于非旋转BTZ黑洞，无论边界划分的分区数量、大小和排列如何，探测时间存在一个共同的有限上限，无法通过增加分区数无限延长探测时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双部纠缠熵最终会饱和，无法追踪永恒虫洞的持续增长；而近期发现真正的三部多熵可在双部熵饱和后继续探测虫洞增长，由此引出：增加分区数能否无限延长探测时间？

Method: 采用多路径极大极小（multiway maximin）方法，并假设所选代表网络是规则的时空稳态网络，对非旋转BTZ黑洞进行理论证明。

Result: 证明存在一个与分区数量、大小及排列无关的固定有限上界，超过该时间后，任何由极大极小方法选出的网络都无法进入黑洞内部，即无法再探测虫洞增长。

Conclusion: 增加分区数不能无限延长对虫洞增长的探测时间；对于非旋转BTZ黑洞，探测能力存在根本性的时间上限。

Abstract: Bipartite entanglement entropy eventually saturates and therefore cannot track the continued growth of an eternal wormhole. Recently, it was shown that genuine tripartite multi-entropy can probe wormhole growth even after bipartite entanglement entropies have saturated. This naturally raises the question: can increasing the number of parties extend the probing time indefinitely? Adopting the multiway maximin prescription and assuming that its selected representatives are regular spacetime-stationary networks, we prove that the answer is no for nonrotating BTZ black holes. We establish a common finite upper bound on the probing time for every fixed finite boundary partition, independent of the number, sizes, and arrangement of its parties. For any network on any Cauchy slice, there exists a comparison network that assigns the entire central region containing the wormhole interior to a single party. A network longer than this comparison network cannot minimize length on that slice. The central length required by a stationary network connecting the two boundaries grows with time, whereas the central length of the comparison network remains uniformly bounded. At sufficiently late times, such a network therefore cannot minimize length on any Cauchy slice containing it and is excluded from the maximin prescription. Consequently, beyond this bound, no network selected by maximin enters the black-hole interior.

</details>


### [145] [Projected DKP spin-1 sectors in a $(2+1)$-dimensional static wormhole: Self-duality, helicity content, and spectral correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19058)
*Luis B. Castro*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用2+1维投影自旋-1 Duffin-Kemmer-Petiau (DKP)方程，阐明了不可约3×3表示的物理内涵，并将其应用于静态虫洞问题，揭示了径向DKP系统中特殊的束缚态分支及准正规模的物理配对特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清2+1维中自旋-1 DKP方程不可约表示的物理含义，并解决先前单表示处理静态虫洞时遗漏的关键谱结构问题。

Method: 采用投影DKP方程分析3×3不可约表示；利用代数对偶性与自洽协变旋度分解；将该结构应用于静态虫洞背景下的径向一阶DKP系统，并结合达布变换研究散射与准正规模性质。

Result: 发现ω²=M²处存在主变量约化所缺失的特殊可归一化束缚态分支；主变量与环形扇区通过达布变换关联；反射/透射概率不变；准正规模在复动量平面上形成两族极点，物理配对为(ω, -ω*)而非全局±ω。

Conclusion: 投影自旋-1 DKP在2+1维中能更完整地刻画粒子手征自由度与拓扑背景耦合效应，揭示出单表示方法无法捕捉的谱精细结构，对虫洞等弯曲时空中的相对论性场论建模具有重要意义。

Abstract: We use the projected spin-1 Duffin--Kemmer--Petiau (DKP) equation in $2+1$ dimensions to clarify the physical content of the irreducible $3\times3$ representations. The projected antisymmetric tensor is algebraically dual to the projected vector, and the two twin representations select conjugate self-dual covariant-curl sectors, which reduce to opposite massive helicities in the flat-space limit. We then apply this structure to the static wormhole problem previously studied with a single irreducible representation. The first-order radial DKP system contains an exceptional normalizable bound-state branch at $ω^2=M^2$ that is absent from the master-variable reduction, whereas the generic master and circular sectors are related by a Darboux transformation. The latter preserves the transmission and reflection probabilities, while the quasinormal modes are organized into two pole families in the complex radial-momentum plane, leading to the physical pairing $(ω,-ω^*)$ rather than a global $\pmω$ prescription.

</details>


### [146] [Confinement--Higgs Complementarity Viewed through Relative Quantum Reference Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19519)
*Kei-Ichi Kondo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过量子参考系（QRF）的相对取向，重新诠释了禁闭-希格斯互补性，并为CDG约化中发现的非局域分离线提供了关系性物理解释，同时验证其与Fradkin-Shenker连续性和Osterwalder-Seiler解析性的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解禁闭与希格斯机制之间的互补关系，尤其在不依赖规范固定的前提下，解释格点模拟中出现的非局域分离线的物理本质。

Method: 基于规范场与标量场各自构建独立的量子参考系（QRF），利用精确的连续和格点恒等式，给出标量-色可观测量的关系性诠释；结合CDG约化、Fradkin-Shenker连续性及Osterwalder-Seiler解析性进行理论分析。

Result: 为CDG约化所得非局域分离线提供了QRF物理解释；证明该分离线与关键解析性与连续性条件相容；发现Wilson型控制可观测量在零plaquette耦合下有精确结果，且不引发此类非局域分离线。

Conclusion: 禁闭-希格斯互补性可统一表述为两个QRF的相对取向；非局域结构源于参考系间的关联，而非规范破缺本身；不同可观测量对相变结构的敏感性存在本质差异。

Abstract: We formulate confinement--Higgs complementarity in terms of the relative orientation between two independently constructed quantum reference frames (QRFs): one derived from the gauge field and the other from the scalar field. Exact continuum and lattice identities give a relational interpretation of the scalar--color observables used in recent gauge-fixing-free $SU(2)$ simulations. In particular, we provide a QRF interpretation of the additional gauge-invariant nonlocal separation line obtained from the Cho--Duan--Ge (CDG) reduction reported in lattice simulations of the radially fixed fundamental $SU(2)$ gauge--scalar model. We explain why such a candidate nonlocal boundary is compatible with Fradkin--Shenker continuity and Osterwalder--Seiler analyticity for the appropriate class of local gauge-invariant observables. Separately, we show that a Wilson-rooted control observable admits an exact results at zero plaquette coupling and that it does not cause such a nonlocal separation line.

</details>


### [147] [Stochastic gauge fixing from the holographic renormalization group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19083)
*Soner Albayrak*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将随机量子化与全息威尔逊重整化之间的字典关系扩展到四维反德西特空间中的杨-米尔斯理论，在树级水平上建立了二者联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立随机量子化与全息威尔逊重整化在非平凡背景（如AdS）下杨-米尔斯理论中的对应关系，深化对量子场论与全息原理之间联系的理解。

Method: 利用Zwanziger的规范固定函数将体空间规范场的径向分量与随机量子化联系起来；采用径向规范实现Parisi-Wu量子化，并将规范模的随机行走解释为纵向体传播子；推导有限截断下的三迹和四迹胶子耦合项。

Result: 得到了AdS中杨-米尔斯理论在有限动量截断下的三迹和四迹胶子耦合的闭式表达式；发现其对横向数据的依赖仅通过一个零模体现，且该零模在平直空间极限下消失。

Conclusion: 证实了随机量子化框架可自然嵌入全息威尔逊重整化方案中，为AdS/CFT中量子化程序提供了新的几何与随机视角，并揭示了截断尺度下规范场耦合的结构特征。

Abstract: We extend the dictionary between stochastic quantization and holographic Wilsonian renormalization to Yang-Mills theory in four-dimensional anti-de Sitter space at tree level. The radial component of the bulk gauge field is Zwanziger's gauge-fixing function; radial gauge is Parisi-Wu quantization, whose random walk of gauge modes is the longitudinal bulk propagator. We obtain the triple- and quadruple-trace couplings of gluons at finite cutoff in closed form. On transverse data, their dependence on the bulk gauge resides in a single zero mode, which drops out of the flat-space limit.

</details>


### [148] [Wilsonian renormalization group flow in QED with the Pauli term](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19778)
*Sorato Nagao,Hiroshi Suzuki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文使用梯度流精确重整化群（GFERG）研究含Pauli项的量子电动力学（QED）的重整化群流，发现大N_f近似下存在一个非平凡不动点，但在有限N_f（如N_f=1）时该不动点消失；Pauli耦合κ_τ在单圈效应下为无关算符，被吸引至与e_τ^3成正比的诱导值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索含Pauli项的QED中是否存在新的非平凡紫外不动点，以理解其可能的渐近安全行为及规范对称性保持机制。

Method: 采用梯度流精确重整化群（GFERG）方法，严格保持规范Ward-Takahashi恒等式，并在大-N_f近似和有限-N_f情形下分析RG流与不动点结构。

Result: 大-N_f下存在e_*=0、κ_*≠0、γ_A=-1的非平凡不动点，具三个相关方向；但有限N_f（尤其N_f=1）时，κ²导致的费米子质量修正消除该不动点；κ_τ为无关算符，流向e_τ^3依赖的诱导值，e_τ²仍呈边缘无关。

Conclusion: 含Pauli项的QED在真实小N_f情形下不支持所发现的大-N_f不动点，其紫外行为由诱导Pauli耦合主导，未呈现渐近安全特征。

Abstract: We study the renormalization group (RG) flow of quantum electrodynamics (QED) with a Pauli term, $κ_τ\Barψσ_{μν}F_{μν}ψ$, by using the gradient flow exact renormalization group (GFERG). In the GFERG, the gauge Ward--Takahashi identity takes the conventional form and is exactly preserved, and the gauge coupling flows only through the photon anomalous dimension~$γ_A$. In the leading large-$N_f$ approximation, the vacuum polarization induced by the Pauli coupling yields a nontrivial fixed point with~$e_*=0$, $κ_*\neq0$, and~$γ_A=-1$. This fixed point has three relevant directions, as does one of the fixed points of the asymptotically safe QED proposed by Gies and Ziebell. At finite~$N_f$, however, the $N_f$-independent one-loop effects on the fermion line, in particular the additive mass renormalization proportional to~$κ^2$, eliminate this fixed point unless $N_f$ is at least several tens; for~$N_f=1$ there is no fixed point at weak Pauli coupling. Along the full flow for~$N_f=1$, $κ_τ$ is irrelevant and is attracted to an induced value~$κ_{\text{ind}}(e_τ)$, which is proportional to~$e_τ^3$ at weak coupling, while $e_τ^2$ is marginally irrelevant as in QED without the Pauli term.

</details>


### [149] [Conditional Principle of Least Action: Gauge Algebra and Noether's Second Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19097)
*V. A. Abakumova,S. L. Lyakhovich*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在受微分方程约束的场类上作用量的条件极值问题，建立了约束方程与受限作用量之间规范对称代数的联系，并据此推广了Noether第二定理及其逆定理，以线性化引力和Cotton方程约束为例进行了说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在微分约束下作用量条件极值问题中，约束方程与作用量之间的规范对称性关系，从而推广Noether第二定理。

Method: 通过分析受微分方程约束的场类上作用量的条件极值，建立约束方程的规范对称代数与受限作用量规范对称代数之间的联系，并由此推导Noether第二定理的扩展形式及其逆定理。

Result: 得到了Noether第二定理及其逆定理的推广形式，并在线性化引力及Cotton方程约束下验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 约束方程与受限作用量的规范对称代数之间存在深刻联系，该联系为推广Noether第二定理提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We consider the problem of a conditional extremum of an action within a class of fields constrained by differential equations. For this setup, we establish the interconnection between the gauge symmetry algebra of the equations constraining the admissible fields and that of the action restricted to this class of fields. Based on this relation between the two gauge algebras, we derive an extension of Noether's second theorem and its converse. The general method is illustrated by the example of linearized gravity with the Einstein-Hilbert action and the class of admissible metrics restricted by the Cotton equation.

</details>


### [150] [On reflection positivity of lattice chiral fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20084)
*Zhiyao Lu,Shu-Heng Shao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文指出重叠Weyl费米子在有限格点间距下不满足反射正定性，导致无法通过标准Osterwalder-Schrader重构得到具有自伴哈密顿量的希尔伯特空间；二维情形下其格点作用量在边界存在时非定域，与连续场论的引力反常一致；而重叠Dirac费米子虽反射正定，但其轴向对称性存在紧致与非紧致的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究格点规范理论中重叠Weyl费米子的数学基础（特别是反射正定性）及其与连续极限物理（如引力反常）的联系，澄清其是否适合作为手征规范理论的严格构造基础。

Method: 运用Osterwalder-Schrader公理框架分析格点费米子体系的反射正定性；结合边界行为与非定域性论证，联系连续理论中的引力反常；对比分析重叠Dirac费米子的轴向对称性结构及Onsager代数性质。

Result: 证明重叠Weyl费米子在2D和4D有限格距下不满足反射正定性；2D下其含边界作用量必然非定域；重叠Dirac费米子虽反射正定，但其轴向对称性无法同时满足紧致性与反射不变性。

Conclusion: 重叠Weyl费米子不适合作为严格手征格点规范理论的基础自由场，其根本障碍源于离散化与拓扑反常之间的深层冲突；需重新审视格点手征性实现的数学前提。

Abstract: We point out that the overlap Weyl fermion, the spacetime lattice realization of a Weyl fermion underlying Lüscher's construction of lattice chiral gauge theories, is not reflection positive in two and four dimensions at finite lattice spacing. Consequently, the standard Osterwalder--Schrader reconstruction does not yield a Hilbert space with a self-adjoint Hamiltonian. In two dimensions, we further show that the overlap Weyl fermion cannot be cut open: the lattice action becomes nonlocal in the presence of a boundary, as expected from the gravitational anomaly of the continuum field theory. By contrast, the overlap Dirac fermion is known to be reflection positive. However, its compact axial symmetry is not invariant under the reflection, whereas a reflection-invariant axial symmetry is noncompact, and both are part of an Onsager algebra.

</details>


### [151] [Classification of Conformal Ising/Tricritical Ising Interfaces Preserving $\mathcal{W}_3^{\mathbb{Z}_2}$ Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19129)
*Bufan Zheng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文分类了保持$\mathcal{W}_3^{\mathbb{Z}_2}$对称性的Ising与三临界Ising模型之间的共形界面，利用折叠技巧和Cardy条件确定了24个基本边界态，并显式构造了其中6个破缺Virasoro对称性的界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解不同临界模型间共形界面的分类及其对称性保持/破缺行为，特别是与RG流和拓扑缺陷相关的物理结构。

Method: 采用折叠技巧将界面问题转化为边界态问题，结合Cardy相容性条件与NIM-rep分析，显式构造非因子化界面并计算其环面配分函数。

Result: 得到24个基本边界态：18个为因子化（保持全部乘积Virasoro对称性），6个为非因子化（破缺该对称性）；后者可由拓扑缺陷线修饰相互关联，其一等价于Gaiotto提出的RG域壁。

Conclusion: 在$\mathcal{W}_3^{\mathbb{Z}_2}$对称性约束下，Ising与三临界Ising模型间的共形界面具有明确的代数分类，非因子化界面揭示了RG流与拓扑缺陷之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We classify conformal interfaces between the Ising and tricritical Ising models that preserve the chiral algebra $\mathcal{W}_3^{\mathbb{Z}_2}$. Using the folding trick, we formulate the problem in terms of boundary states satisfying the Cardy condition and determine the unique NIM-rep compatible with the bulk partition function. We obtain a complete set of 24 fundamental boundary states, of which 18 correspond to factorized interfaces preserving the full product Virasoro symmetry, while the remaining six break this symmetry. We construct these six interfaces explicitly and determine their annulus partition functions. They are related by topological defect line dressings, and one coincides with the RG domain wall proposed by Gaiotto.

</details>


### [152] [Large-$N$ expansion in SU($N$) Yang-Mills-like theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19144)
*Marco Bochicchio,Mauro Papinutto,Francesco Scardino*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了SU(N)规范理论大N展开的拓扑结构，指出't Hooft光滑拓扑在SU(N)和重整化U(N)理论中通常是不完备的，需引入奇异的'捏缩闭曲面'来补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: U(N)理论的大N展开已有成熟拓扑解释（按黎曼曲面亏格分类），但SU(N)理论因U(1)因子被移除而存在微妙差异，需厘清其大N展开的完整拓扑结构。

Method: 通过对比U(N)与SU(N)理论中U(1)因子的解耦行为，分析大N极限下费曼图的拓扑分类，并将SU(N)中出现的额外拓扑解释为奇异的捏缩闭曲面。

Result: 发现SU(N)及重整化U(N) YM类理论的大N展开中，传统't Hooft光滑拓扑不完整；对高扭度准部分子算符关联函数，需引入捏缩闭曲面（如之前发现的捏缩环面的推广）作为补充拓扑。

Conclusion: SU(N)规范理论的大N展开必须包含奇异拓扑（捏缩曲面），这反映了SU(N)与U(N)理论在大N极限下的本质差异，且该现象在重整化后依然存在。

Abstract: It has been known for fifty years that bare U($N$) pure Yang-Mills (YM) theory admits the large-$N$ 't Hooft expansion for the $n$-point connected correlators of gauge-invariant single-trace operators, where the corresponding Feynman diagrams are topologically classified by the sum over the genus $g$ of $n$-punctured smooth orientable closed Riemann surfaces. In fact, 't Hooft expansion extends verbatim to U($N$) YM-like theories whose Lagrangians only involve fields valued in the U($N$) Lie algebra - including supersymmetric (SUSY) extensions of pure YM theory such as $\mathcal{N}=1,4$ SUSY YM. The aim of the present paper is to systematically work out the topology of the large-$N$ expansion in SU($N$) - as opposed to U($N$) - YM-like theories, where a number of subtleties inevitably arise. Indeed, since the U($1$) inside the U($N$) YM-like theories decouples, the large-$N$ U($N$) and SU($N$) expansions should either essentially coincide or intrinsically differ - in the latter case necessarily by extra - possibly singular - topologies, as the 't Hooft expansion in U($N$) YM-like theories already exhausts all the smooth topologies. In fact, we demonstrate that 't Hooft smooth topologies are actually generically incomplete in the large-$N$ expansion of both the SU($N$) and renormalized U($N$) YM-like theories, where renormalization inevitably separates the SU($N$) sector that is interacting from the U($1$) sector that is free. Specifically, we interpret the extra topologies that arise in SU($N$) theories for correlators of higher-twist quasi-partonic operators as singular pinched closed surfaces, thus extending our previous investigation of correlators of twist-$2$ operators for which extra pinched tori arise.

</details>


### [153] [The $\mathbb{Z}_2$ twist line defect of the 3D Ising CFT on the fuzzy sphere from multi-target density matrix renormalization group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19233)
*Jing-Hao Jin,Yang Qi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合模糊球正则化与多目标DMRG算法的新方法，用于高精度研究三维Ising CFT中的Z2扭曲线缺陷，显著超越了传统精确对角化的系统尺寸限制，并实现了受控的连续极限外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模糊球正则化虽适用于三维共形场论研究，但此前对共形缺陷的研究仅限于小系统的精确对角化，缺乏对更大系统和更高精度的分析手段。

Method: 在模糊球上通过施加Z2奇费米子味的反对称边界条件实现Z2扭曲线缺陷，并采用多目标密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）算法，在统一的矩阵乘积态基下同时优化各对称扇区的多个低能态。

Result: 成功获得远超精确对角化能力的缺陷谱（最大2s+1=25），验证了自旋-宇称选择律，识别出有限尺寸修正并实现可控连续极限外推，提升了多个缺陷原初算符维度的精度，并新分辨出四个高激发原初算符。

Conclusion: 模糊球结合多目标DMRG被确立为研究共形缺陷的高精度通用框架。

Abstract: The fuzzy-sphere regularization provides a powerful framework for studying three-dimensional (3D) conformal field theories (CFTs) through radial quantization, but conformal defects have so far been studied with it only by exact diagonalization (ED) of small systems. In this work we realize the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ twist line defect of the 3D Ising CFT on the fuzzy sphere by imposing antiperiodic boundary conditions on the $\mathbb{Z}_2$-odd fermion flavor, and solve it with a multi-target density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) algorithm that optimizes several low-lying states of each symmetry sector in a common matrix-product basis. This gives the defect spectrum at system sizes well beyond the reach of ED (up to $2s+1=25$, or $49$ spinless orbitals). The spectrum obeys the spin-parity selection rule of the twist line and organizes into conformal multiplets along the line. Using the displacement operator as an intrinsic ruler, its protected descendant as a test, and the bulk stress tensor as an independent cross-check, we identify the leading finite-size corrections and obtain controlled continuum extrapolations, which sharpen the dimensions of several known defect primaries and resolve four higher-lying ones. This study establishes the fuzzy sphere combined with multi-target DMRG as a framework for precision studies of conformal defects.

</details>


### [154] [Triple Exchange at the Cosmological Collider in Closed Form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20356)
*Taushif Ahmed,Sumit Banik,Syed Mehedi Hasan,Andreas Rapakoulias*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用费曼积分方法，首次得到了准单场三重交换的完整形状的闭式解，并发现Planck分析中使用的模板在振幅和质量估计上存在偏差，新模板能更准确地恢复这些参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子质量接近哈勃尺度时会在原初双谱中留下特征模式，将暴胀转化为宇宙学对撞机。对于轻场，该信号最大，但其完整形状尚无闭式解。

Method: 使用费曼积分方法，以多重对数函数展开的方式，在无质量与共形耦合极限附近获得精确动量的闭式结果。

Result: 发现Planck分析中使用的模板高估振幅达1.8倍并低估质量；新模板可准确恢复振幅与质量；还计算了坍缩四谱，并结合双谱在弱混合下更严格限制自耦合。

Conclusion: 本文提供了准单场三重交换的首个精确闭式解，改进了CMB和星系巡天数据分析所需的模板，提升了对粒子物理参数的约束能力。

Abstract: Particles with masses near the Hubble scale leave a characteristic pattern in the primordial bispectrum, turning inflation into a cosmological collider. For light fields, where this signal is largest, no closed form is known for the full shape of the quasi-single-field triple exchange. Using Feynman-integral methods, we obtain the first closed-form results, exact in the momenta, as expansions in terms of multiple polylogarithms around the massless and conformally coupled limits. We show that the template used in Planck analyses overestimates the amplitude by up to a factor of 1.8 and underestimates the mass, whereas our new template recovers both, as needed for current and upcoming CMB and galaxy surveys. We also compute the collapsed trispectrum and, combining it with the bispectrum, find that at weak mixing it bounds the self-coupling more tightly.

</details>


### [155] [Holographic Entanglement after a Global Quench in Noncommutative Yang-Mills Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19307)
*Elahe Hassanpour,Komeil Babaei Velni,M. Reza Mohammadi Mozaffar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维N=4非对易杨-米尔斯理论中全局淬火后全息纠缠熵和混合态关联的时间演化，发现不同尺度下存在对数、线性和二次增长阶段，并揭示了蝴蝶速度与纠缠速度在演化中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非对易场论中量子纠缠在非平衡动力学下的演化行为，特别是其与全息对偶及混沌传播（如蝴蝶效应）的联系。

Method: 利用Vaidya几何建模全局淬火过程，结合全息方法计算条形区域的纠缠熵、互信息及纠缠楔截面，分析不同宽度与非对易尺度关系下的早期增长行为与平衡过程。

Result: 发现条形区域纠缠熵存在对数、线性（中间）、二次三种早期增长模式；线性阶段的纠缠速度随非对易性增强；蝴蝶速度调控极值面连续演化；互信息消去对数发散项；纠缠楔截面在线性阶段表现出相同纠缠速度。

Conclusion: 非对易性显著改变淬火后纠缠动力学特征，引入新的标度行为和速度，且纠缠结构演化受蝴蝶速度与几何竞争共同支配。

Abstract: The time evolution of holographic entanglement entropy and mixed-state correlations following a global quench in four-dimensional N=4 noncommutative Yang-Mills theory is investigated using a Vaidya geometry. For strip-shaped regions extended along a noncommutative direction, two distinct early-time growth regimes emerge, depending on the relation between the shell-crossing and noncommutative scales: logarithmic growth, with a coefficient proportional to the injected energy density and the square of the noncommutative scale, followed by quadratic growth. For sufficiently wide strips, an intermediate linear-growth regime appears, governed by an entanglement velocity that increases with noncommutativity. The approach to equilibrium is also examined, revealing the role of the butterfly velocity in the continuous evolution of extremal surfaces, while competing branches can modify the physical saturation time. For two disjoint strips, the leading width-independent logarithmic contribution cancels in the mutual information, whereas the entanglement wedge cross section exhibits an intermediate linear-growth regime governed by the same entanglement velocity, provided the connected entanglement wedge remains dominant.

</details>


### [156] [Thermal deep inelastic scattering, universality and complex geodesics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21127)
*Andrei Parnachev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用全息对偶方法研究了强耦合量子场论中有限温度下深度非弹性散射（DIS）的动力学，揭示了真空与热效应在大虚度下的普适性行为及其与共形对撞机界限的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强耦合量子场论在有限温度下深度非弹性散射中的响应函数行为，特别是真空主导与热主导物理之间的过渡机制。

Method: 采用全息对偶（AdS/CFT）方法，分析强耦合CFT中电流两点函数在大动量转移（大虚度）和有限温度下的行为，并考察其近光锥极限。

Result: 发现响应函数具有高度普适性：其真空-热交叉行为由依赖于通道的系数控制，该系数也决定共形对撞机界限；当任一界限饱和时，响应函数在近光锥极限下退化为纯真空形式；低温吸收指数由近光锥几何中的复测地线传播决定。

Conclusion: DIS中真空与热效应的竞争展现出深刻普适性，全息方法为理解强耦合热场论中的高能散射提供了新视角，并将热响应、共形界限与几何结构统一起来。

Abstract: In deep inelastic scattering (DIS), a lepton scatters off a target hadron whose structure is probed by an exchanged photon with large spacelike virtuality. We consider two-point functions of currents in strongly interacting quantum field theories at finite temperature in this kinematic regime and use a holographic description to compute them. At large virtuality, the crossover from vacuum-dominated to thermally dominated physics corresponds to a transition from exponentially suppressed absorption to power-law behavior of the response functions. This regime is related to a particular near-lightcone limit of thermal CFT correlators. We show that the response functions exhibit a large degree of universality: the dependence on the three-point couplings of currents to the stress tensor enters through a channel-dependent coefficient multiplying the kinematic parameter that controls the relative size of vacuum and thermal effects. The same coefficient controls conformal collider bounds; when either bound is saturated, the corresponding response function takes its vacuum form in the near-lightcone limit. Furthermore, the absorption exponent in the low-temperature regime is related to a complex geodesic propagating in the near-lightcone geometry.

</details>


### [157] [On the Spontaneous symmetry breaking of Conformal Gravity to Weyl and Einstein Gravities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19338)
*Partha Nandi,Gerasimos Kouniatalis,Stelios Stefas,George Zoupanos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在SO(2,4)规范引力框架下研究特殊共形联络是否仍为辅助场，并证明在无源投影分支上可将其代数消去，从而自然导出四维Weyl平方作用量（至Euler密度）；进一步表明在完整Yang-Mills-Higgs理论中该联络仍为辅助场，其动力学不产生独立动能项；最后分析了de Sitter背景下的线性谱，发现质量为零和部分质量（m²=2Λ/3）的自旋-2模，并探讨了由反对称标量6重态诱发的对称性破缺如何连接Weyl引力与爱因斯坦引力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在自发破缺共形规范理论升级为动力学Yang-Mills-Higgs理论后，特殊共形联络是否仍保持辅助场性质，以及何种条件下能自然导出Weyl引力。

Method: 基于SO(2,4)规范理论框架，保留特殊共形联络为独立场，分析其代数运动方程；结合自发对称破缺机制（通过15重态和6重态），推导Weyl作用量及后续向爱因斯坦引力的过渡；在线性化水平上分析de Sitter背景下的谱结构。

Result: 1）在无源投影分支上，特殊共形联络由Schouten张量代数确定，消去后直接得到Weyl²作用量（含Euler密度）；2）在完整YM-Higgs理论中，因so(2,4)不变双线性形式结构，该联络仍为辅助场，无独立动能项；3）线性谱含质量为零和部分质量（m²=2Λ/3）的自旋-2模式；4）反对称6重态破缺可实现Weyl引力到爱因斯坦引力的过渡。

Conclusion: 特殊共形联络在所研究的共形规范引力框架中始终是辅助场，其代数消去自然导出Weyl引力；完整YM-Higgs拓展不改变其辅助性，但仅在无源分支保证精确Weyl约化；该框架为理解共形引力、Weyl引力与爱因斯坦引力之间的层级关系提供了统一规范视角。

Abstract: Does the special-conformal connection remain an auxiliary field when a spontaneously broken conformal gauge theory is promoted to a fully dynamical Yang-Mills-Higgs theory, and under what conditions does its elimination yield Weyl gravity? We address these questions within an $\mathrm{SO}(2,4)$ gauge-theoretic formulation of gravity. Revisiting the gravitational sector selected by spontaneous symmetry breaking through an adjoint $15$-plet, we retain the special-conformal connection as an independent field and demonstrate that, on a source-free projected branch, its algebraic equation of motion determines it in terms of the Schouten tensor. Eliminating this connection yields the four-dimensional Weyl-squared action, up to the Euler density, without imposing the Schouten relation as an additional geometrical constraint. We then extend the analysis to the standard local Yang-Mills-Higgs theory and show that the special-conformal connection remains auxiliary, owing to the structure of the invariant bilinear form of $\mathfrak{so}(2,4)$, which prevents the appearance of an independent kinetic operator. The additional gauge and scalar sectors can modify its algebraic solution, so the exact Weyl reduction is restricted to the source-free branch. We further investigate the linearised spectrum of the reduced Weyl theory around de Sitter spacetime, identifying massless and partially massless spin-2 branches, with the latter occurring at $m_{\mathrm{PM}}^2=2Λ/3$. Finally, we discuss the subsequent symmetry breaking induced by an antisymmetric scalar $6$-plet and its role in connecting the Weyl-gravity phase to Einstein gravity.

</details>


### [158] [The Goldstone--Wilczek Charge on the Rim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19464)
*Laurence A. Jacobs*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究Kaplan-Sen晶格圆盘中磁通Φ变化导致的边缘电荷转移现象，发现电荷跃变并非由边缘算符负本征值个数变化引起，而是通过其极值本征值穿越无穷大（即出现简单极点）实现，并给出该跃变与本征值符号变化之间的定量关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解拓扑系统中磁通调制下电荷在边界上的响应机制，特别是边缘电荷跃变的微观起源及其在边界有效哈密顿量（rim算符）中的体现。

Method: 理论分析结合数值模拟：基于Goldstone-Wilczek公式和谱不对称性η定义边缘电荷；引入rim算符（盘体哈密顿量对边界位点的Schur补）；解析推导极值本征值穿越无穷大与电荷跃变的关系；在不同半径（R=11,19,34）圆盘上进行数值验证。

Result: 发现当磁通Φ从0增至π时，边缘电荷Q_edge发生-1的跃变；该跃变对应rim算符极值本征值λ_ext穿过无穷大，满足ΔQ_edge = ½Δsign λ_ext；该极点的留数可分解为内边缘模与rim耦合强度及该模能级斜率倒数的乘积，且随半径线性增长。

Conclusion: rim算符的极值本征值穿越无穷大是磁通诱导电荷转移的关键指标，揭示了边界有效理论中非平凡拓扑响应的新机制，超越了传统依赖负本征值计数的理解框架。

Abstract: A solenoid flux $Φ=2πα$ threaded through the center of the Kaplan--Sen lattice disk binds a ground-state charge $Q_{\rm core}=α-\mathrm{round}(α)$, and the edge carries exactly the opposite charge. The edge charge is the Goldstone--Wilczek charge of the edge ladder, $Q_{\rm edge}=-\tfrac12η$, with $η$ its spectral asymmetry. At $Φ=π$ one unit of charge crosses the disk. We ask how the rim operator, the Schur complement of the disk Hamiltonian on its boundary sites, registers this transfer. Its count of negative eigenvalues never changes over the flux cycle. Instead, the unit of charge enters the rim through a simple pole: the extremal eigenvalue of the rim operator passes through infinity in the same flux interval as the charge jump, with $ΔQ_{\rm edge}=\tfrac12Δ\,\mathrm{sign}\,λ_{\rm ext}$. The residue factorizes into the coupling of the inner edge mode to the rim and the inverse slope of that mode's level, and grows linearly with the radius. All statements are checked numerically on disks of radius $R=11$, $19$ and $34$.

</details>


### [159] [Local field shifts and the Gribov-Zwanziger horizon functional](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19513)
*Daniel Guimaraes Tedesco*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用光滑紧支集轮廓对Gribov-Zwanziger作用量进行Maggiore-Schaden型重中心化，证明在固定背景和有限截断下该变换保持原始Horizon泛函且不依赖于轮廓；进一步分析了其在Gribov边界附近及非自伴压缩下的响应行为，并验证了传播子结构的保持性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Gribov拷贝问题及Gribov-Zwanziger框架中Horizon条件对轮廓选择的敏感性问题，提升理论在非微扰QCD中的可靠性。

Method: 采用光滑紧支集轮廓进行Maggiore-Schaden型重中心化，结合 coercivity 和围道假设，在固定背景与有限截断下分析Horizon泛函不变性；扩展至规范场积分需额外支配假设；通过最低Faddeev-Popov本征值及其源权重分析Gribov边界附近的响应；推广至非自伴压缩以区分源‘黑暗’与截断引起的‘屏蔽’；并检验复合算符插入下的高斯与预解式结构保持性。

Result: 证明重中心化变换在给定条件下严格保持Horizon泛函，无轮廓残留依赖；揭示Gribov边界附近响应由最低FP本征值及其源权重共同决定；成功分离非自伴情形下的源黑暗与截断屏蔽效应；运输复合插入后仍保持正则化高斯与预解式结构；树级自由空间检验复现RGZ传播子的辅助质量分母形式。

Conclusion: 该重中心化方法增强了Gribov-Zwanziger框架的稳健性与普适性，为理解胶子传播子红外行为及凝聚态动力学提供了更可靠的路径，但未确定凝聚态的动力学演化。

Abstract: We study a Maggiore-Schaden-like re-centering of the Gribov-Zwanziger action using a smooth compactly supported profile. At fixed background and finite cutoff, under the stated coercivity and contour hypotheses, this transformation preserves the original horizon functional with no residual dependence on the profile. The extension to gauge-field integration requires additional domination assumptions. Near a self-adjoint first Gribov horizon, the response depends on both the lowest Faddeev-Popov eigenvalue and its source weight. We extend this visibility analysis to non-self-adjoint compressions, separating source darkness from truncation-induced shielding, and recall continuum transverse $SU(2)$ examples. Transporting the composite insertion preserves the regulated Gaussian and resolvent structure. A separate free-space tree-level check reproduces the auxiliary-mass denominator of the RGZ propagator, without determining condensate dynamics

</details>


### [160] [Renormalization Group Flow from 4d Chern-Simons Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19517)
*Meer Ashwinkumar,Masahito Yamazaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从四维Chern-Simons理论出发，通过费曼图计算和BV形式体系，首次从第一性原理推导了由其表面缺陷诱导的二维可积量子场论（如PCM）的一圈重整化群流，并复现了PCM的渐近自由β函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解二维可积QFT的RG流如何从更高维统一框架（四维Chern-Simons理论）中自然涌现，并建立表面缺陷类型（序/乱序）与不同可积模型RG行为的对应。

Method: 基于四维Chern-Simons理论在Σ×C上的构造，分别处理序表面缺陷（给出仿射Kac-Moody对称性驱动的RG流）和乱序表面缺陷（用于实现PCM），采用标准一圈费曼图计算与BV形式体系下的类解析挠率计算两种互补方法。

Result: 成功导出序缺陷诱导的二维QFT的一圈RG流；首次在四维框架内严格导出无WZ项PCM的一圈RG流，复现其已知的渐近自由β函数。

Conclusion: 四维Chern-Simons理论结合表面缺陷不仅是构造二维可积QFT的有效几何方法，亦能自洽地描述其量子效应（如RG流），为统一理解可积性与重整化提供了新视角。

Abstract: The four-dimensional Chern-Simons theory coupled with surface defects is known to realize two-dimensional integrable quantum field theories (QFTs). We discuss the renormalization group (RG) flow of such two-dimensional integrable QFTs from first-principles Feynman diagram computations intrinsically in the four-dimensional framework. We first derive the one-loop RG flow of two-dimensional QFTs realized by the four-dimensional Chern-Simons theory on $Σ\times C$ coupled with the order surface defects supported on $Σ$. Here, the affine Kac-Moody symmetry of the defects underlies the one-loop RG flow. We then derive the one-loop RG flow of the principal chiral model (PCM) without Wess-Zumino term, which is realized using disorder surface defects, within the Batalin-Vilkovisky formalism. We obtain the nontrivial one-loop RG flow of the PCM, reproducing its known asymptotically-free beta function, using two complementary methods: by explicitly evaluating one-loop Feynman amplitudes, and by computing an analogue of analytic torsion for four-dimensional Chern-Simons theory.

</details>


### [161] [A proof of Nakamura's conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19558)
*Arsen R. Melikyan,Aleksandr Pinzul*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了Nakamura关于2D Toda模型与Ernst方程解之间关系的猜想，利用了作者先前工作中得到的Pfaffian解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Nakamura提出的关于2D Toda模型和Ernst方程解之间关系的猜想。

Method: 基于先前工作中获得的Pfaffian解，进行理论推导与证明。

Result: 成功证明了Nakamura猜想，建立了2D Toda模型与Ernst方程解之间的明确联系。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解可积系统与广义相对论中重要方程（如Ernst方程）之间的深层联系提供了新途径。

Abstract: We prove Nakamura's conjecture on the relation between the 2D Toda model and solutions of the Ernst equation, using the Pfaffian solutions obtained in our previous work arXiv:2610.27420.

</details>


### [162] [Negative and Positive Saddle-Selection Ambiguities in the HTEE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19562)
*H. Ebrahim,M. Ali-Akbari,K. Taheranvarian*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了全息类时纠缠熵（HTEE）中复极值面计算所导致的鞍点选择模糊性问题，发现在超缩放违反Lifshitz背景下的强耦合场论中，最小实部判据与真空约化判据在多数情况下一致，但在第一偶然零点处出现分歧；该模糊性以‘负族’和‘正族’两种形式存在，且后者在共形不变的3+1维情形下依然存在，表明该模糊性是复几何方法的普遍特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决全息类时纠缠熵（HTEE）计算中因复极值面存在多个鞍点而导致的鞍点选择模糊性问题，特别是在破坏共形对称性和洛伦兹不变性的超缩放违反Lifshitz背景下。

Method: 在2+1维和3+1维对偶场论中，推导真空连通与真空非连通复极值面所对应的HTEE表达式s_{v.c.}和s_{v.d.}，并比较其实部，分析最小实部判据与真空约化判据的一致性与分歧。

Result: 两个判据在除第一偶然零点外的所有区域一致；模糊性分为负族（Re(s_{v.c.})=0, Re(s_{v.d.})<0）和正族（Re(s_{v.c.})>0, Re(s_{v.d.})=0）；正族模糊性存在于共形不变的3+1维情形，说明该模糊性具有普适性。

Conclusion: 鞍点选择模糊性并非特定模型的异常现象，而是复几何方法在计算HTEE时的固有且普遍特征。

Abstract: The holographic timelike entanglement entropy (HTEE), computed via complexified extremal surfaces, suffers from a saddle-selection ambiguity: for sufficiently small time intervals (\(Δt\to0\)), both a vacuum-connected and a vacuum-disconnected complex extremal surface contribute, and the minimal-real-part criterion and the vacuum-reduction criterion can favor different saddles. We study this ambiguity in strongly coupled field theories whose dual geometries are hyperscaling-violating Lifshitz backgrounds, in which conformal symmetry and Lorentz invariance are broken by the Lifshitz exponent and the hyperscaling-violation parameter. Working in \(2+1\)- and \(3+1\)-dimensional dual field theories, we derive general expressions for the HTEE in the vacuum-connected and vacuum-disconnected saddles, \(s_{v.c.}\) and \(s_{v.d.}\), respectively, and compare their real parts. We find that the two criteria agree everywhere except at the first accidental zero. The ambiguity appears in two distinct forms. In the \emph{negative} family, \(\operatorname{Re}(s_{v.c.})=0\) and \(\operatorname{Re}(s_{v.d.})<0\), whereas in the \emph{positive} family, \(\operatorname{Re}(s_{v.c.})>0\) and \(\operatorname{Re}(s_{v.d.})=0\). The latter shows that the ambiguity survives even in the conformal \(3+1\)-dimensional case. It therefore seems that the saddle-selection ambiguity is a generic feature of the complexified-geometry prescription.

</details>


### [163] [Entanglement and Charge Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19674)
*Tom Shachar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用复本技巧计算了两个不相交区间上的熵序参量，研究其在全局对称性下对电荷-反电荷缠绕子所携带互信息的度量，并通过扭曲配分函数在复本黎曼面上的平均，得到了自由费米子和紧致玻色子（U(1)及其Z_N子群）的精确解；进一步对非收缩闭合曲线族取平均，得到互补电荷测量中因移除缠绕子导致的Holevo信息损失量；该量对玻色子为零，而对狄拉克费米子则等于费米子奇偶性规范前后理论互信息之差；最后将分析推广至非阿贝尔对称性，借助鞍点展开得到近邻区间极限及大量费米子（N_F≫1）下的普适行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子多体系统中对称性与纠缠结构之间的深层联系，特别是电荷-反电荷缠绕子在子系统间传递的互信息及其对信息提取的影响。

Method: 采用复本方法，构建扭曲配分函数在复本黎曼面上的平均框架；对自由费米子和紧致玻色子进行精确求解；引入对非收缩闭合曲线的双重平均以提取Holevo信息损失；并借助鞍点近似处理非阿贝尔对称性情形。

Result: 获得U(1)及Z_N对称下自由费米子与紧致玻色子的熵序参量精确解；发现Holevo信息损失量在玻色子中为零、在狄拉克费米子中恒等于规范前后互信息之差；推导出非阿贝尔情形下近邻极限和大N_F极限的普适行为。

Conclusion: 熵序参量能有效刻画对称性保护的纠缠结构，其与Holevo信息损失的关联揭示了对称性测量与信息提取之间的本质限制；结果表明玻色子与费米子在对称性纠缠响应上存在根本差异。

Abstract: We compute the entropic order parameter introduced in (1905.10487) using the replica trick for two disjoint intervals. For a global symmetry, this quantity measures the mutual information carried by charge-anticharge intertwiners. We formulate the problem in terms of averages over twisted partition functions on replica Riemann surfaces, and obtain exact solutions for free fermions and compact bosons, for $U(1)$ symmetry and its discrete $\mathbb Z_N$ subgroups. We show that further averaging over both families of non-contractible cycles gives access to an additional quantity, which is the loss of Holevo information associated with complementary-charge measurements that occurs upon removing the intertwiners. For the boson, this quantity vanishes, whereas for Dirac fermions it equals the difference between the mutual information of the fermion-parity-gauged and ungauged theories, independently of the choice of even $\mathbb Z_N$ subgroup. Finally, we extend the analysis to non-Abelian symmetries using saddle expansions, deriving universal behavior in the nearly touching intervals limit and for a large number of fermions $N_F\gg1$.

</details>


### [164] [Algebraic instantons and non-Gaussian vacuum in quantum mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19761)
*Rahim Aziz,Hao Geng,Thomas Schaefer,Mithat Ünsal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在势能为非谐振子形式（如V(x)=16λsin⁴(x/2)）的圆周上粒子的瞬子行为，发现其与标准谐振子情形有本质区别：真空无能隙、瞬子代数衰减、零模位于连续谱阈值、单圈行列式红外发散；量子效应动态生成能隙Eq∝ℏ^{4/3}λ^{1/3}，瞬子嵌入该动力学能隙真空中；通过匹配瞬子轨迹与真空路径积分，精确计算单瞬子振幅及其ℏ^{4/3}标度的前因子，并验证其与数值谱高度吻合；经典瞬子间相互作用按T^{-3}衰减，而真实物理衰减由动力学能隙决定，呈指数形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准瞬子计算依赖于势能具有谐振子型极小值，当真空为非谐（如V∝x^{2n}, n≥2）时，传统方法失效，需发展新方法刻画此类系统的非微扰结构。

Method: 针对圆周上V(x)=16λsin⁴(x/2)模型，利用已知闭式瞬子解与涨落算符，结合路径积分真空部分匹配、中间态求和、红外发散处理及数值谱比对等技术，分析瞬子性质、能隙生成机制与相互作用修正。

Result: 发现代数瞬子、阈值零模、红外发散行列式；量子生成能隙E_q∝ℏ^{4/3}λ^{1/3}；单瞬子振幅前因子为ℏ^{4/3}标度，与数值结果高度一致；瞬子间相互作用由经典T^{-3}衰减修正为由动力学能隙主导的指数衰减。

Conclusion: 非谐真空下瞬子物理需超越标准半经典框架，量子动力学可自发产生能隙并重塑瞬子结构与相互作用，该机制类比于Yang-Mills理论中的质量生成，揭示了量子力学中深层的非微扰自组织现象。

Abstract: The standard instanton calculus in quantum mechanics is based on harmonic minima in the potential. When the perturbative vacuum is anharmonic, $V\simeqλx^{2n}$ with $n\ge2$, the standard construction fails. We study this problem for a particle on a circle and subject to a potential $V(x)=16λ\sin^4(x/2)$. The instanton and its fluctuation operator are known in closed form, but they differ qualitatively from the familiar ones. Because $V''=0$ at the minimum, the classical vacuum is gapless, the instanton decays algebraically (an algebraic instanton), the zero mode is located at the threshold of a continuous spectrum, and the one-loop determinant is infrared divergent. Quantum mechanics generates a non-perturbative gap $E_q\propto\hbar^{4/3}λ^{1/3}$, and the instanton is embedded in a vacuum that is dynamically gapped, similar to what happens in Yang--Mills theory. We show that the one-instanton amplitude can be computed by matching the instanton trajectory to the exact vacuum part of the path integral. The resulting prefactor scales as $\hbar^{4/3}$, and this expression agrees with the numerical spectrum to a very high accuracy. The classical interaction between instantons decays as $T^{-3}$, whereas the correct result is an exponential decay controlled by the dynamically generated gap, beyond the quantum time scale. We provide an alternative derivation of this result based on a sum over intermediate states.

</details>


### [165] [On $ι$Quiver (BPS) Algebras and (Strict) Partitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19856)
*Jiakang Bao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了与仿射A型quiver BPS代数相关的split型ι-quiv er代数，旨在构造可能源于带定向面的D-膜系统（如C×C²/Zₙ）的扭曲仿射Yangian和椭圆ι-量子环面代数，并探讨其表示论与组合结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建源于带orientifold的D-膜系统的扭曲仿射Yangian和椭圆ι-量子环面代数，以统一描述相关BPS物理与代数结构。

Method: 通过分析split型ι-quiver代数的代数性质（如极小生成元表示、coideal结构），结合quiver理论中的orientifold作用，并研究其具有组合基（如彩色严格/对称分拆）的表示。

Result: 给出了ι-quiver代数在有理情形下的极小表示及coideal结构；识别出n=1,2时特定参数极限下由（彩色）严格/对称分拆标记的表示，并恢复已知的BPS计数结果。

Conclusion: split型ι-quiver代数为理解带orientifold的仿射quiver BPS物理提供了代数框架，其组合表示揭示了与分拆理论及BPS态计数的深刻联系。

Abstract: We consider the $ι$quiver algebras of split type associated with the quiver BPS algebras of affine type A. They are supposed to give twisted affine Yangians and (elliptic) $ι$quantum toroidal algebras that could possibly arise from D-branes on $\mathbb{C}\times\mathbb{C}^2/\mathbb{Z}_n$ with orientifolds. We mention some properties of these algebras, such as the minimalistic presentations in the rational cases and the coideal structures. We also investigate how one might identify these algebras from the quiver theories with orientifold actions. Moreover, we discuss some representations with combinatorial bases of these algebras. In particular, the algebras with $n=1,2$ in certain limits of the parameters have representations labelled by (coloured) strict/symmetric partitions, which in the case of $n=1$ recovers the known BPS counting of certain theories.

</details>


### [166] [Towards a foundation model for Type IIB flux vacua](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19950)
*Sven Krippendorf,Magdalena Larfors,Zhimei Liu,Andreas Schachner,Moritz Walden*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了使用生成模型（如Transformer和扩散模型）来采样和定向搜索Type IIB弦论中不同Calabi-Yau流形上的通量真空，通过分阶段训练（预训练+强化学习微调）及跨几何迁移学习，验证了其在多流形间建模真空分布、提升目标性质搜索效率与知识迁移的可行性，为构建Type IIB通量景观基础模型奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Type IIB通量景观的系统刻画亟需可扩展的真空构造方法，传统枚举或优化难以应对高维、多几何复杂性。

Method: 采用Transformer与扩散模型生成离散化通量及近似复结构模和轴子-稀子场值；引入分阶段训练：先预训练建模真空分布，再用强化学习后训练实现物理性质（如低超对称破缺能标）导向采样；并探索小h^{1,2}几何上习得结构向大h^{1,2}几何迁移的微调策略。

Result: 生成模型能有效学习多个Calabi-Yau几何上的通量真空联合分布；强化学习后训练显著提升目标物理性质（如稳定真空、低能标）的采样成功率；跨h^{1,2}尺度的微调迁移可行，表明模型具备一定几何泛化能力。

Conclusion: 生成模型可作为统一框架，实现跨几何通量真空建模、定向搜索与知识迁移，为构建Type IIB通量景观的基础模型提供了首个实证基础。

Abstract: Progress in characterising the Type IIB flux landscape requires scalable methods for constructing flux vacua. In this work, we investigate generative models as a framework for sampling vacua across multiple Calabi-Yau geometries and for directing searches towards specified physical properties. We compare transformers and diffusion models trained to generate quantised fluxes together with approximate values of the complex structure moduli and axio-dilaton. We exploit a staged training procedure comprising pre-training, and post-training via reinforcement learning, and assess how each stage improves the generated distributions and enables targeted sampling. We further examine whether structures learned from geometries with smaller $h^{1,2}$ can be transferred to geometries with larger $h^{1,2}$ through fine-tuning on specialised datasets. Our results provide evidence that generative models can learn distributions of flux vacua across distinct compactifications, improve searches for vacua with selected properties and transfer information between geometries. This establishes a concrete basis for developing a foundation model of the Type IIB flux landscape.

</details>


### [167] [Neumann zero modes and the locality of the derivative expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19953)
*C. D. Fosco,F. D. Mazzitelli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在存在满足Neumann边界条件的无质量场时，Casimir相互作用能的导数展开（DE）中出现的非解析项，并提出需在DE中加入新的非局域项以修正；通过分析导致该效应的特定模式，构建了其涨落的有效作用量，成功复现已知非解析贡献，并预测了3+1维零温下Neumann条件下存在的新非解析项|k∥|³，以及圆柱-平面构型中零模环图引起的(L/R)^{3/2}阶修正项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当系统包含满足Neumann边界的无质量场时，Casimir相互作用能的导数展开（DE）会出现非解析项，而标准DE作为局域泛函无法描述此类行为，因此需探究其物理起源并补充恰当的非局域修正。

Method: 通过识别主导非解析行为的特定模式（零模），构建其有效作用量；结合有效场论思想，类比质量粒子圈图引入非局域项；对圆柱-平面几何进行具体计算，提取零模环图贡献并与数值拟合结果比较。

Result: 确认了Neumann边界下DE需添加非局域项；复现了已知非解析贡献；预测了3+1维零温下|k∥|³阶新非解析项及其参数无关系数；在圆柱-平面构型中得到(L/R)^{3/2}阶修正项，并给出显式系数，与现有数值拟合一致。

Conclusion: Casimir相互作用在Neumann边界与无质量场共存时，导数展开必须扩展为包含源自零模涨落的非局域项；该修正具有普适性，且可定量解释特定几何下的数值偏差。

Abstract: The derivative expansion (DE) provides a useful approximation to the Casimir interaction energy between two close surfaces. It has the form of a local functional, obtained from a zero momentum expansion. However, when the system involves a massless field satisfying Neumann conditions, that expansion may develop nonanalytic terms. We present here a study on how this property reflects itself in the need to add to the DE some novel, nonlocal terms. These are, we argue, akin to the ones introduced in effective theories because of the presence of massless particles in loops. We pinpoint the mode responsible for this effect, and construct the effective action due to its fluctuations, showing that it reproduces known nonanalytic contributions, and present the corresponding nonlocal addition to the DE. We also predict a new nonanalyticity: Neumann conditions in $3+1$ dimensions at zero temperature produce a term of order $|{\mathbf k}_\shortparallel |^3$, with a parameter-free coefficient, between the (local) second and fourth orders of the expansion. We show that, for a cylinder in front of a plane, the zero-mode loop adds to the local expansion a term of relative order $(L/R)^{3/2}$, with a coefficient that we compute explicitly, showing that it accounts for the behaviour of the existing numerical fits for that geometry.

</details>


### [168] [Equivariant Localization with Electric Monodromy in M-theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20117)
*Ji-seong Chae*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文扩展了M理论中AdS₃背景的等变局域化方法，以处理具有非零电通量周期的超对称解；通过Killing旋量恒等式关联电/磁通量，并导出 warped 体积、引力反常与电通量之间的关系；成功复现了两类紧致背景的中心荷，并首次直接从十一维超引力导出其R-流水平；还研究了一类含固定点、单值性与边界贡献共存的非紧半BPS几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统等变局域化方法难以处理含非零电通量周期的AdS₃超对称背景，而此类背景在AdS/CFT对应中具有重要物理意义（如中心荷与R-流计算），亟需推广方法以统一纳入电势单值性效应。

Method: 基于等变局域化框架，引入电势的加性单值性修正；结合Killing旋量恒等式建立电通量、磁通量与warped体积的代数关系；利用超对称约束和场强方程推导引力反常与电通量的联系；将方法应用于具体AdS₃ × 内空间构型并验证结果。

Result: 1) 给出含电通量周期的AdS₃背景warped体积的完整分解（含固定点、单值性与外边界三部分）；2) 建立电通量—磁通量—Maxwell电荷—warped体积的显式关系；3) 导出引力反常与电通量的精确公式；4) 复现AdS₃×S³×S³×T²与AdS₃×S³×K3×S¹的中心荷；5) 首次从11维超引力直接导出前者R-流水平；6) 分析非紧半BPS AdS₃×S³×S³纤维化几何中的三类贡献共存情形。

Conclusion: 电通量周期及其引发的电势单值性是AdS₃超对称背景中不可忽略的关键几何—物理效应；所发展的广义等变局域化方法为从第一性原理计算二维CFT中心荷、R-对称性及反常提供了统一、普适的超引力工具；结果强化了AdS₃/CFT₂对应在非平凡拓扑与非紧情形下的自洽性。

Abstract: We extend equivariant localization for supersymmetric AdS$_3$ backgrounds of M-theory to solutions with nonzero electric flux periods. The associated local electric potential acquires additive monodromy, contributing to the warped volume alongside R-symmetry fixed points and outer boundaries. Using Killing-spinor identities, we relate the electric periods to magnetic flux and, for a class of compact backgrounds, the warped volume to the electric Maxwell charge. We also derive a relation between the gravitational anomaly and the electric flux. We apply these results to AdS$_3$ $\times S^3 \times S^3 \times T^2$ and AdS$_3$ $\times S^3 \times K3 \times S^1$, recovering their central charges and deriving the R-current levels of the former directly from eleven-dimensional supergravity. Finally, we study a noncompact half-BPS AdS$_3$ $\times S^3\times S^3$ geometry fibered over a periodic half-cylinder, where fixed-point, monodromy and outer-boundary contributions coexist.

</details>


### [169] [On Thermodynamics of AdS Black Holes in Square-Root Nonlinear Electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20162)
*A. Belhaj,S. E. Ennadifi,M. Jemri,M. Nach*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于平方根非线性电动力学的新型黑洞解，并利用黑洞力学与热力学类比，研究其稳定性、P-v临界性和相变行为，发现该黑洞无需额外修正即可呈现范德瓦尔斯行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受指数型Maxwell模型启发，探索新型黑洞解及其热力学性质，特别是是否存在不需额外修正的范德瓦尔斯行为。

Method: 基于平方根非线性电动力学构建新黑洞解，结合黑洞热力学类比方法，分析其热力学稳定性、P-v临界现象及相变特性。

Result: 所得黑洞展现出典型的范德瓦尔斯型P-v临界行为和相变特征，且在普遍比值计算中无需引入通常所需的额外修正项。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，特定形式的非线性电动力学可自然导出具有标准临界行为的黑洞热力学，为理解引力与热力学深层联系提供了新途径。

Abstract: Inspired by exponential Maxwell models, we propose a new black hole solution in square root nonlinear electrodynamics. Using the analogy between black hole mechanics and thermodynamics, we investigate the corresponding stability, the P-v criticality and the phase transitions. As a result, we find that the obtained black hole exhibits Van der Waals behaviors without additional corrections which typically appear in universal ratio computations.

</details>


### [170] [Decoding SO(5) Deconfined Multicriticality: Conformal Bootstrap and Fuzzy-Sphere Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20214)
*Zhijin Li,Tinghong Shen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过共形自举法和模糊球正则化方法研究了去禁闭量子临界点（DQCP）的性质，发现其可能对应一个真实的SO(5)多临界共形场论（CFT），并与量子蒙特卡洛（QMC）及模糊球数值结果高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DQCP的本质尚不明确，存在真实多临界CFT与伪临界性（由附近复固定点主导）两种竞争解释，需结合高精度数值与解析方法予以甄别。

Method: 采用SO(5)对称性下的混合关联子共形自举法，并结合带WZW项的SO(5)非线性σ模型的模糊球正则化实现；通过特殊共形生成元识别共形原初算符，并利用对称性投影扩展精确对角化规模；与大规模量子蒙特卡洛数据对比。

Result: 自举得到的谱和OPE系数与模糊球数据高度吻合（含16个新识别的原初算符）；最低SO(5)单态标量算符在自举与QMC中均为相关算符（Δ_s=2.816，随系统增大而减小）；模糊球在N=12时识别出52个共形原初算符（其中31个为新发现）。

Conclusion: 混合关联子自举解对应一个具有相关SO(5)单态标量的幺正CFT，且与QMC和模糊球的DQCP数据紧密关联；三者共同为二维量子磁体中SO(5)多临界CFT的存在提供了综合证据。

Abstract: The nature of the deconfined quantum critical point (DQCP) remains unsettled: competing explanations invoke a real multicritical conformal field theory (CFT) or pseudocriticality governed by nearby complex fixed points. We use the conformal bootstrap and fuzzy-sphere regularization to compute $SO(5)$ CFT data and compare our results with large-scale quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) data to investigate these scenarios. Using mixed correlators of the lowest vector $φ$, singlet $s$, and traceless symmetric scalar $t$, we characterize the bootstrap cone whose tip lies remarkably close to the position indicated by large-scale QMC. We extract an extensive spectrum across $SO(5)$ irreducible representations, together with operator product expansion (OPE) coefficients. We study a fuzzy-sphere realization of the $SO(5)$ nonlinear sigma model with a level-1 Wess--Zumino--Witten term. Using the special conformal generators, we identify 52 conformal primaries in a system with \(N=10\) Landau orbitals, including 31 not previously classified as primaries in the earlier fuzzy-sphere study. Symmetry projection extends fuzzy-sphere exact diagonalization to $N=12$. The bootstrap spectrum and OPE coefficients show close agreement with fuzzy-sphere data, including 16 of the newly identified primaries in channels accessible to the mixed-correlator bootstrap. The lowest $SO(5)$ singlet scalar is relevant in the bootstrap and QMC results; its fuzzy-sphere data is $Δ_s=2.816$ at $N=12$ and decreases with system size. Our results suggest that the mixed-correlator bootstrap solution represents a unitary CFT with a relevant $SO(5)$ singlet scalar and is closely connected to the QMC and fuzzy-sphere data for the DQCP. The conformal bootstrap, fuzzy-sphere, and QMC together provide comprehensive evidence for an $SO(5)$ multicritical CFT and critical data to decode the phases in two-dimensional quantum magnets.

</details>


### [171] [Off-shell one-loop counterterms of Palatini gravity with an independent background connection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20295)
*F. T. Brandt,G. A. Menezes*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了第一阶（Palatini）形式纯引力在Goldberg变量下背景场方法中的一圈散度，验证了背景Ward-Takahashi恒等式，并发现除曲率平方项外还需引入由背景联络非度规性构成的不变量作为发散项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索纯引力在Palatini形式下量子化时的紫外发散结构，特别是非度规背景连接对反常项的影响。

Method: 采用背景场方法，在Goldberg变量下计算一阶Palatini引力的一圈发散；使用任意维度和任意规范，并特别关注非度规性构造的不变量；验证背景Ward-Takahashi恒等式。

Result: 两、三、四点函数的发散项包含曲率平方项及非度规性构造的新不变量（如非拓扑意义下的Gauss-Bonnet组合）；所有反项在壳上消失，壳外可通过场重定义消除。

Conclusion: Palatini形式纯引力的一圈紫外结构虽与度规形式一致（反项可消去），但其显式形式依赖于非度规性，揭示了背景联络几何自由度对量子效应的关键影响。

Abstract: We compute the one-loop divergences of pure gravity in the first-order (Palatini) formulation, using the background field method in Goldberg variables, with a background connection independent of the background metric. Background Ward-Takahashi identities are verified at one loop. The divergences of the two-point functions, obtained in arbitrary dimension and gauge, require, besides the curvature-squared terms, invariants built from the nonmetricity of the background connection; in particular, the Gauss-Bonnet combination, which is not topological for a nonmetric connection, does not suffice. Three- and four-point functions in the Feynman-de Donder gauge complete the counterterm Lagrangian up to fourth order in the nonmetricity and provide parameter-free tests of it. All the counterterms vanish on shell, as in the metric formulation, and off shell they can be removed by field redefinitions.

</details>


### [172] [Phase Structure and Thermodynamic Geometry of STU Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20328)
*Fil Simovic,Kenan Scavennec,David Kubiznak,Robert B. Mann*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维STU黑洞的热力学性质，通过几何热力学方法分析其相变结构和微观相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从几何热力学角度深入理解STU黑洞的热力学行为及其微观结构特征。

Method: 基于非旋转、成对等电荷的STU黑洞解，推导热力学量的闭式表达式，并构建以温度和热力学体积为涨落变量的Ruppeiner几何。

Result: 发现当电荷比足够大时存在两个临界点，揭示了丰富的相结构，并识别出对应微观吸引与排斥相互作用的参数空间区域。

Conclusion: Ruppeiner曲率可作为探测STU黑洞微观结构的有效工具，且其相结构对电荷配置高度敏感。

Abstract: We study the thermodynamics of four-dimensional STU black holes, a family of charged solutions of gauged N = 8, SO(8) supergravity, from the perspective of geometric thermodynamics.
